# 미식루트 보관 자료

2026-11-12 기준. 개별 점포의 노출 형평성 문제로 미식루트 영역을 기각하여 활성 웹사이트에서 제거했습니다. 다시 도입할 때 참고할 설계와 제거 직전 소스를 보관합니다.

## 마지막으로 합의한 구성

- 테마별 대표 메뉴 카드. 카드에는 대표 메뉴명과 시장 내 판매 점포 목록을 표시합니다.
- 카드의 점포 목록은 두 줄을 넘으면 말줄임표로 줄입니다.
- 상세 팝업에는 이미지, 대표 메뉴명, 설명, 전체 판매 점포 목록을 표시합니다.
- 모바일에서는 팝업 상단과 하단 버튼 영역을 고정하고 가운데 내용만 스크롤합니다.
- 메뉴 목록은 운영자가 직접 입력합니다. 임의의 메뉴나 점포를 자동으로 채우지 않습니다.

## 테마와 데이터

| ID | 이름 | 색상 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| budget | 만 원 안의 행복 | 초록 |
| gluten-free | 글루텐 프리 | 브라운 |
| grill | 불맛 산책 | 레드 |
| vegan | 비건 보물 찾기 | 연두 |
| winter | 겨울 간식 산책 | 골드 |

운영표는 https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1nZfd7rY245ofh-uKZqQDoc6XMAnhOKV6hsULb0oSj8c/edit?gid=112026121 에 있습니다. 웹사이트 메뉴 탭이 대표 메뉴용이고 웹사이트 점포 탭은 이전 점포별 원본입니다.

| 열 | 항목 |
| --- | --- |
| A | 공개 상태 |
| B | 대표 메뉴 |
| C | 설명 |
| D | 시장 내 위치한 대표메뉴 점포들. 한 줄에 한 점포 |
| E | 미식루트 |
| F | 사진 링크 |
| G | 메뉴 ID. 숨긴 식별자 |

## 연결과 복원 순서

1. 아래 소스에서 기능별 파일을 복원하고, 공유 파일은 현재 버전과 비교하여 필요한 부분만 병합합니다.
2. HomeExperience의 테마 선택·카드·팝업과 SiteHeader·지도 페이지의 연결 링크를 복원합니다.
3. routes 페이지와 scripts/export-pages.mjs의 정적 경로 목록을 함께 복원합니다.
4. content-schema의 이전 데이터 필드는 기존 저장 파일을 계속 읽기 위해 남아 있습니다. 저장 데이터와 Google 시트는 삭제하지 않았습니다.
5. Apps Script는 기존 점포용 stores와 대표 메뉴용 menuItems를 함께 제공합니다. 새 웹사이트 API는 menuItems가 있으면 그 목록을 사용합니다.
6. 사진 업로드는 선택한 시트와 행을 검증하고, 4MB 이하 JPG·PNG·WebP를 웹용으로 줄여 Drive에 저장합니다. 대표 메뉴는 F열, 이전 점포표는 J열에 연결합니다.
7. 사진 URL은 교체 시 버전이 바뀝니다. 브라우저 15초, CDN 30초, 목록 메모리 10초의 짧은 캐시를 사용했습니다.
8. GA4 측정 ID는 NEXT_PUBLIC_GA4_MEASUREMENT_ID에 설정합니다. 미식루트 클릭 이벤트는 아래 HomeExperience 원문을 참고합니다.
9. 공개·비공개, 사진 교체, 메뉴별 점포 묶음, 두 줄 말줄임, 전체 점포 팝업, 모바일 고정 헤더·푸터, 관리자 미리보기와 정적 빌드를 검증한 뒤 배포합니다.

외부 Google 시트와 배포된 Apps Script는 별도 자산입니다. 이 문서를 보관하는 작업에서는 해당 자료를 삭제하거나 내용을 초기화하지 않습니다.

## 보관 원문

각 경로 아래에는 제거 직전의 파일 전체가 들어 있습니다. SHA-256은 원본 파일 바이트의 해시입니다. 공유 파일의 원문을 그대로 덮어쓰면 이후의 다른 수정까지 되돌릴 수 있으므로 복원 시 비교하여 병합합니다.

### src/app/(frontend)/StoreModal.tsx

SHA-256 65219cc2dbfab4ce870b065da9cc7dcd97b1bf09ee80df35aa4ab6c9e1de6cc2

```tsx
'use client'
import Image from 'next/image'
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type CSSProperties } from 'react'
import { getRouteColors } from '@/lib/route-colors'
import type { HomeRoute, HomeStore, SiteCopy } from '@/lib/content-schema'
import type { MenuItem } from '@/lib/menu-items'

export default function StoreModal({ store, routes, site, onClose, themeID }: { store: MenuItem | null; routes: HomeRoute[]; site: SiteCopy; onClose: () => void; themeID?: string }) {
  const dialog = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null)
  const palette = getRouteColors(themeID ?? store?.routeIds[0] ?? "budget")
  const themeStyle = { "--route-color": palette.color, "--route-tint": palette.tint, "--route-strong": palette.strong, "--route-accent": palette.accent } as CSSProperties
  useEffect(() => { if (store) dialog.current?.showModal(); else dialog.current?.close() }, [store?.id])
  return <dialog ref={dialog} className="public-store-modal" style={themeStyle} aria-labelledby="public-store-title" onCancel={onClose} onClose={onClose}>{store && <>
    <div className="public-store-modal-head"><span>GUWOL MORAENAE / TASTE</span><button type="button" aria-label="메뉴 상세 닫기" onClick={onClose}>✕</button></div>
    <div className="public-store-modal-body"><StorePhoto key={store.image?.url} image={store.image} name={store.menu} /><div className="public-store-copy"><div className="public-store-themes">{store.routeIds.map(id => routes.find(route => route.id === id)).filter(Boolean).map(route => <span key={route!.id}>{route!.name}</span>)}</div><h2 id="public-store-title">{store.menu}</h2><p className="public-menu-description">{store.description || "설명을 준비하고 있습니다."}</p><section className="public-menu-stores" aria-labelledby="public-menu-stores-title"><h3 id="public-menu-stores-title">시장 내 판매 점포</h3>{store.storeNames.length ? <ul>{store.storeNames.map(name => <li key={name}>{name}</li>)}</ul> : <p>점포 정보를 준비하고 있습니다.</p>}</section></div></div><div className="public-store-modal-foot"><button className="store-detail-button" type="button" data-ga-event="store_detail_close" onClick={onClose}>목록으로 <span aria-hidden="true">→</span></button></div>
  </>}</dialog>
}

function StorePhoto({ image, name }: { image: HomeStore['image']; name: string }) {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'loading' | 'ready' | 'failed'>('loading')
  const showImage = image && status !== 'failed'

  return (
    <div className="public-store-image">
      {(!showImage || status === 'loading') && (
        <div className="public-store-photo-placeholder" role="status">
          <p>{showImage ? '메뉴 사진을 불러오는 중입니다.' : '메뉴 사진 준비 중'}</p>
        </div>
      )}
      {showImage && (
        <Image unoptimized loading="eager" src={image.url} alt={image.alt || name}
          width={image.width} height={image.height} onLoad={() => setStatus('ready')} onError={() => setStatus('failed')} />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

### src/app/(frontend)/MenuSelect.tsx

SHA-256 9acbd78df234384efb3c79bcf5c2cab49695340acfe2ca4eedab293c81c78ade

```tsx
'use client'
import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from 'react'

export default function MenuSelect({ options, value, onChange }: { options: string[]; value: string; onChange: (value: string) => void }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const root = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const trigger = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const id = useId()
  const choices = [{ value: '', label: '전체 메뉴' }, ...options.map(value => ({ value, label: value }))]
  useEffect(() => {
    const outside = (event: PointerEvent) => { if (!root.current?.contains(event.target as Node)) setOpen(false) }
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', outside)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', outside)
  }, [])
  const focusOption = (index: number) => requestAnimationFrame(() => root.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[role=option]')[index]?.focus())
  return <div className="menu-filter custom-menu-select" ref={root} onBlur={event => { if (!event.currentTarget.contains(event.relatedTarget as Node)) setOpen(false) }}>

    <button ref={trigger} id="route-menu-select" type="button" className="menu-select-trigger" aria-label={'메뉴 선택: ' + (value || '전체 메뉴')} aria-haspopup="listbox" aria-controls={id + '-list'} aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen(!open)} onKeyDown={event => {
      if (event.key === 'ArrowDown' || event.key === 'ArrowUp') { event.preventDefault(); setOpen(true); focusOption(event.key === 'ArrowDown' ? Math.max(0, choices.findIndex(choice => choice.value === value)) : choices.length - 1) }
      if (event.key === 'Escape') setOpen(false)
    }}><span id={id + '-value'}>{value || '전체 메뉴'}</span><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m5 7 5 5 5-5" /></svg></button>
    {open && <div id={id + '-list'} className="menu-select-list" role="listbox" aria-label="메뉴 목록" onKeyDown={event => {
      const focused = [...event.currentTarget.querySelectorAll('button')].indexOf(document.activeElement as HTMLButtonElement)
      if (['ArrowDown', 'ArrowUp', 'Home', 'End'].includes(event.key)) { event.preventDefault(); focusOption(event.key === 'Home' ? 0 : event.key === 'End' ? choices.length - 1 : (focused + (event.key === 'ArrowDown' ? 1 : -1) + choices.length) % choices.length) }
      if (event.key === 'Escape') { event.preventDefault(); setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus() }
    }}>{choices.map(choice => <button type="button" role="option" data-ga-event="route_menu_select" data-ga-item={choice.value ? "representative_menu" : "all_menus"} aria-selected={value === choice.value} key={choice.value} onClick={() => { onChange(choice.value); setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus() }}><span>{choice.label}</span>{value === choice.value && <span aria-hidden="true">✓</span>}</button>)}</div>}
  </div>
}

```

### src/app/(frontend)/admin/ThemeManager.tsx

SHA-256 f920adacdd65b8f1e0df6fc0dd240b6af14791d6b663ed492bc10584c42d2ed7

```tsx
'use client'
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import type { HomeRoute, HomeStore } from '@/lib/content-schema'

export default function ThemeManager({ routes, stores, onChange }: { routes: HomeRoute[]; stores: HomeStore[]; onChange: (routes: HomeRoute[]) => void }) {
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<HomeRoute | null>(null)
  const dialog = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null)
  const existing = editing && routes.some(route => route.id === editing.id)
  useEffect(() => { if (editing) dialog.current?.showModal(); else dialog.current?.close() }, [editing?.id])
  const move = (index: number, direction: number) => {
    const next = [...routes]; const other = index + direction
    if (other < 0 || other >= next.length) return
    ;[next[index], next[other]] = [next[other], next[index]]
    onChange(next)
  }
  return <div className="store-manager theme-manager">
    <div className="store-manager-heading"><div><p className="store-eyebrow">GUWOL MORAENAE / TASTE ROUTES</p><h1>미식루트 테마<span>{routes.length}</span></h1><p>테마를 선택해 내용을 수정하고, 화살표로 표시 순서를 조정하세요.</p></div><button className="admin-primary store-add-button" onClick={() => setEditing({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), name: '', description: '', introduction: '', advisory: '', isVisible: false })}>＋ 테마 추가</button></div>
    <div className="store-list-panel"><div className="theme-list-heading" aria-hidden="true"><span>순서</span><span>테마 / 짧은 설명</span><span>연결 점포</span><span>노출상태</span><span>순서 변경</span></div>
      <ul className="theme-manager-list" aria-label="미식루트 테마 목록">{routes.map((route, index) => <li key={route.id}>
        <button className="theme-edit-row" aria-label={route.name + ' 테마 편집'} onClick={() => setEditing(structuredClone(route))}><span className="theme-order">{String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')}</span><span className="theme-name"><strong>{route.name}</strong><span>{route.description || '짧은 설명을 추가해주세요.'}</span></span><span className="theme-store-count">{stores.filter(store => store.routeIds.includes(route.id)).length}<small>개 점포</small></span><span className={route.isVisible ? 'store-visibility is-visible' : 'store-visibility'}><span aria-hidden="true" />{route.isVisible ? '공개' : '비공개'}</span></button>
        <div className="theme-order-actions"><button aria-label={route.name + ' 위로 이동'} disabled={index === 0} onClick={() => move(index, -1)}>↑</button><button aria-label={route.name + ' 아래로 이동'} disabled={index === routes.length - 1} onClick={() => move(index, 1)}>↓</button></div>
      </li>)}</ul>
      {!routes.length && <div className="store-list-empty"><h2>첫 미식루트를 추가해보세요.</h2><p>테마를 만든 후 점포를 연결할 수 있습니다.</p></div>}
    </div><p className="store-manager-footnote">테마 이름을 바꾸어도 연결된 점포는 유지됩니다. 변경 후 임시저장 또는 고객페이지 반영을 눌러주세요.</p>
    <dialog ref={dialog} className="admin-store-dialog store-editor-dialog theme-editor-dialog" aria-labelledby="theme-editor-title" onCancel={() => setEditing(null)} onClose={() => setEditing(null)}>{editing && <form onSubmit={event => {
      event.preventDefault()
      if (!editing.name.trim()) return
      const next = { ...editing, name: editing.name.trim() }
      onChange(existing ? routes.map(route => route.id === editing.id ? next : route) : [...routes, next]); setEditing(null)
    }}><div className="store-editor-header"><div><p className="store-eyebrow">TASTE ROUTE / DETAILS</p><h2 id="theme-editor-title">{existing ? '미식루트 테마 편집' : '새 미식루트 추가'}</h2><p id="store-editor-description">테마의 이름과 고객에게 안내할 내용을 입력하세요.</p></div><button type="button" className="store-editor-close" aria-label="테마 편집창 닫기" onClick={() => setEditing(null)}>✕</button></div>
      <div className="store-editor-body"><div className="store-form-grid">{(['name', 'description', 'introduction', 'advisory'] as const).map(key => <label className="store-field-wide" key={key}>{{ name: '테마명', description: '짧은 설명', introduction: '점포 목록 소개', advisory: '재료·인증 관련 안내' }[key]}{key === 'name' ? <input autoFocus required maxLength={160} value={editing.name} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, name: e.target.value })} /> : <textarea rows={key === 'advisory' ? 4 : 2} maxLength={4000} value={editing[key]} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, [key]: e.target.value })} />}</label>)}</div><section className="store-publish-setting"><div><h3>고객페이지 공개</h3><p>비공개 테마는 고객페이지의 미식루트 목록에서 숨겨집니다.</p></div><label className="store-switch"><input aria-label="테마 고객페이지에 공개" type="checkbox" checked={editing.isVisible} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, isVisible: e.target.checked })} /><span aria-hidden="true" /><strong>{editing.isVisible ? '공개' : '비공개'}</strong></label></section></div>
      <div className="store-editor-footer"><p>점포 연결은 점포 관리에서 수정할 수 있습니다.</p><div><button type="button" onClick={() => setEditing(null)}>취소</button><button type="submit" className="admin-primary">{existing ? '수정 내용 적용' : '테마 추가하기'}</button></div></div>
    </form>}</dialog>
  </div>
}
```

### src/lib/menu-items.ts

SHA-256 b2b7212c3e0b8457a326db9d7d912d5abf7643f3eebdc26ed5d4049ba7c5b163

```typescript
import type { HomeStore } from './content-schema'

export type MenuItem = {
  id: string
  menu: string
  description: string
  storeNames: string[]
  routeIds: string[]
  image: HomeStore['image']
}

export function createMenuItems(stores: HomeStore[]): MenuItem[] {
  const items = new Map<string, MenuItem>()
  for (const store of stores) {
    const menu = store.menu.trim()
    if (!store.isVisible || !menu) continue
    const key = menu.normalize('NFC').replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    const names = (store.storeNames ?? [store.name]).map(name => name.trim()).filter(Boolean)
    const existing = items.get(key)
    if (existing) {
      existing.storeNames = [...new Set([...existing.storeNames, ...names])]
      existing.routeIds = [...new Set([...existing.routeIds, ...store.routeIds])]
      existing.description ||= store.description?.trim() ?? ''
      existing.image ||= store.image
    } else {
      items.set(key, {
        id: store.id,
        menu,
        description: store.description?.trim() ?? '',
        storeNames: [...new Set(names)],
        routeIds: [...store.routeIds],
        image: store.image,
      })
    }
  }
  return [...items.values()]
}
```

### src/lib/demo-stores.ts

SHA-256 d6e2c38aec6b6ca276135cb3fb058d1c8c3685dca545ac931fa4a95a13ec2110

```typescript
import type { HomeRoute, HomeStore } from './content-schema'

// UI examples only: never persisted or mixed with published store records.
export function createDemoStores(routes: HomeRoute[]): HomeStore[] {
  const groups = [ ['잔치국수', '비빔국수', '김밥'], ['쌀국수', '메밀국수', '떡'], ['숯불구이', '철판볶음', '꼬치'], ['두부요리', '채소김밥', '나물비빔밥'], ['호떡', '붕어빵', '군고구마'] ]
  return routes.flatMap((route, routeIndex) => Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, index) => {
    const menu = groups[routeIndex % groups.length][index % 3]
    return { id: `demo-${route.id}-${index}`, name: `[예시] ${menu} 점포 ${index + 1}`, menu, menus: [menu], isVisible: true, price: `예시 ${(4000 + index * 1000).toLocaleString('ko-KR')}원`, hours: '예시 10:00–19:00', image: null, routeIds: [route.id] }
  }))
}
```

```typescript
const routeColors = [
  { color: '#2E8B57', accent: '#4CAF72', tint: '#E8F6E9', ink: '#FFFFFF', strong: '#17643B' },
  { color: '#F8A623', accent: '#FFD878', tint: '#FFF6DD', ink: '#FFFFFF', strong: '#8A5200' },
  { color: '#A8D64E', accent: '#CDEB7A', tint: '#F3FCE6', ink: '#FFFFFF', strong: '#3D5A0A' },
  { color: '#F74C3C', accent: '#FF8A7A', tint: '#FFE9E6', ink: '#FFFFFF', strong: '#B42318' },
  { color: '#8B5A3C', accent: '#C8A07D', tint: '#F5EAE2', ink: '#FFFFFF', strong: '#684026' },
] as const
const routeColorIndex: Record<string, number> = { budget: 0, 'gluten-free': 4, grill: 3, vegan: 2, winter: 1 }
export function getRouteColors(id: string, index = 0) {
  return routeColors[routeColorIndex[id] ?? Math.max(0, index) % routeColors.length]
}
```

### api/public-stores.mjs

SHA-256 151250d47399f9da06b427c45dd76edeb96ee0fd05edece93abbdc5468a3926a

```javascript
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';

const MAX_PHOTO_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024;
const ID = /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,100}$/;

const FEED_TTL_MS = 10_000;
const PHOTO_TTL_SECONDS = 30;
let feedCache;

export async function GET(request) {
  const source = process.env.MARKET_STORES_WEB_APP_URL || 'https://script.google.com/macros/s/AKfycbySQN7Em1ys1DDUVGXiXVsSMWf2v9M2CbMa_lbtpQM1zvx3sm8l7fVCShzB_iwUyqRv/exec';
  if (!/^https:\/\/script\.google\.com\/macros\/s\/[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\/exec$/.test(source)) return unavailable();

  try {
    const query = request ? new URL(request.url).searchParams : new URLSearchParams();
    const photoId = query.get('photo');
    const version = query.get('v');
    if (photoId !== null && (!ID.test(photoId) || (version !== null && !/^[a-f0-9]{24}$/.test(version)))) return missingPhoto();

    const published = await readPublishedStores(source);
    if (photoId !== null) {
      const store = published.find(store => store.id === photoId);
      const photo = publicPhoto(store?.photoLink);
      if (!photo || (version !== null && version !== photo.version)) return missingPhoto();
      return servePhoto(photo.download, version !== null);
    }

    const stores = published.map(store => {
      const photo = publicPhoto(store.photoLink);
      return {
        id: store.id, name: store.name, menu: store.menu, menus: store.menus,
        price: store.price, hours: store.hours, location: store.location, routeIds: store.routeIds,
        ...(typeof store.description === 'string' ? { description: store.description } : {}),
        ...(Array.isArray(store.storeNames) ? { storeNames: store.storeNames } : {}),
        isVisible: true,
        image: photo ? {
          url: '/api/public-stores?photo=' + store.id + '&v=' + photo.version,
          alt: store.name + ' 점포 사진',
          width: 800,
          height: 800,
        } : null,
      };
    });

    return Response.json({ stores }, { headers: cacheHeaders() });
  } catch {
    return unavailable();
  }
}

async function readPublishedStores(source) {
  if (feedCache?.source === source && (feedCache.pending || Date.now() < feedCache.expiresAt)) return feedCache.pending || feedCache.stores;

  const entry = { source, pending: fetchPublishedStores(source) };
  feedCache = entry;
  try {
    entry.stores = await entry.pending;
    entry.expiresAt = Date.now() + FEED_TTL_MS;
    return entry.stores;
  } finally {
    entry.pending = null;
  }
}

async function fetchPublishedStores(source) {
  const result = await fetch(source, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000) });
  if (!result.ok) throw new Error('Store feed unavailable');
  const data = await result.json();
  const stores = Array.isArray(data.menuItems) ? data.menuItems : data.stores;
  if (!Array.isArray(stores) || stores.length > 2000) throw new Error('Invalid store feed');
  return stores.filter(store => store?.isVisible === true && typeof store.id === 'string' && ID.test(store.id));
}

function publicPhoto(value) {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || !value.startsWith('https://drive.google.com/')) return null;
  let source;
  try { source = new URL(value); } catch { return null; }
  const file = source.pathname.match(/^\/file\/d\/([^/]+)(?:\/|$)/)?.[1]
    ?? (['/open', '/uc'].includes(source.pathname) ? source.searchParams.get('id') : null);
  if (!file || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,100}$/.test(file)) return null;
  const download = new URL('https://drive.usercontent.google.com/download');
  download.searchParams.set('id', file);
  download.searchParams.set('export', 'download');
  const key = source.searchParams.get('resourcekey');
  if (key && /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,100}$/.test(key)) download.searchParams.set('resourcekey', key);
  const version = createHash('sha256').update(download.href).digest('hex').slice(0, 24);
  return { download, version };
}

async function servePhoto(url, versioned) {
  const signal = AbortSignal.timeout(5000);
  try {
    for (let hop = 0; hop < 3; hop++) {
      const response = await fetch(url, { redirect: 'manual', signal });
      if (response.status >= 300 && response.status < 400) {
        const location = response.headers.get('location');
        await response.body?.cancel();
        if (!location) return missingPhoto();
        url = new URL(location, url);
        if (url.protocol !== 'https:' || url.username || url.password || url.port ||
          !(url.hostname === 'drive.google.com' || url.hostname.endsWith('.googleusercontent.com'))) return missingPhoto();
        continue;
      }
      const type = response.headers.get('content-type')?.split(';')[0];
      if (!response.ok || !['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp'].includes(type) ||
          Number(response.headers.get('content-length')) > MAX_PHOTO_BYTES || !response.body) {
        await response.body?.cancel();
        return missingPhoto();
      }
      const reader = response.body.getReader();
      const chunks = [];
      let size = 0;
      while (true) {
        const { done, value } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        size += value.byteLength;
        if (size > MAX_PHOTO_BYTES) { await reader.cancel(); return missingPhoto(); }
        chunks.push(value);
      }
      const bytes = Buffer.concat(chunks);
      const valid = type === 'image/jpeg' ? bytes.subarray(0, 3).equals(Buffer.from([255, 216, 255]))
        : type === 'image/png' ? bytes.subarray(0, 8).equals(Buffer.from([137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10]))
        : bytes.toString('ascii', 0, 4) === 'RIFF' && bytes.toString('ascii', 8, 12) === 'WEBP';
      if (!valid) return missingPhoto();
      return new Response(bytes, { headers: { ...cacheHeaders(versioned), 'Content-Type': type, 'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff' } });
    }
  } catch {
    // Unshared, deleted or temporarily unavailable Drive files use the modal placeholder.
  }
  return missingPhoto();
}

function cacheHeaders(versioned = false) {
  return {
    'Cache-Control': versioned ? 'public, max-age=15' : 'public, max-age=0',
    'Vercel-CDN-Cache-Control': 'max-age=' + (versioned ? PHOTO_TTL_SECONDS : 15),
  };
}

function missingPhoto() {
  return new Response(null, { status: 404, headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
}

function unavailable() {
  return Response.json({ error: '점포 목록을 불러올 수 없습니다.' }, {
    status: 503,
    headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' },
  });
}

```

### deploy/market-stores.gs

SHA-256 4154082ec5d3bc3d8e1f3175f00af41b7274b290a112e38e6f77002abc48dc59

```javascript
// Paste into the Apps Script project bound to the website store spreadsheet.
const STORE_SHEET_ID = '1nZfd7rY245ofh-uKZqQDoc6XMAnhOKV6hsULb0oSj8c';
const STORE_COLUMNS = ['공개 상태', '점포명', '대표 메뉴', '선택 메뉴', '가격', '영업시간', '위치', '미식루트', '점포 ID'];
const ROUTE_IDS = {
  '만 원 안의 행복': 'budget',
  '글루텐 프리': 'gluten-free',
  '불맛 산책': 'grill',
  '비건 보물 찾기': 'vegan',
  '겨울 간식 산책': 'winter',
};

const MENU_COLUMNS = ['공개 상태', '대표 메뉴', '설명', '시장 내 위치한 대표메뉴 점포들', '미식루트', '사진 링크', '메뉴 ID'];

function doGet() {
  try {
    const metadata = Sheets.Spreadsheets.get(STORE_SHEET_ID, {
      fields: 'sheets.properties.title',
    });
    const menuSheet = metadata.sheets.some(sheet => sheet.properties.title === '웹사이트 메뉴');
    const ranges = ["'웹사이트 점포'!A1:J2001"];
    if (menuSheet) ranges.push("'웹사이트 메뉴'!A1:G2001");
    const response = Sheets.Spreadsheets.Values.batchGet(STORE_SHEET_ID, {
      ranges, valueRenderOption: 'FORMATTED_VALUE',
    });
    const [headers = [], ...rows] = response.valueRanges[0].values ?? [];
    if (!STORE_COLUMNS.every((header, index) => headers[index] === header)) {
      throw new Error('Store headers changed');
    }
    const result = { stores: publicStores(rows, headers[9] === '점포 사진 (Drive 링크)') };
    if (menuSheet) {
      const [menuHeaders = [], ...menuRows] = response.valueRanges[1].values ?? [];
      if (!MENU_COLUMNS.every((header, index) => menuHeaders[index] === header)) {
        throw new Error('Menu headers changed');
      }
      result.menuItems = publicMenus(menuRows);
    }
    return json(result);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Store feed could not be read');
    return json({ error: 'Store feed is unavailable' });
  }
}

function publicMenus(rows) {
  const seen = new Set();
  const stores = [];
  for (const row of rows) {
    const [visibility, menu, description, sellerList, themes, photoLink, explicitId] = MENU_COLUMNS.map((_, index) => String(row[index] ?? '').trim());
    if (visibility !== '공개' || !menu || menu.length > 160 || explicitId === 'example') continue;
    const routeIds = [...new Set(themes.split(/[,\n]+/).map(theme => ROUTE_IDS[theme.trim()]).filter(id => typeof id === 'string'))];
    if (!routeIds.length) continue;
    const id = /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,94}$/.test(explicitId)
      ? 'sheet-' + explicitId
      : 'sheet-menu-' + Utilities.base64EncodeWebSafe(Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, menu + '\n' + themes, Utilities.Charset.UTF_8)).replace(/=+$/, '');
    if (seen.has(id)) continue;
    seen.add(id);
    const storeNames = [...new Set(sellerList.split(/\r?\n/).map(name => name.trim().slice(0, 160)).filter(Boolean))].slice(0, 500);
    stores.push({
      id, name: menu, menu, menus: [], price: '', hours: '', location: '',
      description: description.slice(0, 4000), storeNames, routeIds,
      image: null, isVisible: true, photoLink: photoLink.slice(0, 2000),
    });
  }
  return stores;
}

function publicStores(rows, photosEnabled) {
  const seen = new Set();
  const stores = [];
  for (const row of rows) {
    const [visibility, name, menu, menuList, price, hours, location, themes, explicitId] = STORE_COLUMNS.map((_, index) => String(row[index] ?? '').trim());
    if (visibility !== '공개' || !name || name.length > 160 || name === '작성 예시' || explicitId === 'example') continue;
    const routeIds = [...new Set(themes.split(/[,\n]+/).map(theme => ROUTE_IDS[theme.trim()]).filter(id => typeof id === 'string'))];
    if (!routeIds.length) continue;
    const id = /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,94}$/.test(explicitId)
      ? 'sheet-' + explicitId
      : 'sheet-' + Utilities.base64EncodeWebSafe(Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, name + '\n' + location, Utilities.Charset.UTF_8)).replace(/=+$/, '');
    if (seen.has(id)) continue;
    seen.add(id);
    const menus = [...new Set(menuList.split(/[,\n]+/).map(item => item.trim().slice(0, 160)).filter(Boolean))].slice(0, 100);
    stores.push({
      id, name, isVisible: true, routeIds, image: null, menus,
      photoLink: photosEnabled ? String(row[9] ?? '').trim().slice(0, 2000) : '',
      menu: menu.slice(0, 4000), price: price.slice(0, 4000),
      hours: hours.slice(0, 4000), location: location.slice(0, 4000),
    });
  }
  return stores;
}

function json(payload) {
  return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(payload)).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
}
```

```javascript
const PHOTO_FOLDER_ID = '1xC4rUS561k3qH4sB82zs_LVX2z4Mx9F2';
const PHOTO_MAX_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024;

function onOpen() {
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().createMenu('점포 사진')
    .addItem('PC에서 업로드', 'openStorePhotoUpload')
    .addToUi();
}

function installStorePhotoButton() {
  const sheet = photoSheet_();
  const title = '점포 사진 업로드';
  if (!sheet.getImages().some(image => image.getAltTextTitle() === title)) {
    const png = Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode('iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAARgAAAA2CAYAAAAcX4Z+AAAAAXNSR0IArs4c6QAAAARnQU1BAACxjwv8YQUAAAAJcEhZcwAADsMAAA7DAcdvqGQAAARxSURBVHhe7Zu9qlQxFEbvs1j7ADZWgqXYaieIWIlYaSViJYhge1sLsbATxFJbsbC1sbDxLa5sIcOePTmZ/O0zJ7nrg4Vec/4m2Vknk4tnV+5dvwAA8ODM/gMAQC8QDAC4gWAAwA0EAwBuIBgAcAPBAIAbCAYA3EAwAOAGggEANxAMALiBYADADQQDAG4gGABwA8EAgBuXUjDP3725CPny4+tB+yzozyl/t+29kD7sfZ87rx5dfPj2aXddifx868X9g2MD0hYy87iOxJCC0RMnN0vnz1yIuYKp6U890XsL5vzz+7172bz+eH5wjoBgtgeCSRSiLtiWpN66NegJnbr2iIIReeQkdp8SwbSObarfNS2x1xoRBJMoxNYiDMktxlxmFcyNZ3f37iGyufb49v82+dOubEJbAMFsjyEFEyN30gkI5jgl5/YSjL6n7LfYdkHvyzw5f7nXhmC2xzSC+f33z25gbOHpCWBzrBC3yKyC0deRTV7bLjx4+3R3TCoe45rb7xqd3HNmYgrB2LeRyEa39xBMztuxpgBryL1PiSQs+h62Py0IZhmd3HNmYgrBfP/1c28gJbrQZxOMTuo+tYKRvQ2b1Pm9BFP6FSmVpTEK2JdSaVL9rqk5ZyaGF4zd+NNZ+nWmLuRjhRjYimCuPry5u4ckNaFrBbMk5KX+7CWY0k1eWc3o83PGKHZsTXLHt+acmRhWMFJwduUiy2rZf9GR5b0txDUEIyKw7T2wXxGkD+wxAf05bZaKXU9iOd9O+lh/9hKMYCWylNjnzhmj2LE1Weo/S805MzGcYGJvMoku+li7LsgawQg6ti2nvQex1YX9dW2gRDAiRP31Q/eXlZpERBMk2lMwQmz8dOTZYgKvFcyxY1toiR2jERlOMFJYeuUihS5vWXucnRT6mFzBpCZoSXpMOkFWaLEsfYbU84fiFTnZ42ITWPpP/6ZOC723YITYfxWQ+9jVk6ZEGiXHttASBHMipPilKKSY7UTQyOSRIrVFP6Jg7N6LfcvHJp5+/qVnsMKOyUUfK/sidgPWQzA1lEij9SvSsesHWoJgJmcrgolJIPZ8diWXIxh9/aVN3GNsRTAlrCWYy84UgrETrSQthaJj23ph5SLRItGTW6JXMrmCsejYthhegil9/i2uYC47CKahUHRsWw/sPpLELptjApLPJP9eOkEDOrYtxoiCSaFj26AMBLPhQrS/crdfgQJWMmEVUzpBAzq2LcYaginNlsf1MjGdYHIKfKQ3nchC5LEkF43socjmb/i5tF8Ca3yuHHoLpuV6qei+XeMeI4FgTlSIa1DaLwEd27YmLeNwqnFd4x4jgWBOVIhrUNovAR3btialz7+FcV3jHiMxnWBKc6pCXIPSCRpoSaw/a2kZh57PAfUgmIkLEcEcXhPWBcFMXIgI5vCasC5TCAYAtgmCAQA3EAwAuIFgAMANBAMAbiAYAHADwQCAGwgGANxAMADgBoIBADcQDAC4gWAAwA0EAwBuIBgAcAPBAIAbCAYA3PgHV/jkiDiuPeQAAAAASUVORK5CYII='), 'image/png');
    sheet.insertImage(png, 12, 1).setWidth(140).setHeight(27)
      .setAltTextTitle(title).assignScript('openStorePhotoUpload');
  }
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();
}

function openStorePhotoUpload() {
  const active = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveRange();
  const sheet = photoSheet_(active && active.getSheet().getSheetId());
  const columns = photoColumns_(sheet);
  if (!active || active.getSheet().getSheetId() !== sheet.getSheetId() ||
      active.getRow() < 2 || active.getNumRows() !== 1) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('웹사이트 점포 탭에서 사진을 등록할 점포 행의 셀을 하나 선택하세요.');
    return;
  }
  const row = active.getRow();
  const data = sheet.getRange(row, 1, 1, 10).getDisplayValues()[0];
  if (!data[columns.name].trim()) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('선택한 행의 점포명을 먼저 입력하세요.');
    return;
  }
  const view = HtmlService.createTemplate(PHOTO_DIALOG_HTML);
  view.row = row;
  view.store = data[columns.name];
  view.storeId = data[columns.id];
  view.previousPhoto = data[columns.photo];
  view.sheetId = sheet.getSheetId();
  view.photoColumn = String.fromCharCode(65 + columns.photo);
  view.published = data[0] === '공개';
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().showModalDialog(view.evaluate().setWidth(460).setHeight(355), '점포 사진 업로드');
}

function saveStorePhoto(form) {
  const row = Number(form.row);
  const sheet = photoSheet_(form.sheetId);
  const columns = photoColumns_(sheet);
  if (!Number.isInteger(row) || row < 2 || row > sheet.getMaxRows()) throw new Error('점포 행을 다시 선택해 주세요.');
  if (typeof form.encodedPhoto !== 'string' || !form.encodedPhoto) throw new Error('이미지 파일을 선택해 주세요.');
  if (form.encodedPhoto.length > Math.ceil(PHOTO_MAX_BYTES / 3) * 4) throw new Error('4MB 이하 이미지를 선택해 주세요.');
  const bytes = Utilities.base64Decode(form.encodedPhoto);
  if (!bytes.length || bytes.length > PHOTO_MAX_BYTES) throw new Error('4MB 이하 이미지를 선택해 주세요.');
  const mime = photoType_(bytes);
  if (!mime) throw new Error('JPG, PNG, WebP 이미지만 올릴 수 있습니다.');

  const lock = LockService.getDocumentLock();
  lock.waitLock(10000);
  let uploaded;
  try {
    const data = sheet.getRange(row, 1, 1, 10).getDisplayValues()[0];
    if (data[columns.name] !== form.store || data[columns.id] !== (form.storeId || '') ||
        data[columns.photo] !== (form.previousPhoto || '')) {
      throw new Error('점포 행이나 사진 링크가 바뀌었습니다. 창을 닫고 다시 선택해 주세요.');
    }
    const label = data[columns.name].replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|\x00-\x1f]/g, '_').slice(0, 70);
    const stamp = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'Asia/Seoul', 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss');
    const ext = mime === 'image/jpeg' ? 'jpg' : mime.split('/')[1];
    const blob = Utilities.newBlob(bytes, mime, label + '-' + stamp + '.' + ext);
    uploaded = DriveApp.getFolderById(PHOTO_FOLDER_ID).createFile(blob);
    if (form.publicPhoto === 'yes') {
      uploaded.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
    }
    let url = uploaded.getUrl();
    const key = uploaded.getResourceKey();
    if (key && !/[?&]resourcekey=/.test(url)) url += (url.includes('?') ? '&' : '?') + 'resourcekey=' + encodeURIComponent(key);
    sheet.getRange(row, columns.photo + 1).setValue(url);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    return '사진을 Drive에 저장하고 ' + String.fromCharCode(65 + columns.photo) + row + '에 링크를 넣었습니다.';
  } catch (error) {
    if (uploaded && sheet.getRange(row, columns.photo + 1).getDisplayValue() === (form.previousPhoto || '')) {
      try { uploaded.setTrashed(true); } catch {}
    }
    throw error;
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

function photoSheet_(sheetId) {
  const book = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  if (!book || book.getId() !== STORE_SHEET_ID) throw new Error("운영표에서 사진을 올려 주세요.");
  const sheet = sheetId
    ? book.getSheets().find(candidate => String(candidate.getSheetId()) === String(sheetId))
    : book.getSheetByName('웹사이트 점포');
  if (!sheet || !['웹사이트 메뉴', '웹사이트 점포'].includes(sheet.getName())) {
    throw new Error('웹사이트 메뉴 또는 웹사이트 점포 탭에서 행을 선택하세요.');
  }
  photoColumns_(sheet);
  return sheet;
}

function photoColumns_(sheet) {
  const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 10).getDisplayValues()[0];
  if (sheet.getName() === '웹사이트 메뉴' && MENU_COLUMNS.every((value, index) => headers[index] === value)) {
    return { name: 1, id: 6, photo: 5 };
  }
  if (sheet.getName() === '웹사이트 점포' && headers[1] === '점포명' && headers[9] === '점포 사진 (Drive 링크)') {
    return { name: 1, id: 8, photo: 9 };
  }
  throw new Error('운영표의 메뉴 및 사진 열을 확인해 주세요.');
}

function photoType_(bytes) {
  const starts = (signature, offset = 0) => signature.every((value, index) => (bytes[offset + index] & 255) === value);
  if (starts([255, 216, 255])) return 'image/jpeg';
  if (starts([137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10])) return 'image/png';
  if (starts([82, 73, 70, 70]) && starts([87, 69, 66, 80], 8)) return 'image/webp';
  return null;
}

const PHOTO_DIALOG_HTML = `<!doctype html>
<html lang="ko"><head><base target="_top"><style>
body{font:14px/1.5 system-ui,sans-serif;margin:20px;color:#17231c}p{margin:0 0 12px}
input[type=file]{display:block;margin-bottom:16px;width:100%}
label{display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:7px;margin-bottom:16px}
button{border:0;border-radius:5px;background:#22613d;color:#fff;padding:9px 14px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer}
button:disabled{opacity:.55;cursor:default}
#message{min-height:24px;margin-top:10px;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
</style></head><body>
<p><strong><?= store ?></strong> · <?= row ?>행</p>
<form id="upload">
  <input type="hidden" name="row" value="<?= row ?>">
  <input type="hidden" name="sheetId" value="<?= sheetId ?>">
  <input type="hidden" name="store" value="<?= store ?>">
  <input type="hidden" name="storeId" value="<?= storeId ?>">
  <input type="hidden" name="previousPhoto" value="<?= previousPhoto ?>">
  <input type="file" name="photo" aria-label="PC의 점포 사진 선택" accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp" required>
  <p>4MB 이하 JPG·PNG·WebP · 업로드 시 웹용으로 자동 최적화됩니다.</p>
  <p><?= previousPhoto ? '현재 링크를 새 사진으로 교체합니다.' : photoColumn + '열에 링크를 넣습니다.' ?></p>
  <label><input type="checkbox" name="publicPhoto" value="yes" <? if (published) { ?>checked<? } ?>> 링크가 있는 사람에게 이 사진의 보기 권한 부여</label>
  <button id="submit" type="submit">Drive에 올리기</button>
  <button type="button" onclick="google.script.host.close()">닫기</button>
</form>
<p id="message" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p>
<script>
const form=document.getElementById('upload');
const submit=document.getElementById('submit');
const message=document.getElementById('message');
async function optimizePhoto(photo) {
  const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(photo);
  try {
    const scale = Math.min(1, 900 / Math.max(bitmap.width, bitmap.height));
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(bitmap.width * scale));
    canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(bitmap.height * scale));
    const context = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!context) return photo;
    context.drawImage(bitmap, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    const webp = await new Promise(resolve => canvas.toBlob(resolve, 'image/webp', 0.78));
    return webp?.type === 'image/webp' && webp.size < photo.size ? webp : photo;
  } finally {
    bitmap.close();
  }
}

function uploadFailed(text) {
  message.textContent = text;
  submit.disabled = false;
  form.elements.photo.disabled = false;
}

form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
  event.preventDefault();
  const photo = form.elements.photo.files[0];
  if (!photo) return;
  if (photo.size > 4*1024*1024) { uploadFailed('4MB 이하 이미지를 선택해 주세요.'); return; }
  if (!['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp'].includes(photo.type)) { uploadFailed('JPG, PNG, WebP 이미지를 선택해 주세요.'); return; }
  const payload = Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form));
  delete payload.photo;
  submit.disabled = true;
  form.elements.photo.disabled = true;
  message.textContent = '웹용 사진을 준비하고 있습니다.';
  let optimized;
  try {
    optimized = typeof createImageBitmap === 'function' ? await optimizePhoto(photo) : photo;
  } catch {
    uploadFailed('이미지를 열 수 없습니다. 다른 사진을 선택해 주세요.');
    return;
  }
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onerror = () => uploadFailed('PC에서 파일을 읽을 수 없습니다.');
  reader.onload = () => {
    message.textContent = '사진을 Drive에 업로드하고 있습니다.';
    payload.encodedPhoto = String(reader.result).split(',')[1];
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(text => { message.textContent = text; })
      .withFailureHandler(error => uploadFailed(error.message))
      .saveStorePhoto(payload);
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(optimized);
});
</script></body></html>`;

```

### deploy/store-sheet.ko.md

SHA-256 76db97102e6e3a030fe78925325a7de08d801f1fc8f43acf7e0d738e831fb3b5

```markdown
# 웹사이트 대표 메뉴 운영표 연결

같은 Google 스프레드시트의 `웹사이트 메뉴` 탭에서 대표 메뉴를 관리합니다. 기존 `웹사이트 점포` 탭은 점포별 원본 자료로 보존합니다.

1. A열 공개 상태, B열 대표 메뉴, C열 설명, D열 시장 내 위치한 대표메뉴 점포들을 입력합니다. 점포명은 한 줄에 하나씩 적습니다.
2. E열에서 다섯 가지 미식루트 중 하나를 선택합니다. 같은 메뉴가 여러 루트에 해당하면 루트별 행으로 나눕니다.
3. 사진을 올릴 메뉴 행을 선택하고 상단 `점포 사진 → PC에서 업로드`를 실행합니다. 최적화된 사진을 기존 Drive 폴더에 저장하고 F열에 연결합니다. 웹사이트 표시용 사진에는 링크 보기 권한을 부여합니다.
4. 숨긴 G열은 메뉴 ID입니다. 기존 값을 유지합니다. 새 행은 비워 두어도 연결 코드가 ID를 만듭니다.
5. 공개 상태·대표 메뉴·미식루트가 모두 채워진 행만 사이트로 전달됩니다. 설명과 사진은 비워 둘 수 있습니다.

`deploy/market-stores.gs`와 `deploy/market-store-photo-upload.gs`를 기존 바운드 Apps Script 프로젝트에 저장합니다. 현재 웹 앱의 새 버전을 배포하면 기존 점포용 `stores`와 새 메뉴용 `menuItems`를 함께 반환합니다.

새 웹사이트의 `api/public-stores.mjs`는 `menuItems`를 우선 사용하고, 새 항목이 없는 이전 연결에서는 기존 점포 자료를 읽습니다. 카드에서는 같은 메뉴의 점포명을 모으고 긴 목록을 두 줄에서 줄입니다. 상세 팝업에서는 전체 목록을 보여 줍니다.

시트 연결 변경과 웹사이트 코드는 별도로 배포합니다. 이후 열린 페이지는 30초 간격으로 새 목록을 확인합니다.
```

### src/app/(frontend)/HomeExperience.tsx

SHA-256 90bd0779a3232d8abcf99f6af03fc06d3b56b786797fa002311e375de2211d05

```tsx
'use client'

import Image from 'next/image'

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import MenuSelect from './MenuSelect'
import { createDemoStores } from '@/lib/demo-stores'
import { createMenuItems, type MenuItem } from '@/lib/menu-items'
import type { HomeMedia, HomeRoute, HomeStore } from '@/lib/home-data'

import type { CSSProperties } from 'react'
import { storeSchema, type SiteCopy } from '@/lib/content-schema'
import SiteHeader from './SiteHeader'
import SiteFooter from './SiteFooter'
import PatternRibbon from './PatternRibbon'
import StoreModal from './StoreModal'
import MarketMap from './MarketMap'
import { getRouteColors } from '@/lib/route-colors'

function routeStyle(id: string, index = 0): CSSProperties {
  const palette = getRouteColors(id, index)
  return { '--route-color': palette.color, '--route-accent': palette.accent, '--route-tint': palette.tint, '--route-ink': palette.ink, '--route-strong': palette.strong } as CSSProperties
}

export default function HomeExperience({
  site,
  routes,
  stores,
  detailMode = false,
  initialRouteID,
  publicFeed = false,
}: {
  site: SiteCopy
  routes: HomeRoute[]
  stores: HomeStore[]
  showDemo?: boolean
  detailMode?: boolean
  initialRouteID?: string
  publicFeed?: boolean
}) {
  const [selectedStore, setSelectedStore] = useState<MenuItem | null>(null)
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1)
  const feedPath = publicFeed ? process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_STORE_FEED_PATH : undefined
  const [sheetStores, setSheetStores] = useState<HomeStore[] | null>(null)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!feedPath) return
    const controller = new AbortController()
    let loading = false
    const refresh = async () => {
      if (loading || document.hidden) return
      loading = true
      try {
        const response = await fetch(feedPath, { signal: controller.signal })
        if (!response.ok) return
        const data = await response.json()
        const parsed = storeSchema.array().max(2000).safeParse(data.stores)
        if (parsed.success && !controller.signal.aborted) {
          setSheetStores(parsed.data.filter(store => store.isVisible))
        }
      } catch {
        // Keep the latest successful list during a temporary connection issue.
      } finally {
        loading = false
      }
    }
    void refresh()
    const timer = window.setInterval(refresh, 30_000)
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', refresh)
    return () => {
      controller.abort()
      window.clearInterval(timer)
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', refresh)
    }
  }, [feedPath])
  const sourceStores = useMemo(() => {
    if (feedPath) return sheetStores ?? stores
    return stores.length === 0 ? createDemoStores(routes) : stores
  }, [feedPath, sheetStores, routes, stores])
  const [activeRouteID, setActiveRouteID] = useState(initialRouteID ?? routes[0]?.id ?? '')
  const [selectedMenu, setSelectedMenu] = useState('')
  const tabsRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const activeRoute = routes.find((route) => route.id === activeRouteID) ?? routes[0]
  useEffect(() => {
    const container = tabsRef.current
    const selected = container?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[aria-selected="true"]')
    if (!container || !selected || container.scrollWidth <= container.clientWidth) return
    const left = selected.offsetLeft
    if (left < container.scrollLeft || left + selected.offsetWidth > container.scrollLeft + container.clientWidth) {
      container.scrollTo({ left: Math.max(0, left - (container.clientWidth - selected.offsetWidth) / 2), behavior: matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches ? 'auto' : 'smooth' })
    }
  }, [activeRoute?.id])
  const routeStores = useMemo(() => sourceStores.filter(store => activeRoute && store.routeIds.includes(activeRoute.id)), [activeRoute, sourceStores])
  const menuItems = useMemo(() => createMenuItems(routeStores), [routeStores])
  const menuOptions = [...new Set(menuItems.map(item => item.menu))]
    .sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'ko'))
  const currentMenu = menuOptions.includes(selectedMenu) ? selectedMenu : ''
  const visibleStores = menuItems.filter(item => !currentMenu || item.menu === currentMenu)

  const [pageSize, setPageSize] = useState(8)
  useEffect(() => {
    const mobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 767px)')
    const updatePageSize = () => { setPageSize(mobile.matches ? 4 : 8); setPage(1) }
    updatePageSize()
    mobile.addEventListener('change', updatePageSize)
    return () => mobile.removeEventListener('change', updatePageSize)
  }, [])
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(visibleStores.length / pageSize))
  const currentPage = Math.min(page, totalPages)
  const pagedStores = visibleStores.slice((currentPage - 1) * pageSize, currentPage * pageSize)
  const poster = site.festival.poster

  return (
    <div className={detailMode ? "site-shell route-detail-shell" : "site-shell"} style={{ '--navy': site.design.navy, '--ink': site.design.navy, '--cream': site.design.cream, '--white': site.design.white, '--blue': site.design.blue, '--muted-background': site.design.mutedBackground, '--yellow': site.design.yellow, '--content-font-size': site.design.fontSize + 'px', '--logo-seconds': site.design.logoSeconds + 's' } as CSSProperties}>
      <SiteHeader site={site} />

      <main id="top">
        {detailMode && <div className="detail-page-inner route-detail-heading"><a className="detail-breadcrumb" href="/">홈 / 미식루트</a><header className="detail-page-heading"><span>{site.routePage.eyebrow}</span><h1>{site.routePage.title}</h1><p>{site.routePage.description}</p></header></div>}
        {!detailMode && <><section className="hero hero-editorial"><div className="hero-inner">
          <div aria-hidden="true" className="hero-shape hero-shape-one" />
          <div aria-hidden="true" className="hero-shape hero-shape-two" />
          <div className="hero-label">
            <span>KOREA GRAND FESTIVAL</span>
          </div>
          <div className="hero-card">
            <div className="hero-copy">
              <h1>
                <span>{site.hero.titleLine1}</span>
                <span>{site.hero.titleLine2}</span>
              </h1>
              <p className="hero-description">{site.hero.description}</p>
              <dl className="hero-event-info">
                <div><dt className="sr-only">행사 일시</dt><svg aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.6"><rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="16" rx="1"/><path d="M7 2v6m10-6v6M3 11h18M7 15h2m3 0h2m3 0h1M7 18h2m3 0h2"/></svg><dd>{site.hero.eventDate}</dd></div>
              </dl>
              <a className="primary-button" href="#routes" data-ga-event="taste_routes_click">
                {site.hero.ctaLabel} <span aria-hidden="true">↘</span>
              </a>
            </div>
            <figure className="hero-figure">
              <div className="hero-image">
                {site.hero.image ? (
                  <Image unoptimized
                    alt={site.hero.image.alt}
                    height={site.hero.image.height}
                    priority
                    sizes="(max-width: 700px) 100vw, 360px"
                    src={site.hero.image.url}
                    width={site.hero.image.width}
                  />
                ) : (
                  <span className="image-empty">대표 이미지를 등록해주세요.</span>
                )}
              </div>
              <figcaption>
                <span>UPCOMING</span>
                {site.hero.caption}
              </figcaption>
            </figure>
            <div className="hero-foot" aria-hidden="true" />
          </div>
          <a aria-label="맛길 섹션으로 이동" className="scroll-cue" href="#routes">
            <b aria-hidden="true">↓</b>
          </a>
        </div></section>

        <PatternRibbon /></>}

        <div className="content-wrap">
          <section className="routes-section" id="routes" style={routeStyle(activeRoute?.id ?? '', routes.findIndex(route => route.id === activeRoute?.id))}><div className="routes-inner">
            <div className="routes-heading">
              <span className="section-label">01 / TASTE ROUTES</span>
              <h2>{detailMode ? site.routePage.sectionTitle : site.routesSection.title}</h2>
            </div>

            {routes.length > 0 ? (
              <>
                <div ref={tabsRef} className="tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="미식루트">
                  {routes.map((route, index) => (
                    <button
                      aria-controls="route-panel"
                      aria-selected={activeRoute?.id === route.id}
                      className={activeRoute?.id === route.id ? 'active' : ''}
                      key={route.id}
                      style={routeStyle(route.id, index)}
                      data-ga-event="taste_route_select" data-ga-item={route.id}
                      onClick={() => { setActiveRouteID(route.id); setSelectedMenu(''); setPage(1) }}
                      role="tab"
                      type="button"
                    >
                      {route.name}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>

                <div className="route-panel" id="route-panel" role="tabpanel">
                  <div className="panel-head">
                    <div>
                      <h3>{activeRoute?.name}</h3>
                      <p>{activeRoute?.introduction || activeRoute?.description}</p>
                    </div>
                    <MenuSelect key={activeRoute?.id} options={menuOptions} value={currentMenu} onChange={value => { setSelectedMenu(value); setPage(1) }} />
                  </div>


                  <div className="store-grid">
                    {visibleStores.length > 0 ? (
                      pagedStores.map((store) => (
                        <article className="store-card menu-card" key={store.id} data-ga-event="store_detail_open" data-ga-item={store.id} onClick={() => setSelectedStore(store)}>
                          <div className="store-card-content">
                          <h3>{store.menu}</h3>
                          <p className="menu-card-stores" title={store.storeNames.join(" · ")}>
                            <span className="sr-only">시장 내 판매 점포 </span>
                            {store.storeNames.join(" · ") || "점포 정보 준비 중"}
                          </p>
                          <button className="store-detail-button" type="button" aria-label={store.menu + " " + site.routesSection.detailLabel}><span className="store-detail-label">{site.routesSection.detailLabel}</span><span aria-hidden="true">→</span></button>
                          </div>
                        </article>
                      ))
                    ) : (
                      <div className="empty-result"><strong>{site.routesSection.preparingTitle}</strong><p>{site.routesSection.preparingDescription}</p></div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <nav className="store-pagination" aria-label="메뉴 페이지"><button type="button" disabled={currentPage === 1} onClick={() => setPage(currentPage - 1)} aria-label="이전 메뉴 페이지">← 이전</button><span><strong>{currentPage}</strong> / {totalPages}</span><button type="button" disabled={currentPage === totalPages} onClick={() => setPage(currentPage + 1)} aria-label="다음 메뉴 페이지">다음 →</button></nav>
                  <div className="route-map"><MarketMap copy={site.mapPage} /></div>
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <div className="empty-result routes-empty">
                {site.routesSection.preparingTitle}
              </div>
            )}
          </div></section>

          {!detailMode && site.festival.isVisible && <PatternRibbon reverse />}

          {!detailMode && site.festival.isVisible && <section className="festival" id="festival"><div className="festival-inner">
            <div className="festival-copy">
              <span className="section-label">02 / MARKET NIGHT</span>
              <h2>{site.festival.heading}</h2>
              <div className="festival-date">
                <b>{site.festival.date}</b>
                <span>{site.festival.schedule}</span>
              </div>
              <p>
                {site.festival.location}
                <br />
                {site.festival.programHours}
              </p>
              <small>{site.festival.note}</small><a className="primary-button festival-detail-link" href="/festival" data-ga-event="festival_schedule_click">{site.festival.detailLabel} <span aria-hidden="true">↗</span></a>
            </div>
            {poster && (
              <a className="poster-link" href={poster.url} rel="noreferrer" target="_blank">
                <Image unoptimized
                  alt={poster.alt}
                  height={poster.height}
                  sizes="(max-width: 700px) 100vw, 330px"
                  src={poster.url}
                  width={poster.width}
                />
              </a>
            )}
          </div></section>}

          <p className="notice">{site.notice}</p>

          <SiteFooter site={site} />
        </div>
      </main>
      <StoreModal themeID={activeRoute?.id} site={site} store={selectedStore ? menuItems.find(store => store.id === selectedStore.id) ?? null : null} routes={routes} onClose={() => setSelectedStore(null)} />
    </div>
  )
}
```

### src/app/(frontend)/SiteHeader.tsx

SHA-256 e5ac165ad016f616ae67b7e4cb9bed4d9bab6823d1bc3977e1c5a3a58e217722

```tsx
'use client'
import Image from 'next/image'
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import type { CSSProperties } from 'react'
import type { SiteCopy } from '@/lib/content-schema'

export default function SiteHeader({ site }: { site: SiteCopy }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [origin, setOrigin] = useState({ x: 'calc(100% - 50px)', y: '48px' })
  const [menuInset, setMenuInset] = useState('24px')
  const button = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const overlay = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const locate = () => { const cross = document.querySelector('.shared-header .header-logo-cross')?.getBoundingClientRect(); if (cross) setMenuInset(window.innerWidth < 768 ? '24px' : `${cross.right}px`); const box = button.current?.getBoundingClientRect(); if (box) setOrigin({ x: `${box.x + box.width / 2}px`, y: `${box.y + box.height / 2}px` }) }
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return
    const oldOverflow = document.body.style.overflow
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'
    const content = [...document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.site-shell > main, .site-shell > footer, .site-shell > .sponsor-loop')]
    const previous = content.map(node => node.inert)
    content.forEach(node => { node.inert = true })
    const trap = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') { setOpen(false); button.current?.focus() }
      if (event.key === 'Tab') {
        const focusable = [button.current, ...overlay.current!.querySelectorAll<HTMLAnchorElement>('a')].filter(Boolean) as HTMLElement[]
        const current = focusable.indexOf(document.activeElement as HTMLElement)
        event.preventDefault(); focusable[(current + (event.shiftKey ? -1 : 1) + focusable.length) % focusable.length]?.focus()
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('keydown', trap); window.addEventListener('resize', locate)
    return () => { document.body.style.overflow = oldOverflow; content.forEach((node,index) => { node.inert = previous[index] }); document.removeEventListener('keydown', trap); window.removeEventListener('resize', locate) }
  }, [open])
  return <>
    <header className={'topbar shared-header' + (open ? ' menu-is-open' : '')}><div className="topbar-inner">
      <a className="header-logos" href="/" aria-label={site.brand + ' 홈'}>
        <span className="header-logo-stack">
          <Image unoptimized priority className="festival-logo logo-layer logo-layer-dark" src="/assets/navigation/Korea-Grand-Festival_logo_black.svg" alt="코리아그랜드페스티벌" width={304} height={170} />
          <Image unoptimized priority className="festival-logo logo-layer logo-layer-light" src="/assets/navigation/Korea-Grand-Festival_logo_white.svg" alt="" aria-hidden="true" width={138} height={74} />
        </span>
        <span className="header-logo-cross" aria-hidden="true">|</span>
        <span className="header-logo-stack">
          <Image unoptimized priority className="market-logo logo-layer logo-layer-dark" src="/assets/navigation/Market-Tonight_logo_black.svg" alt="Market Tonight" width={487} height={180} />
          <Image unoptimized priority className="market-logo logo-layer logo-layer-light" src="/assets/navigation/Market-Tonight_logo_white.svg" alt="" aria-hidden="true" width={99} height={37} />
        </span>
      </a>
      <button ref={button} type="button" className={'menu-trigger fixed-menu-toggle' + (open ? ' is-open' : '')} aria-controls="site-menu" aria-expanded={open} data-ga-event={open ? "site_menu_close" : "site_menu_open"} aria-label={open ? '전체 메뉴 닫기' : '전체 메뉴 열기'} onClick={() => { locate(); setOpen(!open) }}><span /><span /><span /></button>
    </div></header>
    <div ref={overlay} id="site-menu" className={'radial-menu' + (open ? ' is-open' : '')} role="dialog" aria-modal={open ? true : undefined} aria-label="전체 메뉴" aria-hidden={!open} inert={!open} style={{ '--menu-inset': menuInset, '--menu-x': origin.x, '--menu-y': origin.y } as CSSProperties}>
      <div className="radial-menu-content"><p>GUWOL MORAENAE / MARKET TONIGHT</p><nav aria-label="전체 메뉴 항목">{[['/', '홈', 'HOME'], ['/routes', site.navRoutesLabel, 'TASTE ROUTES'], ['/festival', site.navEventLabel, 'FESTIVAL'], ['/map', '지도', 'MARKET MAP']].map(([href,title,english],index) => <a key={href} href={href} data-ga-event="site_nav_click" data-ga-item={english} onClick={() => setOpen(false)}><span>{String(index + 1).padStart(2,'0')}</span><strong>{title}</strong><small>{english} ↗</small></a>)}</nav><p>{site.menuTagline}</p></div>
    </div>
  </>
}
```

```tsx
import MarketMap from './MarketMap'
import type { SiteCopy } from '@/lib/content-schema'
import SiteFrame from './SiteFrame'
export default function MapExperience({site}:{site:SiteCopy}) {
  const copy=site.mapPage
  return <SiteFrame site={site}><div className="detail-page-inner"><a className="detail-breadcrumb" href="/">홈 / 지도</a><header className="detail-page-heading"><span>{copy.eyebrow}</span><h1>{copy.title}</h1><p>{copy.image ? copy.description : copy.preparingDescription}</p></header><MarketMap copy={copy} /><div className="map-info-grid"><article><span>01</span><h2>미식루트</h2><p>{copy.routesDescription}</p><a href="/routes">{copy.routesLinkLabel} →</a></article><article><span>02</span><h2>행사 공간</h2><p>{copy.eventDescription}</p><a href="/festival">{copy.eventLinkLabel} →</a></article></div></div></SiteFrame>
}
```
