```tsx
'use client'
import { Fragment, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { contentSchema, type Content, type HomeMedia, type HomeStore } from '@/lib/content-schema'
import type { ContentState } from '@/lib/content-store'
import './admin.css'
import './preview-visibility.css'
import ThemeManager from './ThemeManager'
import { imageTypeForFile } from '@/lib/upload-types'

const labels: Record<string, string> = {
  menuTagline: '전체 메뉴 하단 문구', browseLabel: '미식루트 이동 버튼', countSuffix: '점포 수 단위', sectionTitle: '상세 페이지 맛길 제목', linkedEyebrow: '연계 행사 안내 제목', linkedDescription: '연계 행사 혜택', linkedNote: '연계 행사 유의사항', routesDescription: '지도 미식루트 소개', eventDescription: '지도 행사 공간 소개', routesLinkLabel: '지도 미식루트 버튼', eventLinkLabel: '지도 행사 안내 버튼',
  timetable: '시간표 이미지', timetableTitle: '시간표 제목', programTitle: '프로그램 영역 제목', programs: '프로그램 목록', time: '시간', place: '장소', color: '포인트 색상', linkedTitle: '연계 행사 제목', linkedSchedule: '연계 행사 일정', linkedLocation: '연계 행사 장소', placeholderTitle: '지도 준비 중 제목', placeholderDescription: '지도 준비 중 설명',
  brand: '상단 사이트 이름', metaTitle: '브라우저·검색 제목', metaDescription: '검색 소개', navRoutesLabel: '미식루트 메뉴', navEventLabel: '행사 메뉴',
  kicker: '히어로 상단 영문', brandTitle: '행사 영문 이름', eventDate: '히어로 행사 일시', eventLocation: '히어로 행사 장소', programLine: '히어로 프로그램 목록',
  eyebrow: '표지 탭', titleLine1: '큰 제목 첫째 줄', titleLine2: '큰 제목 둘째 줄', description: '소개', ctaLabel: '맛길 펼치기 버튼', image: '대표 사진', caption: '사진 아래 문구', footLeft: '표지 하단 왼쪽', footRight: '표지 하단 오른쪽',
  title: '제목', selectedLabel: '선택한 맛길 안내', searchPlaceholder: '검색창 안내', countLabel: '점포 수 안내', advisory: '확인 안내', emptyTitle: '검색 결과 없음 제목', emptyMessage: '검색 결과 없음 설명', preparingTitle: '점포 준비 중 제목', preparingDescription: '점포 준비 중 설명', resetLabel: '검색 초기화 버튼', priceLabel: '가격 항목', hoursLabel: '영업시간 항목', unconfirmedLabel: '미확인 정보 표시', menuUnconfirmedLabel: '대표 상품 미확인 표시', detailLabel: '자세히 보기 버튼',
  isVisible: '고객페이지에 표시', heading: '행사 제목', name: '이름', highlights: '프로그램 소개', date: '행사 날짜', schedule: '행사 시간', location: '행사 장소', programHours: '추가 운영시간', note: '추가 안내', linkLabel: '포스터 버튼', poster: '행사 포스터',
  notice: '하단 유의사항', headingLine1: '마무리 제목 첫째 줄', headingLine2: '마무리 제목 둘째 줄', tagline: '하단 소개', organization: '운영 주체', analyticsNote: '하단 안내 문구', adminLabel: '관리자 작업실 버튼',
  navy: '기본 글자·남색', cream: '페이지 배경', white: '목록·카드 배경', blue: '선택·강조', mutedBackground: '하단·보조 배경', yellow: '표지·노란색', fontSize: '기본 글자 크기', logoSeconds: '기관 띠 이동 속도(클수록 느림)',
}
const sections = [ ['site', '사이트 이름·메뉴'], ['hero', '첫 화면'], ['routesSection', '맛길 안내·점포 목록'], ['festival', '행사 안내'], ['routePage', '미식루트 상세 페이지'], ['festivalPage', '행사 안내 상세 페이지'], ['mapPage', '지도 페이지·이미지'], ['footer', '하단 문구'], ['design', '색상·디자인'] ]
function PhotoField({ label, value, onChange, onError }: { label: string; value: HomeMedia | null; onChange: (value: HomeMedia | null) => void; onError: (message: string) => void }) {
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [uploadError, setUploadError] = useState('')
  const [uploaded, setUploaded] = useState<{ url: string; name: string } | null>(null)
  const fail = (message: string) => { setUploadError(message); onError(message) }
  return <div className="admin-photo"><strong>{label}</strong><p>JPG·PNG·WebP·SVG · 최대 5MB. SVG는 안전한 이미지로 변환합니다. 고객페이지 반영 전에는 관리자에게만 보입니다.</p>
    {value && <><img src={value.url} alt={value.alt} onError={() => setUploadError('첨부된 이미지를 불러오지 못했습니다. 로그인 상태를 확인한 뒤 다시 첨부해주세요.')} /><label>사진 설명<input value={value.alt} onChange={e => onChange({ ...value, alt: e.target.value })} /></label><button type="button" onClick={() => { onChange(null); setUploaded(null); setUploadError('') }}>사진 선택 해제</button></>}
    <div className="admin-file-actions"><label className="admin-file-picker" aria-disabled={busy}><span>{busy ? '업로드 중…' : value ? '파일 교체' : '＋ 파일 첨부'}</span>
      <input className="admin-file-input" aria-label={`${label} 업로드`} disabled={busy} type="file" accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,image/svg+xml,.jpg,.jpeg,.png,.webp,.svg" onChange={async event => {
        const input = event.currentTarget
        const file = input.files?.[0]
        if (!file) return
        setUploadError('')
        if (!imageTypeForFile(file)) { fail('JPG·PNG·WebP·SVG 파일을 선택해주세요.'); input.value = ''; return }
        if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) { fail('사진은 5MB 이하로 올려주세요.'); input.value = ''; return }
        setBusy(true)
        try {
          const body = new FormData(); body.append('file', file)
          const response = await fetch('/api/admin/upload', { method: 'POST', body })
          const data = await response.json()
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || '업로드에 실패했습니다.')
          onChange(data)
          setUploaded({ url: data.url, name: file.name })
        } catch (error) { fail((error as Error).message || '업로드에 실패했습니다.') }
        finally { setBusy(false); input.value = '' }
      }} />
    </label><span>{value ? '이미지가 첨부되어 있습니다.' : '버튼을 눌러 이미지를 선택하세요.'}</span></div>
    {busy && <p role="status">사진을 처리하고 있습니다…</p>}
    {uploadError && <p className="admin-error" role="alert">{uploadError}</p>}
    {!uploadError && uploaded?.url === value?.url && uploaded && <p role="status">첨부 완료: {uploaded.name}. 임시저장 또는 고객페이지 반영을 눌러 저장하세요.</p>}
  </div>
}

export default function AdminWorkspace({ initial, defaults }: { initial: ContentState; defaults: Content }) {
  const [content, setContent] = useState(initial.draft)
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(initial)
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('copy')
  const [selectedSection, setSection] = useState('site')
  const section = sections.some(([key]) => key === selectedSection) ? selectedSection : 'site'
  const [mobile, setMobile] = useState(true)
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('')
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState('all')
  const [visibility, setVisibility] = useState('all')
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<HomeStore | null>(null)
  const [storeError, setStoreError] = useState('')
  const [dirty, setDirty] = useState(false)
  const [programIndex, setProgramIndex] = useState(0)
  const [previewRouteId, setPreviewRouteId] = useState('')
  const previewPage = tab === 'themes' || tab === 'stores' ? 'routePage' : section
  const previewContent = editing ? { ...content, stores: content.stores.some(store => store.id === editing.id) ? content.stores.map(store => store.id === editing.id ? editing : store) : [...content.stores, editing] } : content
  const previewIssue = contentSchema.safeParse(previewContent).error?.issues[0]?.message
  const iframe = useRef<HTMLIFrameElement>(null)
  const dialog = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null)
  const sectionRef = useRef(previewPage); sectionRef.current = previewPage
  const previewRouteRef = useRef(previewRouteId); previewRouteRef.current = previewRouteId
  const contentRef = useRef(previewContent); contentRef.current = previewContent
  useEffect(() => {
    const receive = (event: MessageEvent) => { if (event.origin === location.origin && event.source === iframe.current?.contentWindow && event.data?.type === 'market-preview-ready') iframe.current?.contentWindow?.postMessage({ type: 'market-preview', page: sectionRef.current, routeId: previewRouteRef.current, content: contentRef.current }, location.origin) }
    addEventListener('message', receive); return () => removeEventListener('message', receive)
  }, [])
  useEffect(() => { iframe.current?.contentWindow?.postMessage({ type: 'market-preview', page: previewPage, routeId: previewRouteId, content: previewContent }, location.origin) }, [content, editing, previewPage, previewRouteId])
  useEffect(() => { const warn = (event: BeforeUnloadEvent) => { if (dirty) event.preventDefault() }; addEventListener('beforeunload', warn); return () => removeEventListener('beforeunload', warn) }, [dirty])
  useEffect(() => { if (editing) dialog.current?.showModal(); else dialog.current?.close() }, [editing?.id])
  const update = (next: Content) => { setContent(next); setDirty(true); setMessage('수정 중인 내용입니다. 고객페이지 반영 전에는 공개되지 않습니다.') }
  const updateSite = (key: string, value: unknown) => { setContent(current => ({ ...current, site: { ...current.site, [key]: value } })); setDirty(true); setMessage('수정 중인 내용입니다. 고객페이지 반영 전에는 공개되지 않습니다.') }
  const updateGroup = (group: string, key: string, value: unknown) => {
    setContent(previous => {
      const current = previous.site[group as keyof Content['site']] as object
      const site = { ...previous.site, [group]: { ...current, [key]: value } }
      if (['festival', 'festivalPage'].includes(group) && ['date', 'schedule', 'location'].includes(key)) {
        if (group === 'festival') site.festivalPage = { ...previous.site.festivalPage, [key]: value }
        else site.festival = { ...previous.site.festival, [key]: value }
      }
      return { ...previous, site }
    })
    setDirty(true); setMessage('수정 중인 내용입니다. 고객페이지 반영 전에는 공개되지 않습니다.')
  }
  const report = (text: string) => setMessage(text)
  const save = async (publish: boolean) => {
    setBusy(true); setMessage('저장 중입니다…')
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/content', { method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ content, revision: saved.revision, publish }) })
      const data = await res.json(); if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error)
      setSaved(data); setContent(data.draft); setDirty(false)
      setMessage(publish ? '고객페이지에 반영했습니다. 새로고침하면 변경 내용이 보입니다.' : '임시저장했습니다. 고객페이지는 아직 바뀌지 않았습니다.')
    } catch (error) { setMessage((error as Error).message || '저장에 실패했습니다.') } finally { setBusy(false) }
  }
  const reload = async () => {
    if (dirty && !confirm('저장하지 않은 수정을 버리고 다시 불러올까요?')) return
    setBusy(true)
    try { const res = await fetch('/api/admin/content', { cache: 'no-store' }); const data = await res.json(); if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error); setContent(data.draft); setSaved(data); setDirty(false); setMessage('저장된 내용을 불러왔습니다.') } catch (error) { report((error as Error).message) } finally { setBusy(false) }
  }
  const fields = (group: string, values: Record<string, unknown>) => Object.entries(values).map(([key, value]) => {
    const label = group === 'mapPage' && key === 'preparingDescription' ? '지도 준비 중 소개' : group === 'mapPage' && key === 'description' ? '지도 등록 후 소개' : group === 'festival' && key === 'detailLabel' ? '전체 프로그램 및 시간표 버튼' : labels[key] || key
    const change = (next: unknown) => group === 'site' ? updateSite(key, next) : updateGroup(group, key, next)
    if ((group === 'festival' && key === 'linkLabel') || (group === 'routesSection' && ['searchPlaceholder', 'emptyTitle', 'emptyMessage', 'resetLabel'].includes(key)) || (group === 'hero' && ['footLeft', 'footRight'].includes(key))) return null
    if (['image', 'poster', 'timetable'].includes(key)) return <PhotoField key={`${group}.${key}`} label={label} value={value as HomeMedia | null} onChange={change} onError={report} />
    if (group === 'festivalPage' && key === 'programs' && Array.isArray(value)) {
      const index = Math.min(programIndex, Math.max(0, value.length - 1))
      const selected = value[index] as Content['site']['festivalPage']['programs'][number] | undefined
      return <div key={key} className="admin-program-editor"><h3>{label}</h3><label>편집할 행사 선택<select aria-label="편집할 행사 선택" value={value.length ? index : ''} onChange={e => setProgramIndex(Number(e.target.value))}>{!value.length && <option value="">등록된 행사가 없습니다</option>}{value.map((program, i) => <option key={i} value={i}>{program.title || '제목 없는 행사'}</option>)}</select></label>
        {selected && <fieldset><legend>{selected.title || '행사 상세'}</legend>{Object.entries(selected).map(([field, entry]) => <label key={field}>{labels[field] || field}{field === 'color' ? <select aria-label={labels[field] || field} value={entry} onChange={e => change(value.map((item, i) => i === index ? { ...item, [field]: e.target.value } : item))}>{['yellow', 'blue', 'orange', 'olive'].map(color => <option key={color} value={color}>{{ yellow: '노랑', blue: '파랑', orange: '주황', olive: '검정' }[color]}</option>)}</select> : <textarea aria-label={labels[field] || field} value={entry} maxLength={4000} onChange={e => change(value.map((item, i) => i === index ? { ...item, [field]: e.target.value } : item))} />}</label>)}<button type="button" onClick={() => { if (confirm('선택한 행사를 목록에서 삭제할까요?')) { change(value.filter((_, i) => i !== index)); setProgramIndex(Math.max(0, index - 1)) } }}>선택한 행사 삭제</button><button type="button" disabled={index === 0} onClick={() => { const next = [...value]; [next[index - 1], next[index]] = [next[index], next[index - 1]]; change(next); setProgramIndex(index - 1) }}>위로 이동</button><button type="button" disabled={index === value.length - 1} onClick={() => { const next = [...value]; [next[index + 1], next[index]] = [next[index], next[index + 1]]; change(next); setProgramIndex(index + 1) }}>아래로 이동</button></fieldset>}
        <button type="button" disabled={value.length >= 50} onClick={() => { change([...value, { title: '새 행사', time: '', place: '', description: '', color: 'blue' }]); setProgramIndex(value.length) }}>행사 추가</button></div>
    }
    if (typeof value === 'boolean') return <label key={key} className="admin-check"><input type="checkbox" checked={value} onChange={e => change(e.target.checked)} />{label}</label>
    if (typeof value === 'number') return <label key={key}>{label}<input aria-label={label} type="number" min={key === 'fontSize' ? 12 : 10} max={key === 'fontSize' ? 22 : 90} value={value} onChange={e => change(Number(e.target.value))} /></label>
    if (group === 'design') return <label key={key}>{label}<div className="admin-color"><input aria-label={`${label} 선택`} type="color" value={String(value)} onChange={e => change(e.target.value)} /><input aria-label={label} value={String(value)} maxLength={7} pattern="#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}" onChange={e => change(e.target.value)} /></div></label>
    return <Fragment key={key}>{group === 'routesSection' && key === 'selectedLabel' && <label>미리볼 맛길<select aria-label="미리볼 맛길" value={previewRouteId} onChange={e => setPreviewRouteId(e.target.value)}><option value="">첫 번째 공개 맛길</option>{content.routes.filter(route => route.isVisible).map(route => <option key={route.id} value={route.id}>{route.name}</option>)}</select></label>}<label>{label}<textarea aria-label={label} rows={key === 'metaDescription' ? 3 : 2} value={String(value ?? '')} maxLength={['brand', 'metaTitle'].includes(key) ? 160 : 4000} onChange={e => change(e.target.value)} /></label></Fragment>
  })
  const siteRootFields = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(content.site).filter(([key, value]) => typeof value === 'string' && key !== 'notice'))
  const visibleStores = content.stores.filter(s => `${s.name} ${s.menu}`.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) && (theme === 'all' || s.routeIds.includes(theme)) && (visibility === 'all' || s.isVisible === (visibility === 'visible')))
  return <div className="admin-workspace">
    <header className="admin-top"><div><strong>나의 시장 작업실</strong><span>관리자 전용</span></div><nav><a href="/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">고객페이지 열기 ↗</a><button onClick={async () => { if (dirty && !confirm('저장하지 않은 수정이 있습니다. 로그아웃할까요?')) return; const res = await fetch('/api/admin/logout', { method: 'POST' }); if (res.ok) location.assign('/admin/login'); else report('로그아웃에 실패했습니다.') }}>로그아웃</button></nav></header>
    <nav className="admin-tabs" aria-label="관리 항목">{[['copy', '문구·사진·디자인'], ['themes', '미식루트 테마 관리'], ['stores', '점포 관리']].map(([key, title]) => <button key={key} aria-pressed={tab === key} className={tab === key ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => setTab(key)}>{title}</button>)}</nav>
    <div className="admin-savebar"><p role="status">{message || '현재 저장된 초안을 편집 중입니다.'}</p><div><button disabled={busy} onClick={() => save(false)}>임시저장</button><button className="admin-primary" disabled={busy} onClick={() => save(true)}>고객페이지 반영</button></div></div>
    <div className="admin-toolbar"><button disabled={busy} onClick={reload}>저장된 내용 다시 불러오기</button><button onClick={() => { if (confirm('현재 공개된 내용으로 편집 내용을 되돌릴까요?')) update(structuredClone(saved.published)) }}>현재 공개본 불러오기</button><a href="/api/admin/content?download=1">저장 데이터 백업 ↓</a><span>마지막 공개: {saved.publishedAt ? new Date(saved.publishedAt).toLocaleString('ko-KR') : '초기 콘텐츠'}</span></div>
    <div className={`admin-columns ${tab !== 'copy' ? 'stores-layout' : ''}`}><section className="admin-editor" inert={busy}>
      {tab === 'copy' && <><h1>사이트를 직접 편집하세요.</h1><p>문구·사진·색상을 바꾸면 오른쪽 미리보기가 바뀝니다. 고객페이지 반영을 눌러야 방문자에게 공개됩니다.</p><nav className="admin-section-tabs">{sections.map(([key, title]) => <button key={key} className={section === key ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => setSection(key)}>{title}</button>)}</nav><h2>{sections.find(([key]) => key === section)?.[1]}</h2>{['festival', 'festivalPage'].includes(section) && <p>날짜·시간·장소는 행사 안내와 상세 페이지에서 함께 수정됩니다. 기존 값은 편집 전까지 보존됩니다.</p>}{fields(section, section === 'site' ? siteRootFields : content.site[section as keyof Content['site']] as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)}{section === 'footer' && fields('site', { notice: content.site.notice })}<button onClick={() => { if (confirm('이 영역의 기본 문구와 설정을 불러올까요?')) { if (section === 'site') { const next = { ...content.site }; for (const key of Object.keys(siteRootFields)) (next as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[key] = (defaults.site as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[key]; update({ ...content, site: next }) } else updateSite(section, defaults.site[section as keyof Content['site']]) } }}>이 영역 기본값 불러오기</button></>}
      {tab === 'themes' && <ThemeManager routes={content.routes} stores={content.stores} onChange={routes => update({ ...content, routes })} />}
      {tab === 'stores' && <div className="store-manager">
        <div className="store-manager-heading"><div><p className="store-eyebrow">GUWOL MORAENAE / STORES</p><h1>점포 관리<span>{content.stores.length}</span></h1><p>점포를 선택해 정보를 확인하고 수정하세요.</p></div><button className="admin-primary store-add-button" onClick={() => { setStoreError(''); setEditing({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), name: '', menu: '', price: '', hours: '', image: null, routeIds: [], isVisible: false }) }}><span aria-hidden="true">＋</span> 점포 추가</button></div>
        <div className="store-summary" aria-label="점포 노출상태 필터">{[['all', '전체 점포', content.stores.length], ['visible', '공개', content.stores.filter(s => s.isVisible).length], ['hidden', '비공개', content.stores.filter(s => !s.isVisible).length]].map(([key, title, count]) => <button key={key} className={visibility === key ? 'selected' : ''} aria-pressed={visibility === key} onClick={() => setVisibility(String(key))}><span className={'store-status-dot ' + key} aria-hidden="true" />{title}<strong>{count}</strong></button>)}</div>
        <div className="store-list-panel"><div className="store-list-toolbar"><label className="store-search"><span aria-hidden="true">⌕</span><span className="sr-only">점포명·대표 상품 검색</span><input type="search" placeholder="점포명 또는 대표 상품으로 검색" value={search} onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)} /></label><label className="store-theme-filter"><span className="sr-only">미식루트 필터</span><select value={theme} onChange={e => setTheme(e.target.value)}><option value="all">전체 미식루트</option>{content.routes.map(r => <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>{r.name}</option>)}</select></label>{(search || theme !== 'all' || visibility !== 'all') && <button className="store-reset" onClick={() => { setSearch(''); setTheme('all'); setVisibility('all') }}>필터 초기화</button>}</div>
          <div className="store-list-meta"><span>검색 결과 <strong>{visibleStores.length}</strong>개</span><span>행을 누르면 상세 편집창이 열립니다.</span></div>
          <div className="store-list-head" aria-hidden="true"><span>점포 / 대표 상품</span><span>미식루트</span><span>가격대 / 영업시간</span><span>노출상태</span><span /></div>
          <ul className="store-list" aria-label="점포 목록">{visibleStores.map(store => <li key={store.id}><button type="button" className="store-list-row" aria-label={store.name + ' 상세 편집'} onClick={() => { setStoreError(''); setEditing(structuredClone(store)) }}>
            <span className="store-identity"><span className="store-thumbnail">{store.image ? <img src={store.image.url} alt="" /> : <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 10v10h16V10M3 10l2-6h14l2 6M3 10c0 3 4 3 4 0 0 3 5 3 5 0 0 3 5 3 5 0 0 3 4 3 4 0M9 20v-6h6v6" /></svg>}</span><span className="store-name-copy"><strong>{store.name}</strong><span>{store.menu || content.site.routesSection.menuUnconfirmedLabel}</span></span></span>
            <span className="store-theme-tags">{store.routeIds.length ? store.routeIds.map(id => <span key={id}>{content.routes.find(r => r.id === id)?.name || '미선택'}</span>) : <span className="unassigned">미식루트 미선택</span>}</span>
            <span className="store-operating"><span>{store.price || '가격 확인 중'}</span><small>{store.hours || '영업시간 확인 중'}</small></span>
            <span className={store.isVisible ? 'store-visibility is-visible' : 'store-visibility'}><span aria-hidden="true" />{store.isVisible ? '공개' : '비공개'}</span><span className="store-row-arrow" aria-hidden="true">↗</span>
          </button></li>)}</ul>
          {!visibleStores.length && <div className="store-list-empty"><span aria-hidden="true">⌕</span><h2>{content.stores.length ? '조건에 맞는 점포가 없어요' : '첫 번째 점포를 등록해보세요'}</h2><p>{content.stores.length ? '검색어를 바꾸거나 필터를 초기화해보세요.' : '점포 추가 버튼으로 대표 상품과 사진, 미식루트를 등록할 수 있습니다.'}</p><button onClick={() => { if (content.stores.length) { setSearch(''); setTheme('all'); setVisibility('all') } else { setStoreError(''); setEditing({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), name: '', menu: '', price: '', hours: '', image: null, routeIds: [], isVisible: false }) } }}>{content.stores.length ? '필터 초기화' : '점포 추가'}</button></div>}
        </div><p className="store-manager-footnote">구월모래내시장 통합 관리 · 편집 후 임시저장하거나 고객페이지에 반영해주세요.</p>
      </div>}
    </section><aside className="admin-preview"><div className="admin-preview-head"><div><h2>실시간 미리보기</h2><p>수정 중인 내용 · 관리자만 볼 수 있어요</p></div><div><button className={mobile ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => setMobile(true)}>모바일</button><button className={!mobile ? 'active' : ''} onClick={() => setMobile(false)}>PC</button></div></div>{previewIssue && <p role="alert">미리보기는 마지막 정상 입력을 표시합니다: {previewIssue}</p>}<div className="admin-preview-stage"><iframe ref={iframe} title="고객페이지 실시간 미리보기" src="/admin/preview" className={mobile ? 'mobile' : 'desktop'} /></div><p>미리보기 안에서 스크롤하거나 미식루트를 선택해 확인할 수 있습니다.</p></aside></div>
    <dialog className="admin-store-dialog store-editor-dialog" ref={dialog} aria-labelledby="store-editor-title" aria-describedby="store-editor-description" onCancel={() => setEditing(null)} onClose={() => setEditing(null)}>{editing && <form onSubmit={e => {
      e.preventDefault()
      if (!editing.name.trim()) { setStoreError('점포명을 입력해주세요.'); return }
      if (editing.isVisible && !editing.routeIds.length) { setStoreError('공개할 점포는 미식루트를 하나 이상 선택해주세요.'); return }
      update({ ...content, stores: content.stores.some(s => s.id === editing.id) ? content.stores.map(s => s.id === editing.id ? { ...editing, name: editing.name.trim(), menus: [...new Set((editing.menus ?? []).map(menu => menu.trim()).filter(Boolean))] } : s) : [...content.stores, { ...editing, name: editing.name.trim() }] }); setEditing(null)
    }}><div className="store-editor-header"><div><p className="store-eyebrow">GUWOL MORAENAE / STORE DETAILS</p><h2 id="store-editor-title">{content.stores.some(s => s.id === editing.id) ? '점포 상세 편집' : '새 점포 추가'}</h2><p id="store-editor-description">기본 정보와 사진, 연결할 미식루트를 관리하세요.</p></div><button className="store-editor-close" type="button" aria-label="점포 편집창 닫기" onClick={() => setEditing(null)}>✕</button></div>
      <div className="store-editor-body">{storeError && <p className="admin-error" role="alert">{storeError}</p>}<section className="store-editor-basics"><div className="store-form-section-heading"><span>01</span><h3>기본 정보</h3></div><div className="store-form-grid"><label className="store-field-wide">점포명 <span className="store-required">*</span><input autoFocus required placeholder="점포 이름을 입력하세요" maxLength={160} value={editing.name} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, name: e.target.value })} /></label><label className="store-field-wide">대표 상품<textarea placeholder="대표 상품이나 메뉴를 입력하세요" maxLength={4000} value={editing.menu} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, menu: e.target.value })} /></label>{(['price', 'hours'] as const).map(key => <label key={key}>{{ price: '가격대', hours: '영업시간·휴무' }[key]}<textarea placeholder={key === 'price' ? '예: 5,000원 ~ 10,000원' : '예: 10:00–20:00 / 매주 월요일 휴무'} maxLength={4000} value={editing[key]} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, [key]: e.target.value })} /></label>)}<label className="store-field-wide">위치<textarea placeholder="점포 주소 또는 시장 내 위치를 입력하세요" maxLength={4000} value={editing.location ?? ""} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, location: e.target.value })} /></label></div></section>
      <section className="store-menu-editor"><div className="store-form-section-heading"><span>메뉴</span><h3>메뉴 선택 항목</h3></div><label>메뉴 (한 줄에 하나)<textarea placeholder={'예: 국수\n순대\n김밥'} value={(editing.menus ?? []).join('\n')} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, menus: e.target.value.split('\n') })} onBlur={e => setEditing({ ...editing, menus: [...new Set(e.target.value.split('\n').map(menu => menu.trim()).filter(Boolean))] })} /></label><p className="store-menu-help">이 메뉴는 점포 상세에 표시됩니다. 미식루트 필터는 대표 상품을 사용합니다.</p></section><section><div className="store-form-section-heading"><span>02</span><h3>점포 사진</h3></div><PhotoField label="대표 사진" value={editing.image} onChange={image => setEditing(current => current?.id === editing.id ? { ...current, image } : current)} onError={setStoreError} /></section>
      <section><div className="store-form-section-heading"><span>03</span><h3>미식루트 연결</h3><small>여러 개 선택 가능</small></div><div className="store-theme-options" role="group" aria-label="미식루트 테마 선택">{content.routes.map(route => <label key={route.id} className={editing.routeIds.includes(route.id) ? 'selected' : ''}><input type="checkbox" checked={editing.routeIds.includes(route.id)} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, routeIds: e.target.checked ? [...editing.routeIds, route.id] : editing.routeIds.filter(id => id !== route.id) })} /><span><strong>{route.name}</strong><small>{route.isVisible ? route.description : '비공개 테마'}</small></span></label>)}</div>{!content.routes.length && <p>미식루트 테마 관리에서 테마를 먼저 추가해주세요.</p>}</section>
      <section className="store-publish-setting"><div><h3>고객페이지 공개</h3><p>확인한 정보만 공개해주세요. 비공개 점포는 고객에게 보이지 않습니다.</p></div><label className="store-switch"><input aria-label="고객페이지에 공개" type="checkbox" checked={editing.isVisible} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, isVisible: e.target.checked })} /><span aria-hidden="true" /><strong>{editing.isVisible ? '공개' : '비공개'}</strong></label></section></div>
      <div className="store-editor-footer"><p>적용 후 상단에서 저장 또는 공개 반영해주세요.</p><div><button type="button" onClick={() => setEditing(null)}>취소</button><button className="admin-primary" type="submit">{content.stores.some(s => s.id === editing.id) ? '수정 내용 적용' : '점포 추가하기'}</button></div></div>
    </form>}</dialog>
  </div>
}
```

### src/app/(frontend)/admin/preview/Preview.tsx

SHA-256 aab90d20d86332727389cf50df3d139f6d343bbed277afc467c313246c65c231

```tsx
'use client'
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { contentSchema, type Content } from '@/lib/content-schema'
import HomeExperience from '../../HomeExperience'
import FestivalExperience from '../../FestivalExperience'
import MapExperience from '../../MapExperience'

export default function Preview({ initial }: { initial: Content }) {
  const [view, setView] = useState('home')
  const [section, setSection] = useState('site')
  const [routeId, setRouteId] = useState('')
  const [content, setContent] = useState(initial)
  const lastSelection = useRef('')
  useEffect(() => {
    const receive = (event: MessageEvent) => {
      if (event.origin !== location.origin || event.source !== parent || event.data?.type !== 'market-preview') return
      const result = contentSchema.safeParse(event.data.content)
      if (!result.success) return
      setContent(result.data)
      const page = typeof event.data.page === 'string' ? event.data.page : 'site'
      const selectedRoute = typeof event.data.routeId === 'string' ? event.data.routeId : ''
      const selection = page + ':' + selectedRoute
      if (lastSelection.current !== selection) {
        lastSelection.current = selection
        setSection(page)
        setRouteId(selectedRoute)
        setView(['routePage', 'festivalPage', 'mapPage'].includes(page) ? page : 'home')
      }
    }
    // Keep internal navigation inside this draft, instead of loading published pages
    // and losing the message listener when a preview link is clicked.
    const navigate = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      const link = (event.target as Element).closest<HTMLAnchorElement>('a[href]')
      if (!link || link.target === '_blank' || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey || event.shiftKey || event.altKey || event.button !== 0) return
      const url = new URL(link.href, location.href)
      if (url.origin !== location.origin) return
      if (url.pathname === '/') { event.preventDefault(); setView('home'); setSection(url.hash === '#routes' ? 'routesSection' : url.hash === '#festival' ? 'festival' : 'hero'); window.scrollTo(0, 0) }
      else if (url.pathname === '/routes' || /^\/routes\/[^/]+$/.test(url.pathname)) { event.preventDefault(); setRouteId(decodeURIComponent(url.pathname.split('/')[2] || '')); setView('routePage'); window.scrollTo(0, 0) }
      else if (url.pathname === '/festival' || url.pathname === '/map') { event.preventDefault(); setView(url.pathname === '/festival' ? 'festivalPage' : 'mapPage'); window.scrollTo(0, 0) }
      else if (url.pathname.startsWith('/admin')) event.preventDefault()
    }
    addEventListener('message', receive)
    document.addEventListener('click', navigate)
    parent.postMessage({ type: 'market-preview-ready' }, location.origin)
    return () => { removeEventListener('message', receive); document.removeEventListener('click', navigate) }
  }, [])
  useEffect(() => {
    const target = view === 'home' ? ({ hero: '.hero', routesSection: '#routes', festival: '#festival', footer: '#partners' } as Record<string, string>)[section] : ''
    if (target) document.querySelector(target)?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' })
    else window.scrollTo(0, 0)
  }, [view, section])
  if (view === 'festivalPage') return <FestivalExperience site={content.site} />
  if (view === 'mapPage') return <MapExperience site={content.site} />
  const routes = content.routes.filter(route => route.isVisible)
  return <HomeExperience key={view + ':' + routeId} detailMode={view === 'routePage'} initialRouteID={routeId} {...content} routes={routes} stores={content.stores.filter(store => store.isVisible && store.routeIds.some(id => routes.some(route => route.id === id)))} />
}
```

### src/app/(frontend)/styles.css

SHA-256 d2a32c4b3e2571e3368a0d43ed6818a6a7c9fc9f0ee2827819fbed8c1484821a

```css
@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Noto+Sans+KR:wght@400;700;800;900&display=swap');
@import 'tailwindcss';

:root {
  color-scheme: only light;
  --navy: #1d3974;
  --blue: #287eb8;
  --cream: #fff4d6;
  --white: #fffef8;
  --yellow: #f4bf22;
  --orange: #ee4729;
  --green: #2c6e4e;
  --purple: #8d7da3;
  --ink: #142c5e;
  --muted: #66718a;
  color: var(--ink);
  background: var(--navy);
  font-family:
    'Noto Sans KR', Pretendard, 'Apple SD Gothic Neo', 'Malgun Gothic', Arial, sans-serif;
  font-synthesis: none;
}

@layer base {
  html {
    scroll-behavior: smooth;
  }

  body {
    min-width: 320px;
    min-height: 100vh;
    background: var(--navy);
  }

  button,
  a,
  input,
  select {
    outline-offset: 4px;
  }

  a {
    color: inherit;
    text-decoration: none;
  }

  button {
    cursor: pointer;
  }

  ::selection {
    color: var(--white);
    background: var(--orange);
  }
}

.figma-custom-code-fragment {
  display: contents;
}

.figma-bypass-link {
  position: fixed;
  z-index: 2147483647;
  top: 8px;
  left: 8px;
  padding: 8px 12px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  color: #fff;
  background: #111827;
  font:
    600 14px/1.2 system-ui,
    sans-serif;
  transform: translateY(-150%);
}

.figma-bypass-link:focus {
  transform: translateY(0);
}

.site-shell {
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 1800px;
  min-height: 100vh;
  margin: 0 auto;
  overflow: clip;
  background: var(--cream);
}

.topbar {
  position: sticky;
  z-index: 60;
  top: 0;
  display: flex;
  width: 100%;
  height: 78px;
  padding: 0 clamp(20px, 4vw, 64px);
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  color: var(--white);
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--navy) 92%, transparent);
  border-bottom: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--white) 22%, transparent);
  backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
}

.brand {
  display: flex;
  gap: 12px;
  align-items: center;
}

.brand-mark {
  display: grid;
  width: 48px;
  height: 48px;
  border: 2px solid var(--cream);
  border-radius: 9px 18px 9px 18px;
  place-items: center;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--yellow);
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: -0.06em;
  transform: rotate(-4deg);
  transition: transform 180ms ease;
}

.brand:hover .brand-mark {
  transform: rotate(4deg) scale(1.06);
}

.brand-name {
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: -0.04em;
}

.menu-trigger {
  display: flex;
  width: 54px;
  height: 46px;
  padding: 10px 7px;
  gap: 7px;
  align-items: stretch;
  justify-content: center;
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--white) 45%, transparent);
  border-radius: 999px;
  flex-direction: column;
  transition:
    color 180ms ease,
    background 180ms ease,
    transform 180ms ease;
}

.menu-trigger span {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: 2px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: currentColor;
  transition: transform 180ms ease;
}

.menu-trigger span:nth-child(2) {
  width: 70%;
  align-self: flex-end;
}

.menu-trigger:hover {
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--cream);
  transform: rotate(-3deg);
}

.menu-trigger:hover span:nth-child(1) {
  transform: translateX(4px);
}

.menu-trigger:hover span:nth-child(3) {
  transform: translateX(-4px);
}

.menu-overlay {
  position: fixed;
  z-index: 100;
  inset: 0;
  display: grid;
  overflow-y: auto;
  place-items: center;
  visibility: hidden;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--white);
  clip-path: circle(0 at calc(100% - 54px) 38px);
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
}

.menu-overlay:target {
  visibility: visible;
  animation: menu-reveal 360ms cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.8, 0.2, 1) both;
  pointer-events: auto;
}

body:has(.menu-overlay:target) {
  overflow: hidden;
}

.menu-overlay::before {
  position: absolute;
  top: -90px;
  left: -70px;
  width: 250px;
  height: 250px;
  background: var(--yellow);
  clip-path: polygon(
    50% 0,
    62% 28%,
    88% 12%,
    75% 39%,
    100% 50%,
    73% 61%,
    87% 89%,
    61% 73%,
    50% 100%,
    39% 73%,
    12% 89%,
    27% 61%,
    0 50%,
    26% 39%,
    12% 12%,
    39% 28%
  );
  content: '';
  animation: slow-spin 26s linear infinite;
}

.menu-overlay::after {
  position: absolute;
  right: -75px;
  bottom: -30px;
  width: 280px;
  height: 100px;
  border: 4px solid var(--navy);
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: repeating-linear-gradient(105deg, var(--purple) 0 10px, var(--white) 10px 15px);
  content: '';
  transform: rotate(-9deg);
}

@keyframes menu-reveal {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    clip-path: circle(0 at calc(100% - 54px) 38px);
  }

  to {
    opacity: 1;
    clip-path: circle(150% at calc(100% - 54px) 38px);
  }
}

.menu-overlay-head {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 2;
  top: 0;
  right: 0;
  left: 0;
  display: flex;
  height: 78px;
  padding: 0 clamp(20px, 4vw, 64px);
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  border-bottom: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--navy) 20%, transparent);
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: 0.13em;
}

.menu-overlay-head > a {
  position: relative;
  width: 50px;
  height: 50px;
  border: 2px solid var(--navy);
  border-radius: 50%;
  transition:
    color 180ms ease,
    background 180ms ease,
    transform 180ms ease;
}

.menu-overlay-head > a span {
  position: absolute;
  top: 23px;
  left: 12px;
  width: 24px;
  height: 2px;
  background: currentColor;
}

.menu-overlay-head > a span:first-child {
  transform: rotate(45deg);
}

.menu-overlay-head > a span:last-child {
  transform: rotate(-45deg);
}

.menu-overlay-head > a:hover {
  color: var(--white);
  background: var(--navy);
  transform: rotate(90deg);
}

.menu-list {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2;
  display: flex;
  width: min(86%, 920px);
  padding: 110px 0 90px;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.menu-list a {
  display: grid;
  padding: 18px 4px;
  border-bottom: 2px solid var(--navy);
  grid-template-columns: 54px 1fr auto;
  align-items: center;
  transition:
    color 180ms ease,
    padding 180ms ease,
    background 180ms ease;
}

.menu-list a:first-child {
  border-top: 2px solid var(--navy);
}

.menu-list a > span {
  display: grid;
  width: 34px;
  height: 34px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  place-items: center;
  color: var(--white);
  background: var(--navy);
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 900;
}

.menu-list strong {
  font-size: clamp(34px, 4.6vw, 68px);
  font-weight: 900;
  line-height: 1;
  letter-spacing: -0.07em;
}

.menu-list small {
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
}

.menu-list a:hover {
  padding-right: 18px;
  padding-left: 18px;
  color: var(--white);
  background: var(--orange);
}

.menu-list a:hover > span {
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--yellow);
}

.menu-overlay-foot {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 2;
  bottom: 26px;
  left: clamp(20px, 4vw, 64px);
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
}

.hero {
  position: relative;
  min-height: 830px;
  overflow: hidden;
  color: var(--white);
  background: var(--navy);
  isolation: isolate;
}

.hero::before {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: -3;
  inset: 0;
  opacity: 0.26;
  background-image: radial-gradient(circle at 1px 1px, var(--cream) 1px, transparent 0);
  background-size: 24px 24px;
  content: '';
}

.hero::after {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: -2;
  top: 0;
  right: 0;
  width: 48%;
  height: 100%;
  background: linear-gradient(
    145deg,
    transparent 0 15%,
    color-mix(in srgb, var(--blue) 38%, transparent) 15% 58%,
    transparent 58%
  );
  content: '';
}

.hero-shape {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: -1;
  pointer-events: none;
}

.hero-shape-one {
  top: 15%;
  left: -45px;
  width: 150px;
  height: 150px;
  background: var(--orange);
  clip-path: polygon(
    50% 0,
    62% 28%,
    88% 12%,
    75% 39%,
    100% 50%,
    73% 61%,
    87% 89%,
    61% 73%,
    50% 100%,
    39% 73%,
    12% 89%,
    27% 61%,
    0 50%,
    26% 39%,
    12% 12%,
    39% 28%
  );
  animation: slow-spin 24s linear infinite;
}

.hero-shape-two {
  right: 2%;
  bottom: 5%;
  width: 230px;
  height: 82px;
  border: 4px solid var(--cream);
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: repeating-linear-gradient(105deg, var(--purple) 0 9px, var(--navy) 9px 13px);
  transform: rotate(-9deg);
}

@keyframes slow-spin {
  to {
    transform: rotate(360deg);
  }
}

.hero-label {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 5;
  top: 34px;
  left: clamp(24px, 6vw, 100px);
  display: flex;
  gap: 14px;
  align-items: center;
  color: var(--cream);
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.hero-label span {
  padding: 8px 13px;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--cream);
  font-size: 9px;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  clip-path: polygon(4% 0, 100% 5%, 95% 100%, 0 92%);
}

.hero-card {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  width: min(100%, 1560px);
  min-height: 830px;
  margin: 0 auto;
  padding: 106px clamp(26px, 6vw, 100px) 56px;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1.15fr) minmax(380px, 0.72fr);
  grid-template-rows: 1fr auto;
  gap: 36px 5%;
  align-items: center;
}

.hero-copy {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 3;
}

.hero-kicker {
  width: fit-content;
  margin-bottom: 18px;
  padding: 7px 12px;
  border: 1px solid var(--cream);
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.18em;
}

.hero h1 {
  display: flex;
  max-width: 840px;
  flex-direction: column;
  font-weight: 900;
  line-height: 0.82;
  letter-spacing: -0.09em;
}

.hero h1 span {
  display: block;
  width: fit-content;
  font-size: clamp(72px, 8.3vw, 145px);
  transition: transform 240ms ease;
}

.hero h1 span:first-child {
  color: var(--yellow);
  transform: rotate(-2deg);
  text-shadow: 5px 5px 0 var(--blue);
}

.hero h1 span:last-child {
  margin-top: 12px;
  margin-left: 6%;
  color: var(--white);
  transform: rotate(1deg);
  text-shadow: 5px 5px 0 var(--orange);
}

.hero-copy:hover h1 span:first-child {
  transform: rotate(1deg) translateX(10px);
}

.hero-copy:hover h1 span:last-child {
  transform: rotate(-1deg) translateX(-8px);
}

.hero-copy > p:not(.hero-kicker) {
  max-width: 470px;
  padding: 38px 0 26px;
  color: var(--cream);
  font-size: clamp(15px, 1.15vw, 19px);
  font-weight: 700;
  line-height: 1.75;
}

.primary-button {
  display: inline-flex;
  min-height: 62px;
  padding: 10px 12px 10px 24px;
  gap: 20px;
  align-items: center;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--cream);
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 900;
  clip-path: polygon(3% 0, 100% 7%, 96% 100%, 0 91%);
  transition: transform 180ms ease;
}

.primary-button span {
  display: grid;
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  place-items: center;
  color: var(--white);
  background: var(--orange);
  font-size: 22px;
}

.primary-button:hover {
  transform: rotate(-2deg) scale(1.04);
}

.hero-figure {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 3;
  width: min(100%, 560px);
  justify-self: end;
}

.hero-image {
  position: relative;
  width: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
  aspect-ratio: 1;
  border: 11px solid var(--cream);
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--cream);
  box-shadow: 14px 18px 0 var(--blue);
  transition:
    border-radius 260ms ease,
    transform 260ms ease;
}

.hero-image:hover {
  border-radius: 44% 56% 48% 52% / 52% 45% 55% 48%;
  transform: rotate(2deg) scale(1.015);
}

.hero-image img {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  object-fit: cover;
  object-position: center 61%;
  transform: scale(1.23);
}

.hero-figure figcaption {
  position: absolute;
  right: -3%;
  bottom: 6%;
  display: flex;
  width: 78%;
  padding: 16px 20px;
  gap: 12px;
  align-items: center;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--cream);
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 900;
  clip-path: polygon(4% 0, 100% 8%, 94% 100%, 0 88%);
  transform: rotate(2deg);
}

.hero-figure figcaption span {
  padding: 5px 9px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--yellow);
  font-size: 9px;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
}

.hero-foot {
  display: flex;
  z-index: 2;
  grid-column: 1 / -1;
  padding-top: 22px;
  border-top: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--cream) 32%, transparent);
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  color: var(--cream);
  font-size: 12px;
}

.scroll-cue {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 8;
  right: 26px;
  bottom: 24px;
  display: flex;
  gap: 10px;
  align-items: center;
  color: var(--cream);
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: 0.13em;
  writing-mode: vertical-rl;
}

.scroll-cue b {
  display: grid;
  width: 32px;
  height: 32px;
  border: 1px solid currentColor;
  border-radius: 50%;
  place-items: center;
  font-size: 14px;
  writing-mode: horizontal-tb;
}

.loop-line {
  overflow: hidden;
  padding: 16px 0 18px;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--yellow);
  border-top: 4px solid var(--cream);
  border-bottom: 4px solid var(--cream);
  font-size: clamp(22px, 2.6vw, 42px);
  font-weight: 900;
  line-height: 1;
  letter-spacing: -0.04em;
  white-space: nowrap;
  transform: rotate(-0.5deg) scale(1.01);
}

.loop-line div {
  width: max-content;
  animation: loop-type 24s linear infinite;
}

@keyframes loop-type {
  to {
    transform: translateX(-50%);
  }
}

.content-wrap {
  background: var(--navy);
}

.routes-section {
  position: relative;
  padding: clamp(90px, 10vw, 160px) 6% clamp(90px, 9vw, 140px);
  overflow: hidden;
  background: var(--cream);
}

.routes-section::after {
  position: absolute;
  pointer-events: none;
  content: '';
}

.routes-section::after {
  right: -36px;
  bottom: 70px;
  width: 100px;
  height: 100px;
  background: var(--orange);
  clip-path: polygon(
    50% 0,
    62% 28%,
    88% 12%,
    75% 39%,
    100% 50%,
    73% 61%,
    87% 89%,
    61% 73%,
    50% 100%,
    39% 73%,
    12% 89%,
    27% 61%,
    0 50%,
    26% 39%,
    12% 12%,
    39% 28%
  );
}

.routes-heading {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2;
  display: grid;
  max-width: 1200px;
  margin: 0 auto;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1.4fr) minmax(260px, 0.6fr);
  gap: 36px;
  align-items: end;
}

.section-label {
  display: block;
  width: fit-content;
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  padding: 7px 12px;
  border: 1px solid currentColor;
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
}

.routes-heading .section-label {
  grid-column: 1 / -1;
  margin-bottom: -12px;
}

.routes-heading h2 {
  max-width: 780px;
  font-size: clamp(54px, 6.6vw, 105px);
  font-weight: 900;
  line-height: 0.94;
  letter-spacing: -0.08em;
  text-shadow: 4px 4px 0 var(--yellow);
}

.routes-heading p {
  max-width: 400px;
  padding: 18px 20px;
  color: var(--cream);
  background: var(--navy);
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 700;
  line-height: 1.75;
  clip-path: polygon(3% 0, 100% 6%, 96% 100%, 0 91%);
  transform: rotate(1deg);
}

.tabs {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 3;
  display: flex;
  max-width: 1200px;
  margin: 58px auto 0;
  gap: 8px;
  overflow-x: auto;
  scrollbar-width: none;
}

.tabs::-webkit-scrollbar {
  display: none;
}

.tabs button {
  min-width: max-content;
  min-height: 50px;
  padding: 10px 19px;
  border: 2px solid var(--navy);
  border-radius: 8px 17px 8px 17px;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--white);
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 900;
  transition:
    color 160ms ease,
    background 160ms ease,
    transform 160ms ease;
}

.tabs button:nth-child(even) {
  transform: rotate(1deg);
}

.tabs button:hover {
  transform: translateY(-4px) rotate(-1deg);
}

.tabs button.active {
  color: var(--white);
  background: var(--navy);
}

.route-panel {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2;
  max-width: 1200px;
  margin: 20px auto 0;
  padding: clamp(26px, 4vw, 52px);
  border: 3px solid var(--navy);
  border-radius: 26px;
  background: var(--white);
  box-shadow: 10px 10px 0 var(--blue);
}

.panel-head {
  display: flex;
  gap: 30px;
  align-items: flex-end;
  justify-content: space-between;
}

.panel-head h3 {
  font-size: clamp(32px, 3vw, 48px);
  font-weight: 900;
  line-height: 1.05;
  letter-spacing: -0.06em;
}

.panel-head p {
  max-width: 480px;
  padding-top: 10px;
  font-size: 14px;
  line-height: 1.7;
}

.filters {
  display: flex;
  gap: 8px;
}

.filters input,
.filters select {
  height: 50px;
  border: 2px solid var(--navy);
  border-radius: 8px;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--cream);
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.filters input {
  width: 220px;
  padding: 0 16px;
}

.filters select {
  width: 145px;
  padding: 0 36px 0 14px;
  appearance: none;
}

.select-wrap {
  position: relative;
}

.select-wrap img {
  position: absolute;
  top: 22px;
  right: 15px;
  pointer-events: none;
}

.result-meta {
  display: flex;
  margin-top: 28px;
  padding: 14px 0;
  border-top: 2px dashed color-mix(in srgb, var(--navy) 35%, transparent);
  border-bottom: 2px dashed color-mix(in srgb, var(--navy) 35%, transparent);
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  font-size: 13px;
}

.result-meta strong {
  display: inline-grid;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  margin-left: 5px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  place-items: center;
  color: var(--white);
  background: var(--orange);
}

.result-meta > span:last-child {
  color: var(--muted);
}

.store-grid {
  display: grid;
  padding-top: 24px;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 18px;
}

.store-card {
  min-height: 330px;
  padding: 22px;
  border: 2px solid var(--navy);
  border-radius: 16px;
  background: var(--cream);
  transition:
    transform 180ms ease,
    box-shadow 180ms ease;
}

.store-card:nth-child(even) {
  background: var(--muted-background, #ece6db);
  transform: rotate(0.5deg);
}

.store-card:hover {
  box-shadow: 8px 8px 0 var(--yellow);
  transform: translate(-3px, -3px) rotate(-0.5deg);
}

.store-photo {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: auto;
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  aspect-ratio: 16 / 10;
  border: 2px solid var(--navy);
  border-radius: 10px;
  object-fit: cover;
}

.market-name {
  display: inline-block;
  margin-bottom: 13px;
  padding: 5px 10px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  color: var(--white);
  background: var(--green);
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.store-card h3 {
  padding-bottom: 12px;
  font-size: 28px;
  font-weight: 900;
  line-height: 1.15;
  letter-spacing: -0.06em;
}

.menu-name {
  padding-bottom: 18px;
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.store-card dl {
  display: flex;
  padding: 17px 0 20px;
  border-top: 2px dashed color-mix(in srgb, var(--navy) 24%, transparent);
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 8px;
  font-size: 13px;
}

.store-card dl div {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 42px 1fr;
  gap: 12px;
}

.store-card dt {
  color: var(--muted);
}

.store-card button {
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 46px;
  border: 2px solid var(--navy);
  border-radius: 8px;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--white);
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 900;
  transition:
    color 160ms ease,
    background 160ms ease;
}

.store-card button:hover {
  color: var(--white);
  background: var(--navy);
}

.empty-result {
  grid-column: 1 / -1;
  display: grid;
  min-height: 250px;
  padding: 28px;
  border: 2px dashed var(--navy);
  border-radius: 14px;
  place-items: center;
  background: var(--cream);
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 800;
  text-align: center;
}

.routes-empty {
  max-width: 1200px;
  margin: 42px auto 0;
}

.image-empty {
  display: grid;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  place-items: center;
  padding: 24px;
  color: var(--cream);
  font-size: 13px;
  text-align: center;
}

.festival {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  min-height: 800px;
  padding: clamp(90px, 10vw, 155px) 8%;
  overflow: hidden;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(310px, 0.68fr);
  gap: 8%;
  align-items: center;
  color: var(--white);
  background: var(--navy);
}

.festival::before {
  position: absolute;
  top: 7%;
  left: 4%;
  width: 230px;
  height: 75px;
  border: 3px solid var(--cream);
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: repeating-linear-gradient(105deg, var(--purple) 0 9px, var(--navy) 9px 13px);
  content: '';
  transform: rotate(-8deg);
}

.festival::after {
  position: absolute;
  right: -35px;
  bottom: -35px;
  width: 170px;
  height: 170px;
  background: var(--orange);
  clip-path: polygon(
    50% 0,
    62% 28%,
    88% 12%,
    75% 39%,
    100% 50%,
    73% 61%,
    87% 89%,
    61% 73%,
    50% 100%,
    39% 73%,
    12% 89%,
    27% 61%,
    0 50%,
    26% 39%,
    12% 12%,
    39% 28%
  );
  content: '';
}

.festival-copy {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2;
  max-width: 680px;
}

.festival-copy .section-label {
  margin-bottom: 34px;
  color: var(--cream);
}

.festival-copy h2 {
  max-width: 700px;
  font-size: clamp(58px, 7vw, 112px);
  font-weight: 900;
  line-height: 0.88;
  letter-spacing: -0.08em;
  text-shadow: 5px 5px 0 var(--blue);
}

.festival-copy > strong {
  display: block;
  width: fit-content;
  margin-top: 30px;
  padding: 8px 14px;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--cream);
  font-size: clamp(18px, 2vw, 30px);
  font-weight: 900;
  clip-path: polygon(3% 0, 100% 7%, 96% 100%, 0 91%);
  transform: rotate(-1deg);
}

.festival-copy > p {
  max-width: 550px;
  padding-top: 24px;
  color: var(--cream);
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 700;
  line-height: 1.8;
}

.festival-date {
  display: flex;
  width: fit-content;
  margin-top: 30px;
  padding: 18px 22px;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 7px;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--yellow);
  clip-path: polygon(2% 0, 100% 5%, 97% 100%, 0 94%);
}

.festival-date b {
  font-size: clamp(42px, 5.4vw, 80px);
  line-height: 1;
  letter-spacing: -0.06em;
}

.festival-date span {
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.festival-copy small {
  display: block;
  padding: 19px 0;
  color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--cream) 70%, transparent);
  font-size: 12px;
}

.festival-copy > a {
  display: inline-flex;
  min-height: 52px;
  padding: 0 22px;
  align-items: center;
  border: 2px solid var(--cream);
  border-radius: 999px;
  color: var(--cream);
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 900;
  transition:
    color 160ms ease,
    background 160ms ease;
}

.festival-copy > a:hover {
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--cream);
}

.poster-link {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 3;
  width: min(100%, 445px);
  justify-self: center;
  transform: rotate(2deg);
  transition: transform 180ms ease;
}

.poster-link::before {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: -1;
  inset: -18px 20px 22px -18px;
  border: 3px solid var(--cream);
  content: '';
  transform: rotate(-4deg);
}

.poster-link:hover {
  transform: rotate(-1deg) scale(1.02);
}

.poster-link img {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: auto;
  border: 8px solid var(--cream);
  box-shadow: 14px 16px 0 var(--blue);
}

.notice {
  padding: 18px 6%;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--yellow);
  border-top: 4px solid var(--cream);
  border-bottom: 4px solid var(--cream);
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 800;
  text-align: center;
}

.sponsor-loop {
  position: relative;
  height: 138px;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: var(--white);
  border-top: 3px solid var(--navy);
  border-bottom: 3px solid var(--navy);
}

.sponsor-caption {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 3;
  top: 12px;
  left: clamp(18px, 4vw, 62px);
  padding: 6px 11px;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--yellow);
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  clip-path: polygon(3% 0, 100% 7%, 96% 100%, 0 91%);
  transform: rotate(-1deg);
}

.sponsor-track {
  display: flex;
  width: max-content;
  padding: 43px 0 9px;
  gap: 44px;
  animation: sponsor-flow 22s linear infinite;
}

.sponsor-loop:hover .sponsor-track {
  animation-play-state: paused;
}

.sponsor-set {
  display: flex;
  width: 900px;
  height: 82px;
  flex: 0 0 900px;
  gap: 16px;
  align-items: center;
}

.sponsor-poster {
  position: relative;
  width: 900px;
  height: 82px;
  overflow: hidden;
  flex: 0 0 900px;
  background: var(--white);
}

.sponsor-poster img {
  position: absolute;
  right: 0;
  bottom: 0;
  left: 0;
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  max-width: none;
  height: auto;
}

.sponsor-name {
  display: grid;
  min-width: max-content;
  height: 50px;
  padding: 0 18px;
  border: 2px solid var(--navy);
  border-radius: 999px;
  place-items: center;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--white);
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 900;
}

@keyframes sponsor-flow {
  to {
    transform: translateX(calc(-50% - 22px));
  }
}

footer {
  display: flex;
  min-height: 460px;
  padding: clamp(75px, 9vw, 130px) 8% 60px;
  gap: 80px;
  align-items: flex-end;
  justify-content: space-between;
  color: var(--navy);
  background: var(--cream);
}

footer h2 {
  font-size: clamp(54px, 7vw, 110px);
  font-weight: 900;
  line-height: 0.9;
  letter-spacing: -0.08em;
  text-shadow: 4px 4px 0 var(--yellow);
}

.footer-inner > div:first-child > p {
  padding-top: 24px;
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.footer-info {
  width: min(100%, 430px);
  padding: 28px;
  border: 2px solid var(--navy);
  border-radius: 14px;
  background: var(--white);
  box-shadow: 8px 8px 0 var(--blue);
  font-size: 13px;
  line-height: 1.7;
  transform: rotate(1deg);
  transition: transform 180ms ease;
}

.footer-info:hover {
  transform: rotate(0deg) translateY(-4px);
}

.footer-card-label {
  display: block;
  width: fit-content;
  margin-bottom: 23px;
  padding: 5px 9px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  color: var(--white);
  background: var(--navy);
  font-size: 8px;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: 0.11em;
}

.footer-info-block {
  display: grid;
  padding: 14px 0;
  border-top: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--navy) 22%, transparent);
  grid-template-columns: 48px 1fr;
  gap: 12px;
}

.footer-info-block strong,
.footer-social > strong {
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 900;
}

.footer-info-block p {
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 12px;
}

.footer-social {
  display: flex;
  min-height: 58px;
  padding: 12px 0;
  gap: 10px;
  align-items: center;
  border-top: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--navy) 22%, transparent);
}

.footer-social > strong {
  width: 38px;
}

.social-icon {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  width: 34px;
  height: 34px;
  border: 2px solid var(--navy);
  border-radius: 50%;
  place-items: center;
  color: var(--navy);
  font-size: 21px;
  font-weight: 900;
  line-height: 1;
  transition:
    color 160ms ease,
    background 160ms ease,
    transform 160ms ease;
}

.social-icon:hover {
  color: var(--white);
  background: var(--navy);
  transform: rotate(-8deg) scale(1.06);
}

.social-instagram::before {
  width: 14px;
  height: 14px;
  border: 2px solid currentColor;
  border-radius: 4px;
  content: '';
}

.social-instagram::after {
  position: absolute;
  top: 9px;
  right: 9px;
  width: 3px;
  height: 3px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: currentColor;
  content: '';
}

.footer-info a {
  display: inline-flex;
  min-height: 44px;
  padding: 0 17px;
  align-items: center;
  border-radius: 999px;
  color: var(--white);
  background: var(--navy);
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 900;
}

.footer-copyright {
  padding-top: 18px;
  border-top: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--navy) 22%, transparent);
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 9px;
  line-height: 1.6;
}

.sr-only {
  position: absolute;
  width: 1px;
  height: 1px;
  padding: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  clip: rect(0, 0, 0, 0);
  white-space: nowrap;
  border: 0;
}

@media (max-width: 1050px) {
  .hero-card {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(330px, 0.72fr);
  }

  .hero h1 span {
    font-size: clamp(64px, 8vw, 100px);
  }

  .panel-head {
    align-items: flex-start;
    flex-direction: column;
  }

  .filters {
    width: 100%;
  }

  .filters label:first-child,
  .filters input {
    width: 100%;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 760px) {
  .topbar {
    height: 64px;
    padding: 0 14px;
  }

  .brand-mark {
    width: 42px;
    height: 42px;
  }

  .brand-name {
    max-width: 110px;
    overflow: hidden;
    font-size: 11px;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }

  .menu-trigger {
    width: 48px;
    height: 42px;
  }

  .menu-overlay-head {
    height: 64px;
    padding: 0 14px;
  }

  .menu-overlay-head > span {
    font-size: 8px;
  }

  .menu-overlay-head > a {
    width: 42px;
    height: 42px;
  }

  .menu-overlay-head > a span {
    top: 19px;
    left: 9px;
  }

  .menu-list {
    width: calc(100% - 36px);
    padding: 90px 0 78px;
  }

  .menu-list a {
    padding: 15px 2px;
    grid-template-columns: 42px 1fr auto;
  }

  .menu-list a > span {
    width: 29px;
    height: 29px;
  }

  .menu-list strong {
    font-size: 36px;
  }

  .menu-list small {
    font-size: 7px;
  }

  .menu-overlay-foot {
    bottom: 16px;
    left: 18px;
    font-size: 8px;
  }

  .hero {
    min-height: 920px;
  }

  .hero::after {
    width: 100%;
  }

  .hero-shape-one {
    top: 27%;
    left: -52px;
    width: 110px;
    height: 110px;
  }

  .hero-shape-two {
    right: -45px;
    bottom: 17%;
    width: 160px;
    height: 58px;
  }

  .hero-label {
    top: 26px;
    left: 18px;
    gap: 8px;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: flex-start;
    font-size: 10px;
  }

  .hero-card {
    display: flex;
    min-height: 920px;
    padding: 112px 18px 44px;
    flex-direction: column;
  }

  .hero-kicker {
    margin-bottom: 14px;
    font-size: 8px;
  }

  .hero h1 span {
    font-size: clamp(56px, 17vw, 78px);
  }

  .hero h1 span:last-child {
    margin-top: 7px;
    margin-left: 3%;
  }

  .hero-copy > p:not(.hero-kicker) {
    max-width: 320px;
    padding: 24px 0 18px;
    font-size: 13px;
  }

  .primary-button {
    min-height: 53px;
    padding-left: 18px;
    font-size: 11px;
  }

  .primary-button span {
    width: 34px;
    height: 34px;
  }

  .hero-figure {
    width: 70%;
    margin-top: 24px;
    align-self: flex-end;
  }

  .hero-image {
    border-width: 7px;
    box-shadow: 9px 11px 0 var(--blue);
  }

  .hero-figure figcaption {
    right: -17px;
    bottom: 4%;
    width: 113%;
    padding: 12px 14px;
    font-size: 10px;
  }

  .hero-foot {
    margin-top: auto;
    gap: 7px;
    align-items: flex-start;
    flex-direction: column;
    font-size: 9px;
  }

  .scroll-cue {
    display: none;
  }

  .loop-line {
    padding: 12px 0 14px;
    font-size: 22px;
  }

  .routes-section {
    padding: 82px 16px 90px;
  }

  .routes-heading {
    display: block;
  }

  .routes-heading .section-label {
    margin-bottom: 19px;
  }

  .routes-heading h2 {
    max-width: 340px;
    font-size: 52px;
  }

  .routes-heading p {
    max-width: 320px;
    margin-top: 25px;
    font-size: 13px;
  }

  .tabs {
    margin-top: 38px;
    margin-right: -16px;
  }

  .tabs button {
    min-height: 44px;
    padding: 8px 14px;
    font-size: 11px;
  }

  .route-panel {
    margin: 14px 5px 0 0;
    padding: 22px 16px;
    box-shadow: 6px 6px 0 var(--blue);
  }

  .panel-head {
    gap: 20px;
  }

  .panel-head h3 {
    font-size: 32px;
  }

  .filters {
    flex-direction: column;
  }

  .filters label,
  .filters input,
  .filters select {
    width: 100%;
  }

  .result-meta {
    gap: 7px;
    align-items: flex-start;
    flex-direction: column;
  }

  .store-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .empty-result {
    min-height: 215px;
  }

  .festival {
    min-height: 0;
    padding: 90px 18px 105px;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 65px;
  }

  .festival::before {
    top: 25px;
    left: auto;
    right: -65px;
    width: 190px;
    height: 62px;
  }

  .festival-copy h2 {
    font-size: 56px;
  }

  .festival-copy > p {
    font-size: 13px;
  }

  .festival-date b {
    font-size: 45px;
  }

  .poster-link {
    width: 78%;
    justify-self: end;
  }

  .poster-link img {
    border-width: 6px;
    box-shadow: 9px 11px 0 var(--blue);
  }

  .notice {
    padding: 17px 18px;
    text-align: left;
  }

  .sponsor-loop {
    height: 116px;
  }

  .sponsor-caption {
    top: 8px;
    left: 14px;
  }

  .sponsor-track {
    padding-top: 34px;
  }

  .sponsor-set,
  .sponsor-poster {
    width: 720px;
    height: 76px;
    flex-basis: 720px;
  }

  footer {
    min-height: 580px;
    padding: 78px 18px 30px;
    gap: 50px;
    align-items: flex-start;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: space-between;
  }

  footer h2 {
    font-size: 56px;
  }

  .footer-info {
    width: calc(100% - 8px);
    padding: 23px 20px;
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  html {
    scroll-behavior: auto;
  }

  .loop-line div,
  .sponsor-track,
  .hero-shape-one {
    animation: none;
  }

  *,
  *::before,
  *::after {
    transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
  }
}

/* Local CMS: live design controls and individual institution logos. */
.site-shell { font-size: var(--content-font-size, 16px); }
.hero-copy > p, .routes-heading > p, .panel-head > div > p, .festival-copy > p, .footer-inner > div:first-child > p { font-size: var(--content-font-size, 16px); }
.footer-info { background: var(--muted-background, var(--white)); }
.sponsor-loop { height: 152px; }
.sponsor-track { gap: 0; padding: 44px 0 12px; animation: institution-flow var(--logo-seconds, 28s) linear infinite; }
.sponsor-set { width: auto; height: 90px; flex: 0 0 auto; gap: 0; }
.sponsor-logo { display: flex; width: 225px; height: 82px; align-items: center; justify-content: center; padding: 12px 24px; }
.sponsor-logo img { width: 100%; height: 58px; object-fit: contain; }
.footer-admin-button { display: inline-flex; min-height: 44px; align-items: center; margin-top: 18px; padding: 10px 18px; border: 2px solid var(--navy); background: var(--navy); color: var(--white); font-weight: 800; }
.footer-admin-button:hover { background: var(--blue); }
.selected-route-label { display: block; margin-bottom: 8px; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 800; }
.store-details { margin-top: 18px; border-top: 1px solid currentColor; padding-top: 12px; }
.store-details summary { cursor: pointer; font-weight: 800; }
.store-details p { margin-top: 12px; font-size: 13px; }
.empty-result p { margin-top: 12px; }
.empty-result button { margin-top: 16px; padding: 12px 20px; border: 2px solid currentColor; }
@keyframes institution-flow { to { transform: translateX(-50%); } }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { .sponsor-track { animation: none; } .sponsor-loop { overflow-x: auto; } .sponsor-set[aria-hidden=true] { display: none; } }
```

### src/app/(frontend)/responsive.css

SHA-256 ded397a0ae782c6cd757f1536be9cfd3fe77706e4f05b820d1f8514fb42f2929

```css
/* Viewports: Mobile 320–767px / Tablet 768–1199px / Desktop 1200px+. */
.site-shell{--page-gutter:clamp(24px,3vw,56px);width:100%;max-width:none;min-width:320px;margin:0;overflow:clip}
.topbar{height:auto;min-height:96px;padding:0;background:var(--white);color:var(--navy);border-bottom:1px solid color-mix(in srgb,var(--navy) 16%,transparent);backdrop-filter:blur(18px)}
.topbar-inner,.hero-inner,.routes-inner,.festival-inner,.footer-inner,.sponsor-inner{width:100%;max-width:1280px;margin-inline:auto;padding-inline:var(--page-gutter);min-width:0;box-sizing:border-box}
.topbar-inner{min-height:96px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:24px}
.header-logos{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:20px;min-width:0;width:fit-content;flex-shrink:1}
.header-logos img{display:block;height:52px;width:auto;object-fit:contain;min-width:0}
.header-logos .market-logo{aspect-ratio:487.26/180;max-width:200px}.header-logos .festival-logo{aspect-ratio:736.06/245.73;max-width:220px}
.header-logo-cross{font-size:24px;font-weight:400;opacity:.4;flex-shrink:0}
.site-shell .menu-trigger{flex-shrink:0;width:48px;height:48px;color:var(--navy);border-color:color-mix(in srgb,var(--navy) 28%,transparent);background:transparent}
.site-shell .menu-trigger span{background:var(--navy)}
.hero-inner{position:relative;padding:0}.hero{min-height:0}.hero-label{left:var(--page-gutter)}
.hero-card{width:100%;max-width:none;padding:112px var(--page-gutter) 56px;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.2fr) minmax(0,.8fr);min-height:780px;gap:36px clamp(24px,4vw,72px)}
.hero-copy,.hero-figure,.festival-copy,.poster-link{min-width:0}.hero h1{max-width:100%}.hero h1 span{max-width:100%;font-size:clamp(64px,6.7vw,126px);overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.hero-figure{width:min(100%,560px);justify-self:end}.hero-foot{grid-column:1/-1}
.content-wrap{width:100%;max-width:none;margin:0}.routes-section{padding:clamp(72px,7vw,128px) 0;overflow:hidden}
.routes-heading,.tabs,.route-panel,.routes-empty{max-width:none;width:100%;box-sizing:border-box}.routes-heading{gap:24px}.routes-heading h2{font-size:clamp(44px,5vw,82px);max-width:800px}.tabs{margin-inline:0}.route-panel{margin-inline:0}
.panel-head{gap:24px}.panel-head>div:first-child{min-width:0;flex:1}.filters{flex-shrink:0}.filters input{width:clamp(220px,22vw,340px)}
.store-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr))}.store-card{min-width:0}.store-card h3,.menu-name,.store-card dd{overflow-wrap:anywhere}.store-photo{aspect-ratio:4/3;object-fit:cover}
.festival{display:block;min-height:0;padding:clamp(80px,7vw,130px) 0}.festival-inner{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.2fr) minmax(0,.8fr);align-items:center;gap:clamp(32px,6vw,100px)}
.festival-copy{max-width:760px}.festival-copy h2{font-size:clamp(48px,5vw,84px)}.poster-link{width:min(100%,445px);justify-self:end}.festival-date b{font-size:clamp(44px,5vw,76px)}
.notice{padding-inline:max(var(--page-gutter),calc((100% - 1280px)/2 + var(--page-gutter)));font-size:13px;line-height:1.8}
.sponsor-inner{position:relative;height:100%;overflow:hidden;padding:0}.sponsor-caption{left:var(--page-gutter)}
.site-shell footer{display:block;min-height:0;padding:clamp(72px,8vw,128px) 0 60px}.footer-inner{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.2fr) minmax(0,.8fr);align-items:end;gap:clamp(32px,5vw,90px)}
.footer-inner>div:first-child{min-width:0}.footer-inner>div:first-child>p{padding-top:24px;font-size:var(--content-font-size,16px);font-weight:800}.site-shell footer h2{font-size:clamp(48px,5.6vw,100px);line-height:1.1;overflow-wrap:anywhere}.footer-info{justify-self:end;width:min(100%,460px);min-width:0}.footer-copyright{overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.site-shell section[id],.site-shell main[id]{scroll-margin-top:100px}
@media(min-width:1200px){.site-shell{--page-gutter:clamp(32px,3vw,56px)}.hero-copy{align-self:center}.routes-heading{grid-template-columns:1.2fr .8fr}.panel-head{flex-direction:row;align-items:center}.filters{width:auto}.scroll-cue{right:max(24px,calc((100vw - 1800px)/2 + 24px))}}
@media(min-width:768px) and (max-width:1199px){.site-shell{--page-gutter:32px}.topbar-inner{min-height:88px}.header-logos img{height:46px}.hero-card{min-height:700px;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.1fr) minmax(0,.9fr);padding-top:114px}.hero h1 span{font-size:clamp(58px,7vw,84px)}.hero-figure{width:100%}.hero-foot{font-size:11px}.routes-heading{display:block}.routes-heading p{margin-top:24px;max-width:600px}.tabs{flex-wrap:wrap;overflow:visible;gap:10px}.tabs button{min-width:0;flex:1 1 auto}.panel-head{flex-direction:column;align-items:stretch}.filters,.filters label,.filters input{width:100%}.store-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}.festival-inner{gap:40px;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.1fr) minmax(0,.9fr)}.festival-copy h2{font-size:clamp(44px,6vw,64px)}.festival-date{max-width:100%}.festival-date b{font-size:clamp(36px,4.7vw,56px)}.footer-inner{gap:36px}.footer-info{padding:24px}.site-shell footer h2{font-size:clamp(42px,5.8vw,66px)}}
@media(min-width:320px) and (max-width:767px){.site-shell{--page-gutter:20px}.topbar-inner{min-height:76px;gap:12px}.header-logos{gap:10px;max-width:calc(100% - 52px)}.header-logos img{height:auto;max-height:36px;min-height:0}.header-logos .market-logo{width:clamp(78px,25vw,110px);max-width:40%}.header-logos .festival-logo{width:clamp(88px,28vw,120px);max-width:44%}.header-logo-cross{font-size:17px}.site-shell .menu-trigger{width:40px;height:40px;padding:10px}.hero,.hero-card{min-height:0}.hero-label{position:static;flex-direction:row;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px;padding:28px var(--page-gutter) 0;font-size:10px}.hero-label span{font-size:8px;padding:7px 10px}.hero-card{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr);padding:34px var(--page-gutter)
```
