```css
/* Theme directory shares the store manager's full-width treatment. */
.theme-list-heading{display:grid;grid-template-columns:48px minmax(0,1fr) 100px 90px 90px;gap:20px;padding:16px 24px;background:#f8f9fc;border-bottom:1px solid #e8ecf2;font-size:11px;color:#8190a6}.theme-manager-list{list-style:none;padding:0;margin:0}.theme-manager-list>li{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) 90px;align-items:center;gap:20px;padding-right:24px}.theme-manager-list>li+li{border-top:1px solid #edf0f5}.admin-workspace .theme-edit-row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:48px minmax(0,1fr) 100px 90px;align-items:center;gap:20px;width:100%;padding:24px;background:white;text-align:left}.admin-workspace .theme-edit-row:hover{background:#f6f8fd}.theme-order{font-size:13px;color:#8c9db6;font-weight:800}.theme-name{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;min-width:0}.theme-name strong{font-size:16px;font-weight:800;overflow-wrap:anywhere}.theme-name>span{font-size:12px;color:#8b98ab;overflow-wrap:anywhere}.theme-store-count{font-weight:800;font-size:15px}.theme-store-count small{font-weight:400;font-size:10px;margin-left:5px;color:#8b98ab}.theme-order-actions{display:flex;gap:6px}.admin-workspace .theme-order-actions button{padding:0;min-height:36px;width:36px;border-radius:8px;background:#f0f3f8}.theme-editor-dialog .store-publish-setting{margin-top:28px;margin-bottom:8px}
@media(max-width:767px){.theme-list-heading{display:none}.theme-manager-list>li{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) 34px;padding-right:12px;gap:6px}.admin-workspace .theme-edit-row{grid-template-columns:28px minmax(0,1fr) 58px;padding:20px 14px;gap:9px}.theme-name strong{font-size:13px}.theme-name>span{font-size:11px}.theme-edit-row .theme-store-count{grid-column:2;font-size:11px;grid-row:2}.theme-edit-row .store-visibility{grid-column:3;grid-row:1;font-size:10px}.theme-order-actions{flex-direction:column;gap:4px}.admin-workspace .theme-order-actions button{width:30px;min-height:30px}.theme-manager .store-manager-heading h1{font-size:23px}.theme-manager .store-manager-heading>div>p:last-child{max-width:220px}.theme-editor-dialog{width:calc(100vw - 20px)}}
.hero-image .image-empty{color:var(--navy);font-weight:700}.festival-copy h2,.routes-heading h2{word-break:keep-all;overflow-wrap:break-word}

/* Comfortable reading width with full-bleed section backgrounds. */
.site-shell .scroll-cue{left:50%;right:auto;bottom:18px;transform:translateX(-50%);display:flex;flex-direction:column;writing-mode:horizontal-tb;gap:7px;line-height:1.2}
.hero-card{padding-bottom:100px}
@media(min-width:1200px){.hero-card{min-height:740px;gap:32px 48px}.hero h1 span{font-size:clamp(66px,5.5vw,88px)}.hero-figure{width:min(100%,420px)}.routes-heading h2{font-size:clamp(48px,4.5vw,68px)}.festival-copy h2{font-size:clamp(48px,4.5vw,68px)}.site-shell footer h2{font-size:clamp(48px,4.5vw,72px)}}
@media(min-width:320px) and (max-width:767px){.hero-card{padding-bottom:105px}.site-shell .scroll-cue{bottom:20px;font-size:8px;gap:6px}.scroll-cue b{width:28px;height:28px;font-size:12px}}
.admin-columns.stores-layout{max-width:1280px}.admin-columns:not(.stores-layout){max-width:1600px}

/* Interaction and visibility refinements. */
.site-shell{overflow-x:clip;overflow-y:visible}.content-wrap{overflow:visible}.site-shell footer{padding-bottom:max(72px,env(safe-area-inset-bottom))}.footer-inner{overflow:visible}.footer-info{transform:none}.footer-info:hover{transform:none}
.site-shell .scroll-cue b{border:0;border-radius:0;background:none;width:auto;height:auto;font-size:26px;line-height:1;animation:scroll-invitation 1.5s ease-in-out infinite}
@keyframes scroll-invitation{0%,100%{transform:translateY(0);opacity:.6}50%{transform:translateY(8px);opacity:1}}
.loop-line{transform:rotate(-1.2deg) scaleX(1.06);transform-origin:50% 50%;position:relative;z-index:4;border:0;margin-top:-12px;margin-bottom:-12px;padding-block:20px}
.tabs{overflow:visible;padding-top:10px;padding-bottom:8px;flex-wrap:wrap}.tabs button:hover,.tabs button:nth-child(even):hover{transform:translateY(-5px) rotate(-1deg)}.routes-section{overflow:visible}.routes-inner{position:relative}.menu-filter{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:stretch;gap:8px}.menu-filter label{font-size:12px;font-weight:800}.menu-filter select{width:clamp(220px,22vw,320px);max-width:100%;min-height:48px;padding:12px 40px 12px 14px;border:2px solid var(--navy);border-radius:9px;background:var(--cream);font-size:14px;color:var(--navy);text-overflow:ellipsis}
.sponsor-loop{height:48px;min-height:48px;padding:0;border:0;overflow:hidden;background:var(--white)}.sponsor-inner{max-width:none;width:100%;height:48px;padding:0;overflow:hidden}.sponsor-track{height:48px;width:max-content;padding:0;gap:0;animation:institution-flow var(--logo-seconds,28s) linear infinite}.sponsor-set{width:max-content;flex:0 0 auto;height:48px;gap:0;margin:0;padding:0}.sponsor-logo{width:auto;height:48px;flex:0 0 auto;padding:0 24px;box-sizing:content-box}.sponsor-logo img{display:block;width:auto;max-width:none;height:48px;object-fit:contain}
.site-shell label,.section-label,.selected-route-label,.hero-label,.hero-label span,.hero-kicker,.footer-card-label,.market-name,.menu-overlay-foot{font-size:12px}.admin-workspace label,.store-editor-dialog .store-form-grid label,.store-form-section-heading>span,.store-editor-dialog .admin-photo>strong{font-size:12px}.store-menu-editor label{display:block;font-size:12px;font-weight:700}.store-menu-editor textarea{display:block;width:100%;border:1px solid #dce2eb;border-radius:8px;padding:12px;font:inherit;min-height:110px;margin-top:8px}.store-menu-help{font-size:12px;color:#8190a6;margin-top:10px}
@media(max-width:767px){.menu-filter,.menu-filter select{width:100%;min-width:0}.loop-line{padding-block:16px;transform:rotate(-1.2deg) scaleX(1.1);margin-top:-6px;margin-bottom:-6px}.hero-kicker{font-size:12px;letter-spacing:.02em}.hero-label{font-size:12px}.sponsor-logo{padding-inline:20px}.tabs{margin-top:22px}.site-shell footer{padding-bottom:max(56px,env(safe-area-inset-bottom))}}
@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.site-shell .scroll-cue b{animation:none}.sponsor-track{animation:none}.sponsor-inner{overflow-x:auto}.sponsor-set[aria-hidden=true]{display:none}}
.topbar{background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--white) 78%,transparent);backdrop-filter:blur(18px);}
.site-shell>.topbar{background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--white) 78%,transparent);backdrop-filter:blur(18px);}


/* Softer ribbon, custom menu list and refined partner strip. */
.site-shell>.topbar[data-at-top=true]{background:#1c376b;border-bottom-color:transparent}.topbar[data-at-top=true] .header-logos{background:var(--white);padding:8px 14px;border-radius:10px}.topbar[data-at-top=true] .menu-trigger{border-color:#fff7}.topbar[data-at-top=true] .menu-trigger span{background:var(--white)}
.loop-line{font-size:36px;line-height:1.05;padding-block:9px;transform:rotate(-.6deg) scaleX(1.06);margin-block:-6px}
.custom-menu-select{position:relative;min-width:240px;z-index:10}.menu-select-label{font-size:12px;font-weight:800}.menu-select-trigger{width:100%;min-height:48px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:18px;padding:12px 16px;border:2px solid var(--navy);border-radius:10px;background:var(--white);color:var(--navy);font-size:14px;text-align:left}.menu-select-trigger>span{overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap}.menu-select-trigger svg{width:18px;height:18px;flex-shrink:0;fill:none;stroke:currentColor;stroke-width:1.6;transition:transform .2s}.menu-select-trigger[aria-expanded=true] svg{transform:rotate(180deg)}.menu-select-trigger[aria-expanded=true]{background:var(--cream)}.menu-select-list{position:absolute;top:calc(100% + 8px);left:0;right:0;z-index:30;max-height:280px;overflow-y:auto;padding:7px;border:1px solid color-mix(in srgb,var(--navy) 18%,transparent);border-radius:12px;background:var(--white);box-shadow:0 12px 32px #132b5c22;transform-origin:top;animation:menu-unfold .22s ease-out}.menu-select-list>button{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:16px;width:100%;padding:12px;border-radius:7px;text-align:left;font-size:14px;color:var(--navy);background:transparent}.menu-select-list>button:hover,.menu-select-list>button:focus-visible{background:var(--cream)}.menu-select-list>button[aria-selected=true]{background:var(--navy);color:var(--white)}
@keyframes menu-unfold{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(-8px) scaleY(.88)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0) scaleY(1)}}
.demo-store-notice{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:16px;margin-top:24px;padding:13px 16px;border:1px dashed var(--blue);border-radius:9px;background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--blue) 6%,var(--white));font-size:12px;line-height:1.7}.demo-store-notice button{flex-shrink:0;padding:7px 10px;background:var(--white);border:1px solid currentColor;border-radius:6px}.store-pagination{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:24px;margin-top:32px;font-size:13px}.store-pagination button{min-height:44px;padding:10px 16px;border:1px solid var(--navy);border-radius:8px;background:var(--white);color:var(--navy)}.store-pagination button:disabled{opacity:.35;cursor:default}.store-pagination>span{min-width:48px;text-align:center}
.sponsor-loop{height:72px;min-height:72px;display:flex;align-items:center;position:relative;border-block:1px solid color-mix(in srgb,var(--navy) 10%,transparent);background:var(--white)}.sponsor-inner{height:28px}.sponsor-track{height:28px;align-items:center;animation-duration:calc(var(--logo-seconds,28s) * 5)}.sponsor-set{height:28px;align-items:center}.sponsor-logo{height:28px;padding-inline:32px;opacity:.82;transition:opacity .2s}.sponsor-logo:hover{opacity:1}.sponsor-logo img{height:28px;width:auto}.sponsor-logo[data-logo=compact] img{height:20px}.sponsor-loop::before,.sponsor-loop::after{position:absolute;z-index:3;top:0;bottom:0;width:clamp(24px,5vw,88px);content:'';pointer-events:none}.sponsor-loop::before{left:0;background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--white),transparent)}.sponsor-loop::after{right:0;background:linear-gradient(270deg,var(--white),transparent)}
.footer-inner{align-items:center}.footer-info{transform:rotate(5deg)}.footer-info:hover{transform:rotate(5deg)}.site-shell footer{padding-bottom:96px}
@media(max-width:767px){.topbar[data-at-top=true] .header-logos{padding:6px 8px;gap:8px}.loop-line{font-size:36px;padding-block:8px;transform:rotate(-.6deg) scaleX(1.1);margin-block:-4px}.custom-menu-select{min-width:0;width:100%}.demo-store-notice{align-items:flex-start;flex-direction:column;gap:8px}.sponsor-loop{height:64px;min-height:64px}.sponsor-logo{padding-inline:24px}.footer-inner{align-items:center}.footer-inner>div:first-child{align-self:stretch}.footer-info{width:calc(100% - 24px);transform:rotate(5deg)}.footer-info:hover{transform:rotate(5deg)}.site-shell footer{padding-bottom:80px}}
@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.menu-select-list{animation:none}.menu-select-trigger svg{transition:none}.sponsor-track{animation:none}}

/* A single translucent header over the extended hero background. */
.site-shell{--nav-height:96px}.site-shell>.topbar{height:var(--nav-height);min-height:var(--nav-height);background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--white) 78%,transparent);backdrop-filter:blur(18px);border-bottom-color:color-mix(in srgb,var(--navy) 14%,transparent)}.hero{margin-top:calc(-1 * var(--nav-height));padding-top:var(--nav-height)}.hero-foot{height:0;min-height:0;padding:0;margin:0;border-top:1px solid color-mix(in srgb,var(--cream) 32%,transparent)}.hero-card{padding-bottom:100px}.site-shell .scroll-cue{bottom:50px;left:50%;right:auto;transform:translate(-50%,50%);line-height:1}.site-shell .scroll-cue b{line-height:1;font-size:26px}
.route-panel{display:flex;flex-direction:column;height:clamp(520px,calc(100svh - 132px),720px);min-height:0;padding:24px;overflow:visible}.panel-head{flex:0 0 auto;gap:18px}.panel-head h3{font-size:28px;line-height:1.25}.panel-head p{font-size:13px;line-height:1.6}.route-advisory{margin-top:6px;font-size:11px!important}.selected-route-label{margin-bottom:6px}.demo-store-notice{margin-top:14px;padding:8px 12px;font-size:11px}.demo-store-notice button{padding:4px 8px}.result-meta{flex:0 0 auto;margin-block:14px;padding-block:10px;font-size:12px}.store-grid{flex:1 1 auto;min-height:0;overflow-y:auto;overflow-x:hidden;align-content:start;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));grid-auto-rows:176px;gap:12px;padding:4px 6px 8px 0;overscroll-behavior:contain;scrollbar-width:thin}.store-card{display:grid;grid-template-columns:96px minmax(0,1fr);gap:12px;min-height:0;height:176px;padding:12px;border-radius:12px;overflow:hidden;transform:none!important;box-shadow:none}.store-card:hover{transform:none}.store-photo-wrap{height:100%;min-width:0;border-radius:8px;overflow:hidden;background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--blue) 9%,var(--cream))}.store-photo-wrap .store-photo{display:block;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;aspect-ratio:auto;margin:0;border-radius:0}.store-photo-placeholder{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-direction:column;gap:9px;width:100%;height:100%;color:color-mix(in srgb,var(--navy) 55%,transparent)}.store-photo-placeholder svg{width:38px;height:38px}.store-photo-placeholder span{font-size:10px}.store-card-content{min-width:0;min-height:0;overflow-y:auto;scrollbar-width:thin}.store-card-content h3{font-size:17px;line-height:1.35;margin:0 0 5px;letter-spacing:-.03em}.store-card-content .menu-name{font-size:12px;line-height:1.45;margin:0 0 8px}.store-card-content dl{display:block;margin:0;padding-top:8px;border-top:1px solid color-mix(in srgb,var(--navy) 15%,transparent);font-size:11px;line-height:1.45}.store-card-content dl div{display:grid;grid-template-columns:44px minmax(0,1fr);gap:6px;margin-bottom:4px}.store-card-content .store-details{margin-top:7px;padding-top:6px;font-size:11px}.store-card-content .store-details p{font-size:11px}.store-pagination{flex:0 0 44px;margin-top:14px;gap:20px}.store-pagination button{min-height:36px;padding:7px 13px}.store-grid>.empty-result{grid-column:1/-1;min-height:176px;height:100%;margin:0}.footer-info,.footer-info:hover{transform:rotate(2deg)}
@media(min-width:768px) and (max-width:1199px){.site-shell{--nav-height:88px}.store-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}.route-panel{height:calc(100svh - 112px);max-height:780px;min-height:520px}.panel-head{flex-direction:row;align-items:flex-start}.custom-menu-select{min-width:200px}}
@media(max-width:767px){.site-shell{--nav-height:76px}.hero-card{padding-bottom:100px}.site-shell .scroll-cue{bottom:50px;transform:translate(-50%,50%)}.route-panel{height:calc(100svh - 100px);min-height:490px;max-height:800px;padding:16px}.panel-head{gap:12px}.panel-head h3{font-size:24px}.panel-head p{font-size:12px;line-height:1.5}.custom-menu-select{gap:5px}.menu-select-trigger{min-height:42px;padding:9px 12px;font-size:13px}.demo-store-notice{flex-direction:row;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-top:10px;padding:7px 9px;font-size:10px}.demo-store-notice p{line-height:1.5}.demo-store-notice button{font-size:10px}.result-meta{margin-block:10px;padding-block:8px;gap:5px}.store-grid{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr);grid-auto-rows:152px;gap:10px}.store-card{height:152px;grid-template-columns:92px minmax(0,1fr);padding:10px;gap:11px}.store-card-content h3{font-size:16px}.store-card-content .menu-name{font-size:11px;margin-bottom:5px}.store-card-content dl{font-size:10px;padding-top:6px}.store-card-content .store-details{font-size:10px}.store-pagination{margin-top:10px}.footer-info,.footer-info:hover{transform:rotate(2deg)}}
.store-card-content{overflow:hidden}.store-card-content:has(details[open]){overflow-y:auto}.store-card-content h3{padding:0;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}.store-card-content .menu-name{padding:0;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}.store-card-content dl{padding:6px 0 0;gap:0}.store-card-content dd{margin:0;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}.store-card-content .store-details{margin-top:5px;padding-top:5px}
@media(min-width:1200px){.store-grid{grid-template-rows:repeat(2,minmax(144px,1fr));grid-auto-rows:minmax(144px,auto)}.store-card{height:100%}}

/* Square thumbnails and an explicit information disclosure. */
.store-photo-wrap{align-self:start;width:96px;height:96px;aspect-ratio:1/1}.store-photo-wrap .store-photo{width:100%;height:100%;aspect-ratio:1/1;object-fit:cover}.store-card-content .store-info-disclosure{border:0;margin-top:8px;padding:0}.store-info-disclosure>summary{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:6px;min-height:30px;padding:5px 9px;border:0;border-radius:7px;background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--navy) 8%,var(--white));color:var(--navy);font-size:11px;font-weight:800;list-style:none;transition:background .15s}.store-info-disclosure>summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}.store-info-disclosure>summary:hover{background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--navy) 15%,var(--white))}.store-info-disclosure>summary:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--blue);outline-offset:2px}.store-info-icon{width:14px;height:14px;stroke:currentColor;stroke-width:1.5;flex-shrink:0}.store-info-chevron{width:13px;height:13px;stroke:currentColor;stroke-width:1.6;transition:transform .15s}.store-info-disclosure[open] .store-info-chevron{transform:rotate(180deg)}.store-info-body{padding:10px 0 2px;line-height:1.65;font-size:11px}.store-info-body>strong{font-size:12px}.store-info-body>p{margin-top:5px}
@media(max-width:767px){.store-photo-wrap{width:92px;height:92px}.store-card-content .store-info-disclosure{margin-top:6px}.store-info-disclosure>summary{min-height:28px;padding:4px 7px;font-size:10px}}
```

```css
/* Navy route stage with light paper cards and high-contrast controls. */
.site-shell .route-panel{background:var(--navy);color:#fff;box-shadow:7px 7px 0 var(--blue)}
.site-shell .route-panel .panel-head h3,.site-shell .route-panel .panel-head p,.site-shell .route-panel .selected-route-label,.site-shell .route-panel .result-meta{color:#fff}
.site-shell .route-panel .result-meta{border-color:#ffffff30}.site-shell .route-panel .store-pagination{color:#fff}.site-shell .route-panel .store-pagination button{background:var(--yellow);color:var(--navy)}
.site-shell .route-panel .menu-select-trigger{background:var(--cream);color:var(--navy);border:0}.site-shell .route-panel .demo-store-notice{background:#ffffff12;color:#fff}.site-shell .route-panel .demo-store-notice button{background:var(--yellow);color:var(--navy);border:0}
.site-shell .route-panel .store-card,.site-shell .route-panel .store-card:nth-child(even),.site-shell .route-panel .store-card:hover{color:var(--navy);box-shadow:4px 4px 0 var(--blue)}
.site-shell .route-panel .empty-result{color:#fff}.site-shell .route-panel .empty-result button{background:var(--yellow);color:var(--navy)}
.detail-page-heading h1{white-space:pre-line;font-weight:900}.map-placeholder-message p{white-space:pre-line}.published-market-map{margin:0;border-radius:8px;overflow:hidden;box-shadow:6px 6px 0 var(--blue);background:var(--white)}.published-market-map img{display:block;width:100%;height:auto}
.festival-detail-page>.detail-page-heading{position:relative;background:var(--navy);color:var(--cream);padding:48px;margin-top:28px;box-shadow:8px 8px 0 var(--blue);border-radius:3px 28px 3px 28px;background-image:radial-gradient(#fff3 1px,transparent 1px);background-size:24px 24px;margin-bottom:64px}
.festival-detail-page .detail-page-heading h1{color:var(--yellow);font-weight:900;text-shadow:3px 3px 0 var(--blue);letter-spacing:-.055em}.festival-detail-page .detail-page-heading>span{display:inline-block;background:var(--cream);color:var(--navy);padding:8px 14px;transform:rotate(-2deg)}
.festival-detail-page .event-quick-facts span{background:var(--yellow);color:var(--navy);border:0;border-radius:2px;font-weight:900;clip-path:polygon(3% 0,100% 3%,97% 100%,0 97%)}
.festival-detail-page .detail-section-heading h2,.festival-detail-page .event-linked h2,.festival-detail-page .event-program h3{font-weight:900;letter-spacing:-.04em}.festival-detail-page .timetable-art{border:0;box-shadow:6px 6px 0 var(--blue);border-radius:3px 20px 3px 20px}.festival-detail-page .event-program{border:0;border-radius:3px 20px 3px 20px;box-shadow:5px 5px 0 var(--blue)}.festival-detail-page .event-program-time{width:fit-content;background:var(--yellow);color:var(--navy);padding:8px 12px;transform:rotate(-2deg);font-weight:900}.festival-detail-page .event-linked{border-radius:3px 20px 3px 20px;box-shadow:6px 6px 0 var(--blue)}
@media(max-width:767px){.festival-detail-page>.detail-page-heading{padding:30px 22px;margin-bottom:48px}.festival-detail-page .detail-page-heading h1{font-size:38px}}
.festival-detail-page .event-program.yellow{box-shadow:5px 5px 0 var(--yellow)}.festival-detail-page .event-program.orange{box-shadow:5px 5px 0 #ef622f}.festival-detail-page .event-program.olive{box-shadow:5px 5px 0 #111111}.festival-detail-page .event-program.orange .event-program-time{background:#ef622f;color:#fff}.festival-detail-page .event-program.blue .event-program-time{background:var(--blue);color:#fff}.festival-detail-page .event-program.olive .event-program-time{background:#111111;color:#fff}

/* Reserve six card slots so pagination does not resize the panel. */
.site-shell .route-panel{box-shadow:7px 7px 0 var(--yellow)}
.site-shell .store-grid{grid-template-rows:repeat(2,184px);grid-auto-rows:184px;align-content:start}
.site-shell .store-card,.site-shell .store-card:nth-child(even),.site-shell .store-card:hover{height:184px;min-height:0}
.site-shell .tabs button{font-size:17px;line-height:1.4;min-height:54px;padding:14px 18px}
@media(min-width:768px) and (max-width:1199px){.site-shell .store-grid{grid-template-rows:repeat(3,184px)}.site-shell .tabs button{font-size:16px}}
@media(max-width:767px){.site-shell .store-grid{grid-template-rows:repeat(6,168px);grid-auto-rows:168px}.site-shell .store-card,.site-shell .store-card:nth-child(even),.site-shell .store-card:hover{height:168px;min-height:0}.site-shell .tabs button{font-size:15px;min-height:52px;padding:12px 10px}}

/* Match the home section gutters across public detail pages. */
.site-shell .detail-page-inner{width:100%;max-width:1280px;box-sizing:border-box;margin-inline:auto;padding-inline:56px}
.site-shell.route-detail-shell .routes-inner{padding-inline:56px}
@media(max-width:767px){.site-shell .detail-page-inner,.site-shell.route-detail-shell .routes-inner{padding-inline:20px}}

/* Square route surfaces with parallelogram actions at every breakpoint. */
.site-shell #routes,
.site-shell #routes *,
.site-shell #routes::before,
.site-shell #routes::after,
.site-shell #routes *::before,
.site-shell #routes *::after {
  border-radius: 0;
}
.site-shell #routes .tabs button,
.site-shell #routes .store-detail-button {
  clip-path: polygon(6% 0, 100% 0, 94% 100%, 0 100%);
}
.site-shell .festival-copy > .primary-button.festival-detail-link {
  justify-content: space-between;
  min-height: 62px;
  max-width: 100%;
  padding: 10px 12px 10px 24px;
  gap: 20px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 0;
  background: var(--cream);
  color: var(--navy);
  font-size: 13px;
  text-decoration: none;
  clip-path: polygon(3% 0, 100% 7%, 96% 100%, 0 91%);
  transition: transform 180ms ease;
}
.site-shell .festival-copy > .primary-button.festival-detail-link:hover {
  background: var(--cream);
  color: var(--navy);
  transform: rotate(-2deg) scale(1.04);
}
@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .site-shell .festival-copy > .primary-button.festival-detail-link {
    min-height: 54px;
    padding: 9px 12px 9px 18px;
    font-size: 12px;
  }
}

/* Keep both SVG variants loaded and crossfade without shifting the header. */
.shared-header .header-logo-stack { display: inline-grid; flex: 0 0 auto; align-items: center; justify-items: center; }
.shared-header .header-logo-stack .logo-layer { grid-area: 1 / 1; pointer-events: none; transition: opacity 650ms cubic-bezier(.4, 0, .2, 1); }
.shared-header .logo-layer-dark { opacity: 1; }
.shared-header .logo-layer-light { opacity: 0; }
.shared-header.menu-is-open .logo-layer-dark { opacity: 0; }
.shared-header.menu-is-open .logo-layer-light { opacity: 1; }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .shared-header .header-logo-stack .logo-layer { transition: none; }
}
```

### src/app/(frontend)/hero-copy.css

SHA-256 664e922132bd877c1ea67b731acdc0eabe7eeffc36368993ff934d24c20d459d

```css
/* Long-form hero copy retains the site's illustration and festival palette. */
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-card { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1.3fr) minmax(0, .85fr); gap: 40px; padding-top: 114px; padding-bottom: 100px; min-height: 790px; }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy { min-width: 0; }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-kicker { max-width: 100%; margin: 0 0 24px; padding: 0; border: 0; border-radius: 0; font-size: 11px; line-height: 1.6; letter-spacing: .035em; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.hero.hero-editorial h1 { max-width: 100%; gap: 7px; line-height: 1.18; letter-spacing: -.05em; }
.hero.hero-editorial h1 span { max-width: 100%; width: auto; font-size: clamp(38px, 3.5vw, 58px); line-height: 1.18; word-break: keep-all; overflow-wrap: break-word; }
.hero.hero-editorial h1 span:last-child { margin: 0; transform: none; }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-brand-title { margin: 22px 0 0; padding: 0; color: var(--cream); font-size: clamp(24px, 2.3vw, 34px); line-height: 1.2; letter-spacing: -.02em; font-weight: 900; }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-description { max-width: 100%; margin: 20px 0 0; padding: 0; color: var(--cream); font-size: 15px; line-height: 1.8; white-space: pre-line; word-break: keep-all; overflow-wrap: break-word; }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-event-info { display: grid; gap: 12px; margin: 26px 0 0; padding: 22px 0 0; border-top: 1px solid #fff4; }
.hero-event-info > div { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 10px; min-width: 0; }
.hero-event-info svg { width: 18px; height: 18px; flex: 0 0 18px; margin-top: 2px; }
.hero-event-info dd { margin: 0; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.6; font-weight: 800; word-break: keep-all; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-program-line { max-width: 100%; margin: 18px 0 22px; padding: 0; color: var(--cream); opacity: .8; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; line-height: 1.8; letter-spacing: .02em; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure { width: 100%; max-width: 420px; justify-self: center; }
@media (min-width: 768px) and (max-width: 1199px) {
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-card { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1.4fr) minmax(0, .8fr); gap: 28px; padding-inline: 32px; }
  .hero.hero-editorial h1 span { font-size: clamp(32px, 4.1vw, 46px); }
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-description { font-size: 14px; }
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure figcaption { width: 108%; right: -4%; padding: 12px 16px; flex-direction: column; align-items: flex-start; gap: 5px; font-size: 11px; line-height: 1.5; word-break: keep-all; }
}
@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-card { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); min-height: 0; gap: 40px; padding: 34px 20px 100px; }
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-kicker { margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 10px; }
  .hero.hero-editorial h1 { gap: 5px; }
  .hero.hero-editorial h1 span { font-size: clamp(28px, 7.4vw, 42px); line-height: 1.25; }
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-brand-title { margin-top: 18px; font-size: 24px; }
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-description { margin-top: 18px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.8; }
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-event-info { margin-top: 22px; padding-top: 20px; gap: 12px; }
  .hero-event-info dd { font-size: 13px; }
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-program-line { font-size: 10px; margin-bottom: 22px; }
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure { width: min(90%, 360px); margin: 0; }
}
```

### src/app/(frontend)/section-copy.css

```css
/* Keep the longer festival copy readable at every breakpoint. */
.site-shell .routes-heading h2, .site-shell .festival-copy h2 { line-height: 1.12; word-break: keep-all; overflow-wrap: break-word; }
.site-shell.route-detail-shell .routes-heading { display: block; margin-bottom: 20px; }
.site-shell.route-detail-shell .routes-heading .section-label, .site-shell.route-detail-shell .routes-heading > p { display: none; }
.site-shell.route-detail-shell .routes-heading h2 { font-size: clamp(24px, 3vw, 34px); }
.detail-page-heading h1, .detail-section-heading h2, .event-linked h2 { word-break: keep-all; overflow-wrap: break-word; }
.detail-section-heading > div { min-width: 0; }
.event-linked .event-linked-description { font-size: 17px; font-weight: 800; line-height: 1.7; }
.event-linked .event-linked-note { margin-top: 20px; font-size: 12px; line-height: 1.8; opacity: .8; }
.site-shell .festival-copy > .festival-detail-link { max-width: 100%; white-space: normal; }
.site-shell .festival-copy > .festival-detail-link span { flex-shrink: 0; }
.map-info-grid p { word-break: keep-all; overflow-wrap: break-word; }
@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .detail-section-heading { flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: flex-start; }
  .detail-section-heading > div { flex: 1 1 240px; }
  .site-shell footer h2 { line-height: 1.18; }
  .event-linked .event-linked-description { font-size: 15px; }
}

/* Long Korean labels and secondary notes retain clear wrapping and contrast. */
.site-shell .route-panel .result-meta > span:last-child { color: #fff; opacity: .85; }
.map-placeholder-message h2 { font-size: clamp(22px, 5.8vw, 32px); line-height: 1.35; word-break: keep-all; overflow-wrap: break-word; }

/* Fixed route identities: green, gold, lime, red and brown. */
.site-shell #routes .tabs button,
.site-shell #routes .tabs button.active,
.site-shell #routes .tabs button:hover {
  background: var(--route-color);
  color: var(--route-ink);
}
.site-shell #routes .tabs button.active { box-shadow: none; }
.site-shell #routes .tabs button:hover { filter: brightness(1.04); }
.site-shell #routes .tabs button:focus-visible { outline: 0; box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 3px var(--route-ink); }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel { background: var(--route-color); color: var(--route-ink); box-shadow: 7px 7px 0 var(--route-accent); }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .panel-head h3,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .panel-head p,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .selected-route-label,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .result-meta,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .result-meta > span:last-child,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-pagination,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .empty-result,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .demo-store-notice { color: var(--route-ink); }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .result-meta { border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--route-ink) 25%, transparent); }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .result-meta > span:last-child { opacity: 1; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .demo-store-notice { background: #ffffff24; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-pagination button,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .demo-store-notice button,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .empty-result button { background: var(--route-tint); color: var(--navy); }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card:nth-child(even),
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card:hover { box-shadow: 4px 4px 0 var(--route-accent); }

/* White route copy and readable themed store accents. */
.site-shell #routes .tabs button,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .panel-head,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .selected-route-label { text-shadow: 0 1px 2px #0003; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card h3,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card p,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card dt,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card dd,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card .store-photo-placeholder { color: var(--route-strong); }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card .store-detail-button,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card .store-detail-button:hover,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card .store-detail-button:focus-visible,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-pagination button,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .demo-store-notice button,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .empty-result button { background: var(--route-strong); color: #fff; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card .store-detail-button:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--route-strong); outline-offset: 3px; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .result-meta strong {
  display: inline-flex;
  justify-content: center;
  align-items: center;
  min-width: 34px;
  min-height: 32px;
  padding: 4px 10px;
  margin-inline: 4px;
  background: var(--route-strong);
  color: #fff;
  border: 2px solid var(--route-tint);
  font-weight: 900;
  line-height: 1;
}

/* Inherit the selected theme through every nested menu, icon and store control. */
.site-shell #routes .route-panel {
  --blue: var(--route-strong);
  --navy: var(--route-strong);
}
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .menu-select-trigger,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .menu-select-list { color: var(--route-strong); }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .demo-store-notice { border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--route-strong) 35%, transparent); }
```

### src/app/(frontend)/responsive-polish.css

SHA-256 f63c84786c89f8adbec0750fdb61599226e149b48c3956ffd2374fc938ad1439

```css
/* Responsive reference alignment and route-local sticky navigation. */
.site-shell #routes .tabs {
  position: sticky;
  top: var(--nav-height, 96px);
  z-index: 50;
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: nowrap;
  align-items: stretch;
  width: 100%;
  margin-inline: 0;
  padding: 12px 0;
  background: var(--cream);
  box-sizing: border-box;
}
.site-shell #routes .route-panel {
  --route-ink: var(--route-strong);
  background: var(--route-tint);
  color: var(--route-strong);
}
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .panel-head,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .selected-route-label { text-shadow: none; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .demo-store-notice { background: #ffffff75; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-pagination button:disabled { opacity: .55; }
@media (min-width: 768px) {
  .site-shell #routes .tabs { gap: 12px; overflow: visible; }
  .site-shell #routes .tabs button {
    flex: 1 1 0;
    width: 0;
    min-width: 0;
    max-width: none;
    margin: 0;
    white-space: normal;
    transform: none;
  }
  .site-shell #routes .tabs button:hover { transform: translateY(-3px); }
}
@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy > .primary-button {
    display: inline-flex;
    width: fit-content;
    max-width: 100%;
    margin-inline: 0;
    justify-content: flex-start;
  }
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure {
    width: min(74%, 330px);
    max-width: 100%;
    height: auto;
    justify-self: end;
    align-self: start;
    margin: 0 0 0 auto;
  }
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure .hero-image,
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure .hero-image:hover {
    width: 100%;
    height: auto;
    aspect-ratio: 1 / 1;
    border-radius: 50%;
    box-sizing: border-box;
  }
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure .hero-image img {
    display: block;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    object-fit: cover;
  }
  .site-shell #routes .tabs {
    gap: 8px;
    overflow-x: auto;
    overflow-y: hidden;
    overscroll-behavior-x: contain;
    scrollbar-width: thin;
    scrollbar-color: var(--route-strong) transparent;
    -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;
  }
  .site-shell #routes .tabs button {
    flex: 0 0 auto;
    width: max-content;
    min-width: 0;
    max-width: none;
    margin: 0;
    white-space: nowrap;
    transform: none;
  }
  .site-shell #routes .tabs button:hover { transform: none; }
  .site-shell .festival-inner > .poster-link {
    width: min(78%, 330px);
    max-width: 100%;
    align-self: flex-end;
    justify-self: end;
    margin: 12px 0 0 auto;
  }
}

/* Restore the two-line brand title and opposing hover movement. */
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy h1 span {
  width: fit-content;
  max-width: 100%;
  font-size: clamp(42px, 5vw, 76px);
  transition: transform 320ms cubic-bezier(.2, .65, .3, 1);
}
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy h1 span:first-child { transform: rotate(-2deg); }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy h1 span:last-child { margin-left: 24px; transform: rotate(1deg); }
@media (hover: hover) and (pointer: fine) {
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy h1:hover span:first-child { transform: translateX(24px) rotate(1deg); }
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy h1:hover span:last-child { transform: translateX(-20px) rotate(-1deg); }
}
@media (min-width: 768px) and (max-width: 1199px) {
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy h1 span { font-size: clamp(36px, 4.5vw, 54px); }
}
@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy h1 span { font-size: clamp(32px, 8.6vw, 44px); }
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy h1 span:last-child { margin-left: 14px; }
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy h1 span:first-child,
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy h1 span:last-child,
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy h1:hover span:first-child,
  .hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy h1:hover span:last-child { transition: none; transform: none; }
}

/* Neutral reading text; theme hues are reserved for titles and actions. */
.site-shell #routes { background: #FFFAF0; --cream: #FFFAF0; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel { --route-ink: #30343B; color: #30343B; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .panel-head h3,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card h3,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .empty-result strong { color: var(--route-strong); }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .panel-head p,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card p,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card dd,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .menu-select-trigger { color: #30343B; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .selected-route-label,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .result-meta > span:last-child,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card dt,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card .store-photo-placeholder,
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .demo-store-notice p { color: #626873; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card .store-photo-placeholder svg { color: var(--route-strong); }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .menu-select-list button:not([aria-selected=true]) { color: #30343B; }

/* Keep the market name prominent with a compact, balanced hero. */
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-card { min-height: 680px; padding-top: 110px; padding-bottom: 90px; align-items: center; }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy .hero-description { max-width: 32ch; margin-top: 26px; font-size: clamp(15px, 1.3vw, 18px); }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-event-info { margin-top: 24px; padding-top: 20px; }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy > .primary-button { margin-top: 28px; }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure figcaption { justify-content: center; text-align: center; line-height: 1.5; }
@media(max-width:767px) {
 .hero.hero-editorial .hero-card { min-height: 0; padding: 36px 20px 76px; gap: 30px; }
 .hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy .hero-description { margin-top: 22px; }
 .hero.hero-editorial .hero-event-info { margin-top: 20px; padding-top: 18px; }
 .hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy > .primary-button { margin-top: 24px; }
}

/* A viewport-sized opening, including the motif ribbon. */
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-card { min-height: calc(100svh - var(--nav-height) - 70px); padding-top: 92px; padding-bottom: 76px; }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-foot { display: none; }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy .hero-description { max-width: none; line-height: 1.85; }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure figcaption { width: 94%; right: -5%; bottom: 7%; gap: 12px; padding: 16px 20px; flex-direction: row; align-items: center; justify-content: flex-start; text-align: left; }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure figcaption span { flex-shrink: 0; }
@media(max-width:767px) {
 .hero.hero-editorial .hero-label { padding-top: 16px; }
 .hero.hero-editorial .hero-card { min-height: calc(100svh - var(--nav-height) - 112px); padding: 24px 20px 34px; gap: 20px; align-content: space-between; }
 .hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy .hero-description { font-size: 13px; margin-top: 18px; }
 .hero.hero-editorial .hero-event-info { margin-top: 16px; padding-top: 14px; }
 .hero.hero-editorial .hero-copy > .primary-button { margin-top: 18px; }
 .hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure { width: min(66%, 26svh, 300px); }
 .hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure figcaption { width: 122%; right: -4%; bottom: 5%; gap: 7px; padding: 12px 10px; font-size: 10px; }
 .hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure figcaption span { padding: 5px 7px; font-size: 8px; }
}

/* Content-sized image label and a counterflow section divider. */
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure figcaption { width: max-content; max-width: calc(100% + 28px); box-sizing: border-box; }
.pattern-ribbon.pattern-ribbon-reverse { transform: rotate(.6deg) scaleX(1.02); }
.pattern-ribbon-reverse .pattern-ribbon-track { animation-direction: reverse; }

/* Larger artwork, an unobstructed caption, and connected section dividers. */
@media(min-width:1200px) {
 .hero.hero-editorial .hero-card { grid-template-columns: minmax(0,1.12fr) minmax(0,1fr); gap: 36px; }
 .hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure { max-width: 490px; width: 100%; }
}
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure { padding-bottom: 30px; }
.hero.hero-editorial .hero-figure figcaption { bottom: 0; right: 0; transform: rotate(2deg); }

@media(max-width:767px) {

 .site-shell .pattern-ribbon-set span { flex-basis: 38px; }

/* Theme-aware, square store details and concise list metadata. */

.public-store-modal .public-store-image, .public-store-modal .public-store-modal-head button { border-radius: 0; }

.public-store-modal .public-store-copy h2, .public-store-modal .public-store-copy dt { color: var(--route-strong); }
.public-store-modal .public-store-copy dd { color: #30343b; }

.public-store-modal .public-store-image { position: relative; }

.public-store-modal .store-detail-button:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--route-color); outline-offset: 3px; }

.site-shell #routes .store-card-content h3 { display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 2; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: normal; line-height: 1.35; max-height: 2.7em; }
.site-shell #routes .store-card-content dl, .site-shell #routes .store-card-content dd { font-size: 12px; }

.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card-content { width: 100%; display: flex; flex-direction: column; overflow: hidden; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card-content h3 { display: block; -webkit-line-clamp: unset; max-height: none; white-space: nowrap; text-overflow: ellipsis; overflow: hidden; font-size: 18px; line-height: 1.4; margin-bottom: 10px; }

.site-shell #routes .menu-select-list { position: static; width: 100%; box-sizing: border-box; max-height: 152px; border-radius: 0; margin: 0; box-shadow: none; flex: 0 0 auto; }
.site-shell #routes .menu-select-list button { min-height: 44px; border-radius: 0; touch-action: manipulation; }
.site-shell #routes .tabs { width: 100vw; max-width: none; margin-inline: calc(50% - 50vw); padding-inline: max(var(--page-gutter),calc((100vw - 1280px) / 2 + var(--page-gutter))); background: color-mix(in srgb,#FFFAF0 72%,transparent); backdrop-filter: blur(16px); }

/* Market price-tag hierarchy: centered product and accent price. */
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-grid .store-card { height: auto; align-self: start; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card-content { flex: 0 0 auto; text-align: center; }

 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .panel-head, .site-shell:has(.hero-editorial) #routes .route-panel .panel-head { gap: 28px; margin-bottom: 28px; }

 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .panel-head p, .site-shell:has(.hero-editorial) #routes .route-panel .panel-head p { font-size: 16px; line-height: 1.8; margin-top: 12px; padding: 0; }
 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-grid, .site-shell:has(.hero-editorial) #routes .route-panel .store-grid { flex: 0 0 auto; height: auto; max-height: none; overflow: visible; grid-template-rows: none; grid-auto-rows: max-content; gap: 18px; padding: 0 5px 5px 0; }
 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-pagination, .site-shell:has(.hero-editorial) #routes .route-panel .store-pagination { margin-top: 24px; }
}

/* The menu list floats without changing the panel's layout height. */
.site-shell #routes .custom-menu-select { position: relative; z-index: 60; }
.site-shell #routes .menu-select-list { position: absolute; top: calc(100% + 8px); left: 0; right: 0; width: 100%; z-index: 60; max-height: 240px; box-shadow: 0 12px 28px #182d3526; }
/* Details follow the same paper price-tag hierarchy as store cards. */
.public-store-modal .public-store-copy h2 { text-align: center; font-size: 24px; padding-bottom: 14px; border-bottom: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb,var(--route-strong) 20%,transparent); }
.public-store-modal .public-store-copy .modal-menu-row,
.public-store-modal .public-store-copy .modal-price-row { display: block; text-align: center; border: 0; padding: 7px 0; }
.public-store-modal .modal-menu-row dt, .public-store-modal .modal-price-row dt { position: absolute; width: 1px; height: 1px; overflow: hidden; clip-path: inset(50%); }
.public-store-modal .public-store-copy .modal-menu-row dd { font-size: 20px; font-weight: 900; }
.public-store-modal .public-store-copy .modal-price-row dd { font-size: 20px; font-weight: 900; color: #C43E26; }
.public-store-modal .public-store-modal-foot .store-detail-button { background: var(--route-strong); color: #fff; border-radius: 0; }
.public-store-modal .public-store-modal-foot .store-detail-button span { background: transparent; color: #fff; border: 0; border-radius: 0; box-shadow: none; width: auto; height: auto; padding: 0; display: inline; }
@media(max-width:767px) {
 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-grid { grid-template-columns: minmax(0,1fr); }
 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-grid .store-card { width: 100%; padding: 14px 16px; }
 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card-content { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0,1fr) max-content 44px; gap: 8px 12px; text-align: left; align-items: center; }
 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card-content h3 { grid-column: 1 / -1; grid-row: 1; margin: 0 0 2px; text-align: left; }
 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card-content dl { display: contents; }
 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card-content dl div { margin: 0; padding: 0; border: 0; min-width: 0; }
 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card-content dl div:first-child { grid-column: 1; grid-row: 2; justify-content: flex-start; }
 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card-content dl div:nth-child(2) { grid-column: 2; grid-row: 2; justify-content: flex-end; }
 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card-content dl div:nth-child(3) { grid-column: 1 / -1; grid-row: 3; justify-content: flex-start; gap: 6px; }
 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card-content dd { text-align: left; }
 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card-content .store-menu-value,
 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-card-content .store-price-value { font-size: 20px; }
 .site-shell #routes .route-panel .store-detail-button { grid-column: 3; grid-row: 2; margin: 0; align-self: center; }
}
/* Keep theme badges centered and mobile dialog controls within reach. */
.public-store-modal .public-store-themes { justify-content: center; }
@media(max-width:767px) {
 .public-store-modal[open] {
   display: grid;
   grid-template-rows: auto minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
   overflow: hidden;
 }
 .public-store-modal .public-store-modal-body {
   display: flex;
   flex-direction: column;
   overflow-y: auto;
   overscroll-behavior-y: contain;
 }
 .public-store-modal .public-store-image { flex: none; }
 .public-store-modal .public-store-modal-foot {
   padding: 12px 20px max(12px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom));
   border-top: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--route-color) 25%, transparent);
 }
}
.public-store-modal .public-store-modal-foot .store-detail-button { font-size: 12px; }

.site-shell #routes .store-card { cursor: pointer; }
.route-detail-shell .routes-section::before { display: none; }
.festival-detail-page .event-program-meta {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: baseline;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 8px 12px;
  margin-bottom: 12px;
}
.festival-detail-page .event-program-meta .event-program-time,
.festival-detail-page .event-program-meta .event-program-place {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 14px;
}
.festival-detail-page .event-program-meta .event-program-place { text-align: right; }
.festival-detail-page .event-linked-description { white-space: pre-line; }

.site-shell #routes .route-panel .panel-head p,
.site-shell:has(.hero-editorial) #routes .route-panel .panel-head p { margin-top: 0; }
.route-map { margin-top: 24px; }
.route-map .map-placeholder, .route-map .published-market-map { border-radius: 0; }
.route-map .map-placeholder { min-height: clamp(260px, 36vw, 420px); }
.site-shell .fixed-menu-toggle:not(.is-open) span:first-child { transform: translateY(-7px); opacity: 1; }
.site-shell .fixed-menu-toggle:not(.is-open) span:nth-child(2) { transform: none; opacity: 1; }
.site-shell .fixed-menu-toggle:not(.is-open) span:last-child { transform: translateY(7px); opacity: 1; }

.site-shell #routes .route-panel .menu-card .store-card-content { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto; grid-template-rows: auto auto; gap: 8px 12px; text-align: left; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .menu-card h3 { grid-column: 1; grid-row: 1; margin: 0; min-width: 0; text-align: left; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .menu-card .menu-card-stores { grid-column: 1; grid-row: 2; margin: 0; min-width: 0; font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.65; color: #30343b; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; -webkit-line-clamp: 2; overflow: hidden; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.site-shell #routes .route-panel .menu-card .store-detail-button { grid-column: 2; grid-row: 1 / 3; align-self: center; margin: 0; }
.public-store-modal .public-menu-description { margin: 0 0 20px; font-size: 15px; line-height: 1.8; color: #30343b; white-space: pre-wrap; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.public-store-modal .public-menu-stores h3 { margin: 0 0 12px; font-size: 15px; color: var(--route-strong); }
.public-store-modal .public-menu-stores ul { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0; display: grid; gap: 8px; }
.public-store-modal .public-menu-stores li { padding: 10px 12px; border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--route-color) 25%, transparent); font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.6; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
```

### src/app/(frontend)/admin/preview-visibility.css

SHA-256 9d5f6d53c93f2b85315d2ca18acf79a755d760a7e5570d89924006d6a93874a2

```css
/* Preserve the full-width store/theme directory while keeping its draft preview visible. */
.admin-columns.stores-layout > .admin-preview {
  display: block;
  position: static;
  margin-top: 28px;
}
.admin-preview > label {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 8px;
  margin-bottom: 12px;
}
.admin-preview > label select {
  min-width: 0;
  max-width: 100%;
  padding: 8px;
  border: 1px solid #ccd3df;
  background: white;
  font: inherit;
}
.admin-file-actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 12px;
  margin-top: 16px;
}
.admin-file-actions > span {
  color: var(--admin-muted);
  font-size: 12px;
}

/* Direct native file input: no hidden-input click or client handler is needed to open the picker. */
.admin-workspace .admin-photo label.admin-file-picker {
  position: relative;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  min-height: 44px;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 11px 18px;
  background: #1c376b;
  color: white;
  font: inherit;
  font-weight: 700;
  cursor: pointer;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.admin-workspace .admin-photo .admin-file-picker input.admin-file-input[type=file] {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: 1;
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  border: 0;
  opacity: 0;
  cursor: pointer;
}
.admin-file-picker:focus-within { outline: 3px solid #2877b4; outline-offset: 3px; }
.admin-file-picker[aria-disabled=true] { opacity: .55; cursor: wait; }
```

### src/lib/home-data.ts

SHA-256 07bc57df725764be5613123a88eb40b251ebe141478ef54767874ced5f40452e

```typescript
import { readContent } from './content-store'
export type { HomeRoute, HomeStore, HomeMedia } from './content-schema'

export async function getHomeData() {
  const { published } = await readContent()
  const routes = published.routes.filter(route => route.isVisible)
  const ids = new Set(routes.map(route => route.id))
  return { site: published.site, routes, stores: published.stores.filter(store => store.isVisible && store.routeIds.some(id => ids.has(id))) }
}
```

### src/lib/published-media.ts

SHA-256 89a9849b9becbf17e536872c8f51489dbca76697f6ab0d19c6376a9ce5837bc4

```typescript
import type { Content } from './content-schema'

// Keep upload visibility aligned with the components that render each image.
export function isPublishedMedia(content: Content, url: string) {
  const { site, routes, stores } = content
  const media = [site.hero.image,
    site.mapPage.image, site.festivalPage.timetable,
    ...(site.festival.isVisible ? [site.festival.poster] : [])]
  return media.some(image => image?.url === url) || stores.some(store =>
    store.isVisible && store.image?.url === url &&
    store.routeIds.some(id => routes.some(route => route.id === id && route.isVisible)))
}
```

```javascript
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import path from 'node:path'
import { createRequire } from 'node:module'
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process'
const root=process.cwd(),stage=path.join(root,'.pages-build'),base=process.env.PAGES_BASE_PATH ?? '/market-tonight'
const require=createRequire(import.meta.url),ts=require('typescript'),cache=new Map()
function load(file){if(cache.has(file))return cache.get(file).exports;const m={exports:{}};cache.set(file,m);const code=ts.transpileModule(require('node:fs').readFileSync(file,'utf8'),{compilerOptions:{module:ts.ModuleKind.CommonJS,target:ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022}}).outputText;new Function('require','module','exports',code)(id=>id.startsWith('.')?load(path.resolve(path.dirname(file),id+'.ts')):require(id),m,m.exports);return m.exports}
const {contentSchema}=load(path.join(root,'src/lib/content-schema.ts'))
let published
try{published=contentSchema.parse(JSON.parse(await fs.readFile('.local-data/content.json','utf8')).published)}catch(e){if(e.code!=='ENOENT')throw e;published=load(path.join(root,'src/lib/default-content.ts')).defaultContent}
const routes=published.routes.filter(r=>r.isVisible),ids=new Set(routes.map(r=>r.id));const snapshot={site:published.site,routes,stores:published.stores.filter(s=>s.isVisible&&s.routeIds.some(id=>ids.has(id)))}
// Only this known generated directory may be replaced.
if(path.dirname(stage)!==root||path.basename(stage)!=='.pages-build')throw Error('Unexpected build path')
await fs.rm(stage,{recursive:true,force:true});await fs.mkdir(stage,{recursive:true})
await fs.mkdir(path.join(stage,'.figma/make'),{recursive:true});await fs.copyFile('.figma/make/site.json',path.join(stage,'.figma/make/site.json'))
await fs.cp('src',path.join(stage,'src'),{recursive:true});await fs.cp('public',path.join(stage,'public'),{recursive:true})
for(const name of ['admin','api','.figma'])await fs.rm(path.join(stage,'src/app/(frontend)',name),{recursive:true,force:true})
const uploads=new Set();function rewrite(value){if(typeof value==='string'){if(/^\/api\/uploads\/[a-f0-9-]+\.webp$/.test(value)){uploads.add(path.basename(value));return base+'/assets/uploads/'+path.basename(value)}if(value.startsWith('/assets/'))return base+value;return value}if(Array.isArray(value))return value.map(rewrite);if(value&&typeof value==='object')return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value).map(([k,v])=>[k,rewrite(v)]));return value}
const clean=rewrite(snapshot);await fs.mkdir(path.join(stage,'public/assets/uploads'),{recursive:true});for(const file of uploads)await fs.copyFile(path.join(root,'.local-data/uploads',file),path.join(stage,'public/assets/uploads',file))
await fs.writeFile(path.join(stage,'src/lib/pages-snapshot.json'),JSON.stringify(clean));await fs.writeFile(path.join(stage,'src/lib/content-store.ts'),`import snapshot from './pages-snapshot.json'\nimport type { Content } from './content-schema'\nexport async function readContent(){return {published:snapshot as Content}}\n`)
const frontend=path.join(stage,'src/app/(frontend)');for(const file of ['page.tsx','routes/page.tsx','festival/page.tsx','map/page.tsx','routes/[routeId]/page.tsx']){const target=path.join(frontend,file);let text=await fs.readFile(target,'utf8');text=text.replaceAll("'force-dynamic'","'force-static'");if(file.includes('[routeId]'))text+='\nexport async function generateStaticParams(){const {routes}=await getHomeData();return routes.map(route=>({routeId:route.id}))}\n';await fs.writeFile(target,text)}
async function patch(folder){for(const entry of await fs.readdir(folder,{withFileTypes:true})){const file=path.join(folder,entry.name);if(entry.isDirectory())await patch(file);else if(/\.(tsx|ts)$/.test(entry.name)){let text=await fs.readFile(file,'utf8');text=text.replaceAll('href="/"',`href="${base}/"`).replace(/href="\/(routes|festival|map)(["/])/g,`href="${base}/$1$2`).replaceAll("'/assets/",`'${base}/assets/`).replaceAll('"/assets/',`"${base}/assets/`);if(entry.name==='SiteFooter.tsx')text=text.replace(/<a className="footer-admin-button"[\s\S]*?<\/a>/,'');await fs.writeFile(file,text)}}}await patch(frontend)
const packageJson=JSON.parse(await fs.readFile('package.json','utf8'));await fs.writeFile(path.join(stage,'package.json'),JSON.stringify({...packageJson,name:'market-tonight-pages'}));for(const file of ['tsconfig.json','postcss.config.mjs','next-env.d.ts'])await fs.copyFile(file,path.join(stage,file));await fs.symlink(path.join(root,'node_modules'),path.join(stage,'node_modules'),'junction');await fs.writeFile(path.join(stage,'next.config.mjs'),`export default {output:'export',basePath:'${base}',trailingSlash:true,images:{unoptimized:true},env:{NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL:'${process.env.PAGES_ORIGIN || 'https://lemngu.github.io'}',NEXT_PUBLIC_STORE_FEED_PATH:'${process.env.MARKET_ENABLE_STORE_FEED === '1' ? '/api/public-stores' : ''}'}}\n`)
const result=spawnSync(process.execPath,[require.resolve('next/dist/bin/next'),'build'],{cwd:stage,stdio:'inherit',env:{...process.env,NODE_ENV:'production',NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED:'1'}});if(result.status!==0)process.exit(result.status||1)
await fs.writeFile(path.join(stage,'out/.nojekyll'),'');console.log('GitHub Pages files: '+path.join(stage,'out'));console.log('Published stores: '+snapshot.stores.length+'; public upload files: '+uploads.size+'; local admin and drafts excluded.')
```

### scripts/admin-content.test.cjs

SHA-256 0bc182ae01062b2921693aec459a0c5267ee0510e81a6bf71ca7f7626d017b40

```javascript
// Run with: node --test scripts/admin-content.test.cjs
// Authentication is mocked only in this process; the app's auth code is untouched.
const { test, after } = require('node:test')
const assert = require('node:assert/strict')
const fs = require('node:fs')
const os = require('node:os')
const path = require('node:path')
const Module = require('node:module')
const ts = require('typescript')
const root = path.resolve(__dirname, '..')
const directory = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'market-admin-test-'))
process.env.MARKET_DATA_DIRECTORY = directory
process.env.AUTH_URL = 'http://localhost:3100'
let admin = false
const resolve = Module._resolveFilename
Module._resolveFilename = function (name, ...args) {
  return resolve.call(this, name.startsWith('@/') ? path.join(root, 'src', name.slice(2)) : name, ...args)
}
const load = Module._load
Module._load = function (name, ...args) {
  if (name === 'server-only') return {}
  if (name === '@/auth') return { isAdmin: async () => admin }
  return load.call(this, name, ...args)
}
require.extensions['.ts'] = function (module, filename) {
  const output = ts.transpileModule(fs.readFileSync(filename, 'utf8'), {
    compilerOptions: { module: ts.ModuleKind.CommonJS, target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022, esModuleInterop: true }, fileName: filename,
  }).outputText
  module._compile(output, filename)
}
const { defaultContent } = require('../src/lib/default-content.ts')
const { contentSchema } = require('../src/lib/content-schema.ts')
const { convertImage, sanitizeSVG, readUploadForm, MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES } = require('../src/lib/image-upload.ts')
const { imageTypeForFile } = require('../src/lib/upload-types.ts')
const { isPublishedMedia } = require('../src/lib/published-media.ts')
const { readContent, saveContent } = require('../src/lib/content-store.ts')
const upload = require('../src/app/(frontend)/api/admin/upload/route.ts')
const media = require('../src/app/(frontend)/api/uploads/[filename]/route.ts')
const contentAPI = require('../src/app/(frontend)/api/admin/content/route.ts')
const image = { url: '/api/uploads/aaaaaaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa.webp', alt: 'test', width: 32, height: 20 }
const svg = '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="32" height="20"><style>.shape{fill:#f00}</style><rect class="shape" width="32" height="20"/></svg>'
after(() => { assert.ok(path.resolve(directory).startsWith(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'market-admin-test-'))); fs.rmSync(directory, { recursive: true, force: true }) })

test('retired logo settings are ignored while all other saved content is preserved', () => {
  const old = structuredClone(defaultContent)
  old.site.navigation = { festivalLogo: image, marketLogo: image }
  const parsed = contentSchema.parse(old)
  assert.equal('navigation' in parsed.site, false)
  delete old.site.navigation
  assert.deepEqual(parsed, old)
  assert.equal(isPublishedMedia(parsed, image.url), false)
})
test('SVG paths, gradients, styles and local references render into inert WebP', async () => {
  const result = await convertImage(Buffer.from(svg), 'image/svg+xml')
  assert.equal(result.info.width, 32); assert.equal(result.info.height, 20)
  assert.equal(result.info.format, 'webp'); assert.ok(result.data.length > 0)
  await convertImage(Buffer.from('<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20"><defs><linearGradient id="g"><stop stop-color="#f00"/></linearGradient></defs><path fill="url(#g)" d="M0 0h20v20H0z"/></svg>'), 'image/svg+xml')
})
test('SVG scripts, events, foreign objects, external references, entities and CSS tricks are rejected', () => {
  for (const inner of ['<script>alert(1)</script>', '<rect onload="alert(1)"/>', '<foreignObject/>', '<image href="file:///etc/passwd"/>', '<use href="https://example.com/x.svg#id"/>', '<style>@import "https://example.com";</style>', '<rect fill="url(file:///etc/passwd)"/>', '<style>rect{fill:u\\72l(https://example.com)}</style>']) {
    assert.throws(() => sanitizeSVG(Buffer.from(`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">${inner}</svg>`)), inner)
  }
  assert.throws(() => sanitizeSVG(Buffer.from('<!DOCTYPE svg [<!ENTITY test SYSTEM "file:///etc/passwd">]><svg>&test;</svg>')))
})
test('fake MIME, oversized dimensions, empty data, oversize files and chunked oversize bodies are rejected', async () => {
  await assert.rejects(convertImage(Buffer.from(svg), 'image/png'))
  await assert.rejects(convertImage(Buffer.from(svg.replace('width="32"', 'width="20001"')), 'image/svg+xml'))
  await assert.rejects(convertImage(Buffer.alloc(0), 'image/png'))
  await assert.rejects(convertImage(Buffer.alloc(5 * 1024 * 1024 + 1), 'image/png'))
  const body = new ReadableStream({ start(controller) { controller.enqueue(new Uint8Array(MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES + 1)); controller.close() } })
  await assert.rejects(readUploadForm(new Request('http://localhost/upload', { method: 'POST', body, duplex: 'half' })), /UPLOAD_TOO_LARGE/)
})
test('all rendered media types become public; hidden posters/stores and unreferenced uploads stay private', () => {
  for (const [group, key] of [['hero', 'image'], ['mapPage', 'image'], ['festivalPage', 'timetable'], ['festival', 'poster']]) {
    const content = structuredClone(defaultContent); content.site[group][key] = image
    assert.equal(isPublishedMedia(content, image.url), true, group + '.' + key)
  }
  const content = structuredClone(defaultContent); content.site.festival.poster = image; content.site.festival.isVisible = false
  assert.equal(isPublishedMedia(content, image.url), false)
  content.stores.push({ id: 'test', name: 'test', menu: '', price: '', hours: '', isVisible: true, image, routeIds: ['budget'] })
  assert.equal(isPublishedMedia(content, image.url), true)
  content.routes[0].isVisible = false
  assert.equal(isPublishedMedia(content, image.url), false)
})
test('authenticated upload, draft privacy, publication, conflict protection and backups', async () => {
  const request = (origin = 'http://localhost:3100') => { const body = new FormData(); body.set('file', new File([svg], 'hero.svg', { type: 'image/svg+xml' })); return new Request('http://localhost:3100/api/admin/upload', { method: 'POST', headers: { origin }, body }) }
  admin = false
  assert.equal((await upload.POST(request())).status, 401)
  assert.equal((await contentAPI.GET(new Request('http://localhost:3100/api/admin/content'))).status, 401)
  admin = true
  assert.equal((await upload.POST(request('http://evil.example'))).status, 403)
  const response = await upload.POST(request()); assert.equal(response.status, 200)
  const uploaded = await response.json(); assert.match(uploaded.url, /^\/api\/uploads\/[a-f0-9-]+\.webp$/)
  const filename = uploaded.url.split('/').at(-1)
  const getMedia = () => media.GET(new Request('http://localhost:3100' + uploaded.url), { params: Promise.resolve({ filename }) })
  const initial = await readContent(); const content = structuredClone(initial.draft); content.site.mapPage.image = uploaded
  const draft = await saveContent(content, initial.revision, false)
  admin = false; assert.equal((await getMedia()).status, 404)
  admin = true; assert.equal((await getMedia()).status, 200)
  const published = await saveContent(content, draft.revision, true)
  admin = false; const result = await getMedia(); assert.equal(result.status, 200); assert.equal(result.headers.get('content-type'), 'image/webp')
  assert.equal(result.headers.get('x-content-type-options'), 'nosniff')
  await assert.rejects(saveContent(content, draft.revision, true), /CONFLICT/)
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(directory, 'history', `revision-${draft.revision}.json`), 'utf8')).published, draft.published)
  assert.equal((await readContent()).revision, published.revision)
  assert.equal((await media.GET(new Request('http://localhost/'), { params: Promise.resolve({ filename: '../content.json' }) })).status, 404)
})



test('Illustrator metadata, standard version fields and Korean fragment IDs render safely', async () => {
  const source = '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 32 20"><defs><pattern id="새_패턴_견본" width="4" height="4" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse"><rect data-name="레이어 1" width="4" height="4" fill="#f00"/></pattern></defs><rect data-name="아트워크" width="32" height="20" fill="url(#새_패턴_견본)"/></svg>'
  const result = await convertImage(Buffer.from(source), 'image/svg+xml')
  assert.equal(result.info.width, 32); assert.equal(result.info.height, 20)
  assert.throws(() => sanitizeSVG(Buffer.from(source.replace('url(#새_패턴_견본)', 'url(https://example.com/패턴)'))))
})
test('existing real Illustrator logos and timetable upload successfully', async () => {
  for (const name of ['Korea-Grand-Festival_logo_black.svg', 'Market-Tonight_logo_black.svg', 'event/timetable.svg', 'event/pattern.svg']) {
    const result = await convertImage(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'public/assets', name)), 'image/svg+xml')
    assert.ok(result.info.width > 0 && result.info.height > 0, name)
  }
})
test('Windows exports without MIME retain byte-level verification', async () => {
  assert.equal(imageTypeForFile({name:'외부 일러스트.SVG',type:''}), 'image/svg+xml')
  assert.equal(imageTypeForFile({name:'사진.png',type:'application/octet-stream'}), 'image/png')
  assert.equal(imageTypeForFile({name:'문서.txt',type:''}), null)
  assert.equal(imageTypeForFile({name:'사진.png',type:'text/html'}), null)
  await assert.rejects(convertImage(Buffer.from('<html>fake</html>'), imageTypeForFile({name:'fake.svg',type:''})))
})

test('non-hero copy refresh preserves hero, images, relationships and current timetable', () => {
  const { applySectionCopy } = require('../src/lib/section-copy.ts')
  const content = structuredClone(defaultContent)
  content.site.hero.titleLine1 = '유지할 히어로'
  content.site.hero.image = image
  content.site.festival.poster = image
  content.site.festivalPage.timetable = image
  content.site.mapPage.image = image
  content.site.festivalPage.programs[0].time = '18:15–20:15'
  content.site.festivalPage.programs[0].place = '유지할 행사장'
  content.stores = [{ id: 'preserved-store', name: '기존 점포', menu: '기존 메뉴', price: '', hours: '', image, routeIds: ['budget'], isVisible: true }]
  const next = applySectionCopy(content)
  assert.deepEqual(next.site.hero, content.site.hero)
  assert.deepEqual(next.stores, content.stores)
  assert.deepEqual(next.routes.map(route => [route.id, route.name, route.isVisible]), content.routes.map(route => [route.id, route.name, route.isVisible]))
  assert.deepEqual(next.site.festivalPage.programs.map(program => [program.time, program.place, program.color]), content.site.festivalPage.programs.map(program => [program.time, program.place, program.color]))
  assert.deepEqual([next.site.festival.poster, next.site.festivalPage.timetable, next.site.mapPage.image], [image, image, image])
  assert.equal(next.site.routesSection.browseLabel, '내 취향의 미식루트 찾기')
  assert.equal(next.site.footer.headingLine1, '취향 따라 걷고,')
  assert.deepEqual(applySectionCopy(next), next)
})
```

### src/lib/content-schema.ts

SHA-256 28464c1fc17e51787aa5284f1b6dd2ee191a2525df9724d3243beff6f5213cc2

```typescript
import { z } from 'zod'
import { pageDefaults } from './page-defaults'
import { recommendedHeroCopy } from './hero-copy'
import { sectionCopy } from './section-copy'

const text = z.string().max(4000)
const short = z.string().trim().min(1).max(160)
const id = z.string().regex(/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,100}$/)
const imageUrl = z.string().max(2000).refine(v => /^\/api\/uploads\/[a-f0-9-]+\.webp$/.test(v) || /^\/assets\/[a-zA-Z0-9_./%-]+$/.test(v), '업로드한 이미지 또는 assets 경로를 사용해주세요.')
export const mediaSchema = z.object({ url: imageUrl, alt: text, width: z.number().int().positive().max(20000), height: z.number().int().positive().max(20000) }).strict().nullable()
export const routeSchema = z.object({ id, name: short, description: text, introduction: text, advisory: text, isVisible: z.boolean() }).strict()
export const storeSchema = z.object({ id, name: short, isVisible: z.boolean(), menu: text, menus: z.array(short).max(100).optional(), description: text.optional(), storeNames: z.array(short).max(500).optional(), price: text, hours: text, location: text.optional(), image: mediaSchema.or(mediaSchema.unwrap().extend({ url: z.string().regex(/^\/api\/public-stores\?photo=[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,100}(?:&v=[a-f0-9]{24})?$/) })), routeIds: z.array(id).max(100) }).strict()
const color = z.string().regex(/^#[a-fA-F0-9]{6}$/)
const currentSiteSchema = z.object({
  menuTagline: text.default(sectionCopy.menuTagline), brand: short, metaTitle: short, metaDescription: text, navRoutesLabel: text, navEventLabel: text,
  hero: z.object({ kicker: text.default(recommendedHeroCopy.kicker), brandTitle: text.default(recommendedHeroCopy.brandTitle), eventDate: text.default(recommendedHeroCopy.eventDate), eventLocation: text.default(recommendedHeroCopy.eventLocation), programLine: text.default(recommendedHeroCopy.programLine), eyebrow: text, titleLine1: text, titleLine2: text, description: text, ctaLabel: text, image: mediaSchema, caption: text, footLeft: text, footRight: text }).strict(),
  routesSection: z.object({ browseLabel: text.default(sectionCopy.routesSection.browseLabel), countSuffix: text.default(sectionCopy.routesSection.countSuffix), title: text, description: text, selectedLabel: text, searchPlaceholder: text, countLabel: text, advisory: text, emptyTitle: text, emptyMessage: text, preparingTitle: text, preparingDescription: text, resetLabel: text, priceLabel: text, hoursLabel: text, unconfirmedLabel: text, menuUnconfirmedLabel: text, detailLabel: text }).strict(),
  festival: z.object({ detailLabel: text.default(sectionCopy.festival.detailLabel), isVisible: z.boolean(), heading: text, name: text, highlights: text, date: text, schedule: text, location: text, programHours: text, note: text, linkLabel: text, poster: mediaSchema }).strict(),
  routePage: z.object({sectionTitle: text.default(sectionCopy.routePage.sectionTitle), eyebrow: text, title: text, description: text}).strict().default(pageDefaults.routePage),
  festivalPage: z.object({linkedEyebrow: text.default(sectionCopy.festivalPage.linkedEyebrow), linkedDescription: text.default(sectionCopy.festivalPage.linkedDescription), linkedNote: text.default(sectionCopy.festivalPage.linkedNote), eyebrow: text, title: text, description: text, date: text, schedule: text, location: text, timetableTitle: text, timetable: mediaSchema, programTitle: text, programs: z.array(z.object({time:text,title:text,place:text,color:z.enum(['yellow','blue','orange','olive']),description:text}).strict()).max(50), linkedTitle:text, linkedSchedule:text, linkedLocation:text, note:text}).strict().default(pageDefaults.festivalPage),
  mapPage: z.object({preparingDescription: text.default(sectionCopy.mapPage.preparingDescription), routesDescription: text.default(sectionCopy.mapPage.routesDescription), eventDescription: text.default(sectionCopy.mapPage.eventDescription), routesLinkLabel: text.default(sectionCopy.mapPage.routesLinkLabel), eventLinkLabel: text.default(sectionCopy.mapPage.eventLinkLabel), eyebrow: text, title: text, description: text, image:mediaSchema, placeholderTitle:text, placeholderDescription:text}).strict().default(pageDefaults.mapPage),
  notice: text,
  footer: z.object({ headingLine1: text, headingLine2: text, tagline: text, organization: text, analyticsNote: text, adminLabel: text }).strict(),
  design: z.object({ navy: color, cream: color, white: color, blue: color, mutedBackground: color, yellow: color, fontSize: z.number().int().min(12).max(22), logoSeconds: z.number().int().min(10).max(90) }).strict(),
}).strict()
// Read old drafts without resurrecting the retired logo editor. Other fields stay strict.
export const siteSchema = z.preprocess(value => {
  if (value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)) {
    const { navigation: _retiredLogoSettings, ...site } = value as Record<string, unknown>
    return site
  }
  return value
}, currentSiteSchema)
export const contentSchema = z.object({ site: siteSchema, routes: z.array(routeSchema).max(100), stores: z.array(storeSchema).max(2000) }).strict().superRefine((data, ctx) => {
  const ids = new Set(data.routes.map(r => r.id))
  if (ids.size !== data.routes.length || new Set(data.stores.map(s => s.id)).size !== data.stores.length) ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', message: '중복된 항목 ID가 있습니다.' })
  data.stores.forEach((s, i) => {
    if (s.routeIds.some(r => !ids.has(r))) ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['stores', i, 'routeIds'], message: '존재하지 않는 미식루트입니다.' })
    if (s.isVisible && !s.routeIds.length) ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['stores', i, 'routeIds'], message: '공개 점포는 미식루트를 하나 이상 선택해주세요.' })
  })
})
export type Content = z.infer<typeof contentSchema>
export type SiteCopy = Content['site']
export type HomeRoute = Content['routes'][number]
export type HomeStore = Content['stores'][number]
export type HomeMedia = NonNullable<HomeStore['image']>
```

```typescript
  festivalPage: {
    title: '서로 다른 하루가,\n같은 저녁에서 만나는 곳.',
    description: '음악과 예술, 먹거리와 사람이 어우러지는 특별한 금요일. 구월모래내시장의 새로운 밤을 만나보세요.',
    timetableTitle: '놓치지 말아야 할 오늘의 타임테이블', programTitle: '골목마다 펼쳐지는 특별한 즐거움',
    linkedEyebrow: '시장에서 만나는 특별한 구매 혜택', linkedTitle: '강화 석모도 고시히까리쌀 할인 판매',
    linkedDescription: '고시히까리쌀 10kg, 39,000원! 구매 시 온누리상품권 1만 원권 증정.',
    linkedNote: '1인 최대 2포까지 구매 가능하며, 준비된 물량 소진 시 조기 종료됩니다.',
  },
  mapPage: {
    title: '구월과 모래내,\n두 시장을 잇는 특별한 산책.',
    description: '먹거리부터 공연과 체험까지! MARKET TONIGHT의 주요 장소를 지도에서 확인해 보세요.',
    preparingDescription: '먹거리부터 공연과 체험까지! MARKET TONIGHT의 주요 장소를 한눈에 볼 수 있는 지도를 준비하고 있습니다.',
    placeholderTitle: 'MARKET TONIGHT 축제 지도',
    placeholderDescription: '실제 행사 배치도를 준비하고 있습니다.\n현재 배경과 표시는 디자인 확인용 예시입니다.',
    routesDescription: '다섯 가지 테마를 따라 구월모래내시장의 숨은 맛집을 발견해 보세요.',
    eventDescription: '아트 플리마켓, 버스킹, DJ RAVE가 열리는 장소를 확인해 보세요.',
    routesLinkLabel: '내 취향의 미식루트 찾기', eventLinkLabel: '전체 프로그램 및 시간표 보기',
  },
  footer: { headingLine1: '취향 따라 걷고,', headingLine2: '미식을 발견해요', tagline: 'MARKET TONIGHT · 구월모래내시장' },
} as const

const routeIntroductions: Record<string, string> = {
  budget: "만원 한 장으로 즐기는 든든한 행복!\n부담 없이 즐기는 가성비 맛집 코스",
  'gluten-free': "저탄고지 애호가는 여기 모여라!\n글루텐을 배제한 미식 테마 코스",
  grill: "지글지글 불 위에서 완성되는 맛!\n시장의 대표 불맛을 찾아 떠나는 산책",
  vegan: "시장에서 발견하는 비건 식당!\n취향과 건강을 함께 생각하는 비건 탐방",
  winter: "쌀쌀한 계절, 손끝까지 따뜻하게!\n시장 안에 달달하고 따끈한 겨울 간식",
}

export function updateProgramCopy(program: { time: string; title: string; place: string; color: 'yellow' | 'blue' | 'orange' | 'olive'; description: string }) {
  if (/아트\s*플리마켓/.test(program.title)) return { ...program, title: '아트 플리마켓', description: '40개 로컬 셀러와 함께하는 취향 발견! 책과 음반, 수공예품을 만나보세요.' }
  if (/스쿨뮤직.*팝업/.test(program.title)) return { ...program, description: '인천 로컬 브랜드 스쿨뮤직과 함께 악기를 직접 보고, 연주하고, 경험해 보세요.' }
  if (/말왕/.test(program.title)) return { ...program, title: '말왕 토크쇼·사인회', description: '크리에이터 말왕과 나누는 도전과 일상의 이야기! 현장 Q&A와 사인회까지 함께하세요.' }
  if (/스쿨뮤직.*버스킹/.test(program.title)) return { ...program, description: '인천 청년 아티스트들의 라이브 무대! 시장 골목을 음악으로 채우는 특별한 시간.' }
  if (/DJ\s*RAVE.*Doodle/i.test(program.title)) return { ...program, description: 'DJ 비트에 몸을 맡기고, 테이블 위에 자유롭게 그려보세요. 음악과 낙서가 예술이 되는 밤!' }
  return program
}

export function applySectionCopy(content: Content): Content {
  const next = structuredClone(content)
  next.site.menuTagline = sectionCopy.menuTagline
  next.site.metaTitle = sectionCopy.metaTitle
  next.site.metaDescription = sectionCopy.metaDescription
  next.site.routesSection = { ...next.site.routesSection, ...sectionCopy.routesSection }
  next.site.festival = { ...next.site.festival, ...sectionCopy.festival }
  next.site.routePage = { ...next.site.routePage, ...sectionCopy.routePage }
  next.site.festivalPage = { ...next.site.festivalPage, ...sectionCopy.festivalPage, programs: next.site.festivalPage.programs.map(updateProgramCopy) }
  next.site.mapPage = { ...next.site.mapPage, ...sectionCopy.mapPage }
  next.site.footer = { ...next.site.footer, ...sectionCopy.footer }
  next.site.notice = sectionCopy.routesSection.advisory
  next.routes = next.routes.map(route => routeIntroductions[route.id] ? { ...route, introduction: routeIntroductions[route.id] } : route)
  return next
}
```

### src/app/(frontend)/layout.tsx

SHA-256 297bfacca83dc0c0fa7da5076a9d76121513fd39ebde19b74f4723971ed8fcb6

```tsx
import React from 'react'
import type { Metadata, Viewport } from 'next'
import Script from 'next/script'
import { BodyCustomCode, renderHeadCustomCode } from '@/lib/make-custom-code'
import { makeSiteSettings, sanitizeHtmlAttribute } from '@/lib/make-site-settings'
import './styles.css'
import './responsive.css'
import './detail-pages.css'
import './hero-copy.css'
import './section-copy.css'
import './responsive-polish.css'
import { readContent } from '@/lib/content-store'

const defaultTitle = '구월모래내 맛길 안내서'
const title = makeSiteSettings.title ?? defaultTitle
const description =
  makeSiteSettings.description ??
  '두 시장, 다섯 가지 취향. 인천 구월모래내시장의 맛길을 만나보세요.'
const favicon = makeSiteSettings.icons?.icon
const socialImage = makeSiteSettings.openGraph?.image
const analyticsSetting = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GA4_MEASUREMENT_ID?.trim() || makeSiteSettings.analytics?.googleAnalyticsId
const googleAnalyticsId = analyticsSetting && /^G-[A-Z0-9]+$/.test(analyticsSetting) ? analyticsSetting : undefined

export const viewport: Viewport = { colorScheme: 'only light' }

export async function generateMetadata(): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { published: { site } } = await readContent()
  return { metadataBase: new URL(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || 'http://localhost:3000'), title: site.metaTitle, description: site.metaDescription, openGraph: { title: site.metaTitle, description: site.metaDescription } }
}

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const language = sanitizeHtmlAttribute(makeSiteSettings.language) || 'ko'
  const addBypassLinks = makeSiteSettings.accessibility?.addBypassLinks === true
  const customScripts = makeSiteSettings.customScripts

  return (
    <html lang={language}>
      <head>
        {renderHeadCustomCode(customScripts?.headStart, 'head-start')}
        {renderHeadCustomCode(customScripts?.headEnd, 'head-end')}
      </head>
      <body className="min-h-screen antialiased">
        <BodyCustomCode html={customScripts?.bodyStart} />
        {addBypassLinks && (
          <a className="figma-bypass-link" href="#root">
            Skip to content
          </a>
        )}
        <div id="root" tabIndex={-1}>
          {children}
        </div>
        {googleAnalyticsId && (
          <Script id="market-google-analytics" strategy="afterInteractive">
            {`
(() => {
  if (/(^|\\/)admin(\\/|$)/.test(location.pathname)) return;

  const measurementId = ${JSON.stringify(googleAnalyticsId)};
  window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
  window.gtag = function () { window.dataLayer.push(arguments); };
  window.gtag('js', new Date());
  window.gtag('config', measurementId);

  const tag = document.createElement('script');
  tag.async = true;
  tag.src = 'https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=' + measurementId;
  document.head.append(tag);

  document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
    if (!(event.target instanceof Element)) return;
    const action = event.target.closest('[data-ga-event]');
    if (!action || action.closest(':disabled')) return;
    const item = action.getAttribute('data-ga-item');
    window.gtag('event', action.getAttribute('data-ga-event'), item ? { item_id: item } : {});
  }, true);
})();
`}
          </Script>
        )}
        <BodyCustomCode html={customScripts?.bodyEnd} />
      </body>
    </html>
  )
}
```
